							api.NotifyEvent = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
								local notificationSettings = sharedState.NotificationSettings
								if not notificationSettings.Enabled or not notificationSettings.Events[arg] then
									return
								end
								local text6 = notificationSettings.Templates[arg]
								if type(text6) ~= "string" then
									return
								end

								for k, value24 in pairs(arg2) do
									text6 = text6:gsub("%%" .. k .. "%%", function()
										return tostring(value24)
									end)
								end

								notify(text6, arg3 or arg)
							end

							waitAtLeast = function(arg)
								local num36 = arg or 0
								local heartbeat2 = RunService.Heartbeat
								local now3 = tick()

								repeat
									heartbeat2:Wait()
								until num36 <= tick() - now3

								return true
							end

							api.GetRuntimeGate = function(arg)
								local value24 = sharedState.RuntimeGates[arg]
								if value24 then
									return value24
								end
								local bindableEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
								sharedState.RuntimeGates[arg] = bindableEvent
								return bindableEvent
							end

							api.SetRuntimeToggle = function(arg, arg2)

									value24:Fire()
								end
							end

							api.WaitForRuntimeToggle = function(arg)
								if featureState[arg] then
									return true
								end
								local value24 = api.GetRuntimeGate(arg)

								while true do
									value24.Event:Wait()
									if not (unloaded or featureState[arg]) then
										continue
									end
									break
								end

								return not unloaded
							end

							api.WakeRuntimeGates = function()

									runtimeGate:Fire()
								end
							end

							api.WaitForRuntimeCondition = function(arg, arg2)
								if arg2() then
									return true
								end
								local value24 = api.GetRuntimeGate(arg)

								while true do
									value24.Event:Wait()
									local value25 = unloaded
									local value26

									if unloaded then
										value26 = value25
									else
										value26 = arg2()
									end

									if not value26 then
										continue
									end
									break
								end

								return not unloaded
							end

							api.UpdateKeybindMenu = function()
								local onlyAssignedKeybinds = not uiLib.IsMobile and featureState.OnlyAssignedKeybinds

								for _, value24 in options, nil, nil do
									if not (value24.Type ~= "KeyPicker" or not value24.SetMenuVisibility) then

										if value25 == nil then
											local state12 = value24.MenuVisible ~= false
											sharedState.KeybindMenuDefaults[value24] = state12
											value25 = state12
										end

										value24:SetMenuVisibility(value25 and (not onlyAssignedKeybinds or value24.Value ~= "None"))
									end
								end
							end

							if listfiles and delfile then
								local ok, result = pcall(listfiles, ".")

								if not (ok and type(result) == "table") then
									result = {}
								end

								for _, value24 in result, nil, nil do
									local match, state12

									do
										local value25
										match, value25 = (value24:match("[^/\\]+$") or value24):match("^(%w+)_(%x+)%.[%w]+$")
										state12 = match and #value25 == 32
									end

									local match2

									if state12 then
										match2 = match:match("^Resonance") or match == "AdminSound"
									else
										match2 = state12
									end

									if match2 then
										pcall(delfile, value24)
									end
								end
							end

							api.LoadCustomAsset = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
								if type(arg) ~= "string" or arg == "" then
									return nil, "Empty URL"
								end

								if not (writefile and getcustomasset) then
									return nil, "Custom assets are not supported"
								end
								local text6 = (arg2 or "ResonanceAsset") .. "_" .. HttpService:GenerateGUID(false):gsub("%-", "") .. (arg3 or "")
								local ok, result = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, arg)
								if not ok or type(result) ~= "string" or result == "" then
									return nil, "Download failed"
								end

								if not pcall(writefile, text6, result) then
									if delfile then
										pcall(delfile, text6)
									end

									return nil, "File write failed"
								end

								sharedState.CustomAssets.Files[text6] = text6
								local ok2, result2 = pcall(getcustomasset, text6)
								if not ok2 or not result2 then
									api.ClearCustomAsset(text6)
									return nil, "Custom asset registration failed"
								end
								task.delay(2, api.ClearCustomAsset, text6)
								return result2, text6
							end

							api.ClearCustomAsset = function(arg)
								local value24 = sharedState.CustomAssets.Files[arg]

								if value24 and delfile then
									pcall(delfile, value24)
								end

								sharedState.CustomAssets.Files[arg] = nil
							end

							api.ClearCustomAssets = function()
								for k in pairs(sharedState.CustomAssets.Requests) do
									local requests = sharedState.CustomAssets.Requests
									requests[k] = requests[k] + 1
								end

								for k in pairs(sharedState.CustomAssets.Files) do
									api.ClearCustomAsset(k)
								end
							end

							api.SetCursorIconColor = function(cursorIconColor)
								configValues.CursorIconColor = cursorIconColor
								local cursorIconImage = sharedState.CursorIconImage

								if not (cursorIconImage and cursorIconImage.Parent) then
									for _, value24 in uiLib.ScreenGui:QueryDescendants("ImageLabel") do
										local parent2 = value24.Parent

										if value24.ZIndex == 3 and parent2 and parent2:IsA("GuiObject") and parent2.ZIndex == 11000 then
											sharedState.CursorIconImage = value24
											cursorIconImage = value24
											break
										end
									end
								end

								if cursorIconImage then
									cursorIconImage.ImageColor3 = cursorIconColor
								end
							end

							api.ResolveAssetInput = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5, arg6)
								local match = tostring(arg or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
								local customAssets = sharedState.CustomAssets
								customAssets.Requests[arg2] = (customAssets.Requests[arg2] or 0) + 1
								local value24 = customAssets.Requests[arg2]

								if match == "" then
									api.ClearCustomAsset(arg2)
									arg4(arg5)
									return
								end

								local text6 = match:lower()
								local match2 = match:match("^%d+$") or text6:match("^rbxassetid://(%d+)$")

								if not match2 and text6:find("roblox.com", 1, true) then
									match2 = text6:match("[?&]id=(%d+)") or text6:match("/asset/(%d+)") or text6:match("/library/(%d+)")
								end

								if match2 then
									api.ClearCustomAsset(arg2)
									local num

									if arg6 then
										num = tonumber(match2)
									else
										num = "rbxassetid://" .. match2
									end

									arg4(num, false)
									return
								end

								local text7

								if arg3 == "sound" then
									text7 = ".mp3"
								else
									text7 = "image"
								end

								if not text6:match("^https?://") or arg3 == "sound" and not text6:match("%.mp3[?#]?.*$") then
									local value25 = notify
									local text8 = ("Enter a Roblox %* ID or a direct %* link."):format(arg3, text7)
									value25(text8)
									return
								end

								local text8 = ".mp3"

								if arg3 ~= "sound" then
									local match3 = (text6:match("^[^?#]+") or text6):match("%.([%w]+)$")

									if match3 ~= "png" and match3 ~= "jpg" and match3 ~= "jpeg" and match3 ~= "webp" then
										match3 = "png"
									end

									text8 = "." .. match3
								end

								task.spawn(function()
									local value25, value26 = api.LoadCustomAsset(match, "Resonance" .. arg2, text8)

									if value24 ~= customAssets.Requests[arg2] or unloaded then
										value25 = value25 and value26

										if value25 then
											api.ClearCustomAsset(value26)
										end

										return
									end

									if not value25 then
										local text9 = arg2:gsub("(%l)(%u)", "%1 %2")
										local value27 = notify
										local text10 = ("Couldn't load the %*: %*."):format(text9:lower(), value26)
										value27(text10)
										return
									end

									api.ClearCustomAsset(arg2)
									customAssets.Files[arg2] = value26
									arg4(value25, true)
								end)
							end

							api.Char = function(arg)
								if arg == nil then
									arg = localPlayer
								end

								if arg then
									if weakCache[arg] then
										local character = weakCache[arg].Character
										local rootPart = weakCache[arg].RootPart
										local humanoid = weakCache[arg].Humanoid
										if character and character.Parent and rootPart and rootPart.Parent and humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
											return character, rootPart, humanoid
										end
										weakCache[arg] = nil
									end

									local character = arg.Character

									if character then
										local realHrp = character:FindFirstChild("realHrp") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
										local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
										weakCache[arg] = { Character = character, RootPart = realHrp, Humanoid = humanoid }
										return character, realHrp, humanoid
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					do
						api.ToggleMotor6D = function(arg, enabled, arg2)
							if not arg then
								return
							end
							local humanoid = arg:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
							local torso = arg:FindFirstChild("Torso")
							humanoid = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
							if not torso or not humanoid then
								return
							end

							if not humanoid.Value then
								for _, value24 in torso:GetChildren() do
									if value24:IsA("Motor6D") then
										value24.Enabled = enabled
									end
								end
							end

							if arg2 and (enabled == false or humanoid.Value) then
								for _, value24 in { "Left Leg", "Right Leg", "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Head" }, nil, nil do
									local ballSocketConstraint = arg:FindFirstChild(value24)
									ballSocketConstraint = ballSocketConstraint and ballSocketConstraint:FindFirstChild("BallSocketConstraint")

									if ballSocketConstraint then
										ballSocketConstraint.Enabled = enabled
									end
								end
							end
						end

						api.RepositionLimbs = function(arg, arg2)
							local humanoidRootPart = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local torso = arg:FindFirstChild("Torso")
							if not humanoidRootPart or not torso then
								return
							end
							local neck = torso:FindFirstChild("Neck")
							if neck and neck.Enabled then
								return
							end
							local lookVector = humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector
							local vector = Vector3.new(lookVector.X, 0, lookVector.Z)
							if vector.Magnitude < 0.001 then
								return
							end
							local cframe = CFrame.lookAt(humanoidRootPart.Position, humanoidRootPart.Position + vector.Unit)

							for k, value24 in pairs(sharedState.LimbOffsetsRL) do
								local value25 = arg:FindFirstChild(k)

								if value25 and value25:IsA("BasePart") then
									local cFrame2 = cframe * value24

									if value25.CFrame ~= cFrame2 then
										value25.CFrame = cFrame2
									end

									if arg2 then
										api.ResetVelocity(value25)
									end
								end
							end
						end

						bodyVelocity = nil
						bodyGyro = nil
						value23 = nil

						api.StopFly = function()
							if bodyVelocity then
								bodyVelocity:Destroy()
								bodyVelocity = nil
							end

							if bodyGyro then
								bodyGyro:Destroy()
								bodyGyro = nil

								value26.PlatformStand = false
							end
						end

						api.StartFly = function(parent2)
							bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
							bodyGyro.P = 90000
							bodyGyro.D = 1000
							bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
							bodyGyro.CFrame = parent2.CFrame
							bodyGyro.Parent = parent2
							bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
							bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
							bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
							bodyVelocity.Parent = parent2
						end

						api.LightPhysicalProperties = PhysicalProperties.new(0.01, 0.01, 0.01, 0.01, 0.01)

						api.ToggleCollisions = function(arg, canCollide, arg2, arg3)
							if canCollide == nil then
								canCollide = false
							end

							local lightPhysicalProperties

							if arg3 then
								lightPhysicalProperties = api.LightPhysicalProperties
							else
								lightPhysicalProperties = nil
							end

							for _, child in pairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("BasePart") then
									local state12

									if canCollide then
										state12 = child.Name == "Torso" or child.Name == "Head"
									else
										state12 = canCollide
									end

									if state12 then
										if child.CanCollide ~= canCollide then
											child.CanCollide = canCollide
										end
									elseif not canCollide then
										if child.CanCollide ~= canCollide then
											child.CanCollide = canCollide
										end

										if not arg2 then
											local ragdollLimbPart = child:FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart")

											if ragdollLimbPart and ragdollLimbPart.CanCollide then
												ragdollLimbPart.CanCollide = false
											end
										end
									end

									if child.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and child.CustomPhysicalProperties ~= lightPhysicalProperties then
										child.CustomPhysicalProperties = lightPhysicalProperties
									end
								end
							end
						end

						api.CreateAlign = function(parent2, arg)
							local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
							attachment.Name = "RSC"
							attachment.Parent = parent2
							local alignPosition = Instance.new("AlignPosition")
							alignPosition.Mode = 0
							alignPosition.Name = "RSC"
							alignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
							alignPosition.Responsiveness = 200
							alignPosition.Parent = parent2
							alignPosition.Attachment0 = attachment

							alignPosition.Destroying:Once(function()
								if attachment then
									attachment:Destroy()
								end
							end)

							if arg then
								local alignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
								alignOrientation.Mode = 0
								alignOrientation.Name = "RSC"
								alignOrientation.MaxTorque = math.huge
								alignOrientation.Responsiveness = 200
								alignOrientation.Parent = parent2
								alignOrientation.Attachment0 = attachment

								alignOrientation.Destroying:Once(function()
									if attachment then
										attachment:Destroy()
									end
								end)

								return alignPosition, alignOrientation
							end

							return alignPosition
						end

						api.ResetVelocity = function(arg)
							if not arg or not arg:IsA("BasePart") then
								return false
							end

							if arg.AssemblyAngularVelocity ~= Vector3.zero then
								arg.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
							end

							if arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity ~= Vector3.zero then
								arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
							end

							return true
						end

						api.Teleport = function(arg, cFrame2, arg2, arg3)
							if not arg or not arg:IsA("BasePart") or not cFrame2 then
								return false
							end
							local value24 = api.Char()
							local value25 = value24 and arg:IsDescendantOf(value24)

							if typeof(cFrame2) == "CFrame" then
								if arg2 then
									cFrame2 *= arg2
								end
							elseif typeof(cFrame2) == "Vector3" then
								local cframe = CFrame.new

								if arg2 then
									cFrame2 += arg2.Position
								end

								cFrame2 = cframe(cFrame2)
							else
								if not (typeof(cFrame2) == "Instance" and cFrame2:IsA("BasePart")) then
									return false
								end

								if arg2 then
									cFrame2 = cFrame2.CFrame * arg2
								else
									cFrame2 = cFrame2.CFrame
								end
							end

							if value25 and (arg.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" or arg.Name == "Torso") and sharedState.States["Anti Grab"] then
								value22 = cFrame2
							end

if value25 and featureState.InvisibleTeleportation and (arg.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" or arg.Name == "Torso") and not sharedState.States.InvisibleTeleporting then
							local teleportDelta = cFrame2.Position - arg.Position

							if teleportDelta.Magnitude > 30 then
								if sharedState.States["Anti Grab"] then
									value22 = cFrame2
								end

								sharedState.States.InvisibleTeleporting = true

								task.spawn(function()
									local targetCFrame = cFrame2

									while arg.Parent and not unloaded do
										local remaining = targetCFrame.Position - arg.Position

										if remaining.Magnitude <= 30 then
											break
										end

										arg.CFrame = CFrame.new(arg.Position + remaining.Unit * 30) * (targetCFrame - targetCFrame.Position)
										RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
									end

									if arg.Parent then
										arg.CFrame = targetCFrame
									end

									api.ResetVelocity(arg)
									sharedState.States.InvisibleTeleporting = nil
								end)

								return true
							end
						end

													if arg.CFrame ~= cFrame2 then
								arg.CFrame = cFrame2
							end

							if arg3 ~= false then
								api.ResetVelocity(arg)
							end

							return true
						end

						api.PivotTo = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
							if not arg or not arg:IsA("Model") or typeof(arg2) ~= "CFrame" then
								return false
							end
							local num36 = arg2 * arg:GetPivot():Inverse()
							arg3 = arg3 or arg:QueryDescendants("BasePart")
							arg4 = arg4 or {}
							table.clear(arg4)

							for i, value24 in ipairs(arg3) do
								local assemblyRootPart = value24:IsDescendantOf(arg) and value24.AssemblyRootPart

								if assemblyRootPart and not value24.Anchored and not assemblyRootPart.Anchored then
									arg4[i] = num36 * value24.CFrame
								end
							end

							for k, value24 in pairs(arg4) do
								arg3[k].CFrame = value24
							end

							return true
						end

						api.BPTeleport = function(arg, position)
							local primaryPart

							if arg:IsA("Model") then
								primaryPart = arg.PrimaryPart
							else
								primaryPart = arg
							end

							if not primaryPart then
								return
							end
							local bodyPosition = primaryPart:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyPosition")

							if bodyPosition then
								bodyPosition:Destroy()
							end

							local bodyPosition2 = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
							bodyPosition2.Position = position
							bodyPosition2.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
							bodyPosition2.D = 250
							bodyPosition2.Parent = primaryPart
							api.ResetVelocity(arg)

							task.delay(1, function()
								bodyPosition2:Destroy()
							end)
						end

						api.GetMagnitude = function(arg, arg2)
							if not arg or not arg2 then
								return
							end
							return (arg.Position - arg2.Position).Magnitude
						end

						api.GetClosestPlayer = function(arg)
							local huge = math.huge
							local value24 = nil

							for _, value25 in pairs(sharedState.PlayerList) do
								local value26, value27 = api.Char(value25)

								if value27 then
									local magnitude = (value27.Position - arg.Position).Magnitude

									if magnitude < huge then
										huge = magnitude
										value24 = value25
									end
								end
							end

							return value24, huge
						end

						api.OwnedPlot = function(arg)
							local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
							if not plots then
								return
							end

							for _, child in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
								local plotSign = child:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
								if not plotSign then
									continue
								end
								local thisPlotsOwners = plotSign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
								if not thisPlotsOwners then
									continue
								end

								for _, child2 in pairs(thisPlotsOwners:GetChildren()) do
									if child2.Value == arg.Name then
										return child
									end
								end
							end
						end

						api.WhichPlot = function(arg)
							local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
							if not plots then
								return
							end

							for _, child in pairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
								local plotArea = child:FindFirstChild("PlotArea")
								if not plotArea then
									continue
								end
								local partsInPart = workspace:GetPartsInPart(plotArea)

								for _, value24 in pairs(partsInPart) do
									if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(value24.Parent) == arg then
										local inOwnedPlot2 = arg:FindFirstChild("InOwnedPlot")
										return child, inOwnedPlot2 and inOwnedPlot2.Value
									end
								end
							end
						end

						api.CheckPlot = function(arg)
							local table16 = {}
							local plotArea = arg:FindFirstChild("PlotArea")
							if not plotArea then
								return
							end

							for _, value24 in pairs(workspace:GetPartsInPart(plotArea)) do
								local playerFromCharacter = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(value24.Parent)

								if playerFromCharacter and not table16[playerFromCharacter] and playerFromCharacter:FindFirstChild("InOwnedPlot") and playerFromCharacter.InOwnedPlot.Value == true then
									table16[playerFromCharacter] = true
								end
							end

							if not next(table16) then
								return nil
							end
							return true, table16
						end

						api.BreakPlot = function(arg)
							local inPlot2 = nil

							if arg:IsA("Player") then
								inPlot2 = arg:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
								if not inPlot2 then
									return
								end
								arg = api.WhichPlot(arg)
								if not arg then
									return
								end
							end

							if sharedState.BrokenPlots[arg.Name] then
								return true
							end
							local plotArea = arg:FindFirstChild("PlotArea")
							if not plotArea then
								return
							end
							local NinjaShuriken = api.SpawnToy("NinjaShuriken")
							if not NinjaShuriken then
								return
							end
							local stickyPart = NinjaShuriken and NinjaShuriken:WaitForChild("StickyPart", 0.4)
							local stickyWeld = stickyPart and stickyPart:WaitForChild("StickyWeld", 0.4)
							local cframe = CFrame.new(1099511627776, 1099511627776, 1099511627776)

							if arg.Name == "Plot5" then
								cframe = CFrame.new(2147483648, 2147483648, 2147483648)
							end

							if not stickyWeld or not api.SNO(stickyPart) then
								return
							end
							remotes.StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, plotArea, cframe)
							local now3 = tick()

							while true do
								task.wait()
								if not (stickyWeld.Part1 == plotArea and (not inPlot2 or not inPlot2.Value) or not stickyWeld.Parent or tick() - now3 > 0.8) then
									continue
								end
								break
							end

							if not stickyWeld.Parent or stickyWeld.Part1 ~= plotArea or inPlot2 and inPlot2.Value == true then
								if NinjaShuriken then
									api.DestroyToy(NinjaShuriken)
								end

								return false
							end

							sharedState.BrokenPlots[arg.Name] = NinjaShuriken

							NinjaShuriken.Destroying:Once(function()
								sharedState.BrokenPlots[arg.Name] = nil
							end)

							return NinjaShuriken
						end

						api.DestroyPlot = function(arg)
							local plotItems = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
							if not plotItems then
								return
							end
							local value24 = plotItems:FindFirstChild(arg)
							if not value24 or not next(value24:GetChildren()) then
								return
							end
							local YouDecoy = api.SpawnToy("YouDecoy")
							if not YouDecoy then
								return
							end
							YouDecoy.Name = "HouseBreakerRSC"
							local humanoidRootPart = YouDecoy:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 0.4)
							if not humanoidRootPart or not api.SNO(humanoidRootPart) then
								api.DestroyToy(YouDecoy)
								return false
							end
							api.RNO(humanoidRootPart)
							local bodyVelocity2 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
							bodyVelocity2.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
							bodyVelocity2.Velocity = Vector3.zero
							bodyVelocity2.Parent = humanoidRootPart
							local bodyAngularVelocity = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
							bodyAngularVelocity.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
							bodyAngularVelocity.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(500000, 500000, 500000)
							bodyAngularVelocity.Parent = humanoidRootPart

							for _, child in ipairs(value24:GetChildren()) do
								if not (not child:IsA("Model") or not YouDecoy.Parent) then
									local primaryPart = child.PrimaryPart or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

									if primaryPart then
										local now3 = tick()

										repeat
											humanoidRootPart.CFrame = primaryPart.CFrame
											task.spawn(api.RepositionLimbs, YouDecoy, true)
											RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
										until tick() - now3 > 0.25
									end
								end
							end

							bodyVelocity2:Destroy()
							bodyAngularVelocity:Destroy()
							api.DestroyToy(YouDecoy)
						end

						api.ClaimPlot = function(arg)
							local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
							if not plots then
								return
							end

							if api.OwnedPlot(localPlayer) then
								return
							end
							local value24 = plots:FindFirstChild(arg)
							if not value24 then
								return
							end

							if api.CheckPlot(value24) then
								return
							end
							local plotSign = value24:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
							if not plotSign then
								return
							end
							local sign = plotSign:FindFirstChild("Sign")
							if not sign then
								return
							end
							local plus = sign:FindFirstChild("Plus")
							if not plus then
								return
							end
							local plusGrabPart = plus:FindFirstChild("PlusGrabPart")
							if not plusGrabPart then
								return
							end
							api.SNO(plusGrabPart)
							return plusGrabPart:FindFirstChild("AnchoredPartGrab") and plusGrabPart.AnchoredPartGrab.Value == localPlayer.Name
						end

						api.IsNetworkOwner = function(arg)
							if not (arg and arg:IsA("BasePart")) then
								return
							end
							local assemblyRootPart = arg.AssemblyRootPart or arg
							return not assemblyRootPart.Anchored and assemblyRootPart.ReceiveAge == 0
						end

						api.IsPartOwner = function(arg)
							if not (arg and arg:IsA("BasePart")) then
								return
							end
							local value24 = sharedState.PartOwnerCache[arg]
							if value24 and value24.Parent then
								return value24.Value == localPlayer.Name
							end
							local parent2 = arg.Parent
							local head = arg.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" and parent2 and parent2:FindFirstChild("Head")
							head = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
							if head and head.Value == localPlayer.Name then
								sharedState.PartOwnerCache[arg] = head
								return true
							end
							local partOwner = arg:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
							if partOwner and partOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name then
								sharedState.PartOwnerCache[arg] = partOwner
								return true
							end
							parent2 = parent2 and parent2:FindFirstChild("PartOwner", true)
							if parent2 then
								sharedState.PartOwnerCache[arg] = parent2
								return parent2.Value == localPlayer.Name
							end
							return false
						end

						api.IsProtectedPlayer = function(arg, arg2)
							local state12 = sharedState.AdminImmune[arg] == true

							if not state12 then
								state12 = arg2 == true and featureState.WhitelistedBypass and sharedState.Whitelisted[arg] == true
							end

							return state12
						end

						do
							local weakCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

							api.GetCustomVelocity = function(arg)
								local now3 = os.clock()
								local position = arg.Position
								local value24 = weakCache[arg]
								if not value24 then
									weakCache[arg] = { Position = position, Time = now3, Linear = Vector3.zero }
									return Vector3.zero
								end
								local num36 = now3 - value24.Time
								if num36 < 0.001 then
									return value24.Linear
								end
								local num37 = (position - value24.Position) / num36

								if num36 > 0.25 or num37.Magnitude > 300 then
									value24.Linear = Vector3.zero
								else
									value24.Linear = value24.Linear:Lerp(num37, 1 - math.exp(-num36 * 40))

									if value24.Linear.Magnitude < 0.05 then
										value24.Linear = Vector3.zero
									end
								end

								value24.Position = position
								value24.Time = now3
								return value24.Linear
							end
						end
					end

					api.PredictCFrame = function(arg, arg2)
						local cFrame2 = arg2 or arg.CFrame
						if not featureState.PositionPrediction then
							return cFrame2
						end
						local num36 = math.clamp(localPlayer:GetNetworkPing(), 0, 0.35)
						return cFrame2 + api.GetCustomVelocity(arg) * num36
					end

					api.Grab = function(arg, arg2)
						local value24, value25, value26 = api.Char()
						if not value25 or not value26 or value26.Health == 0 or not arg or not arg.Parent or not arg:IsA("BasePart") then
							return
						end
						local num36 = (arg2 or value25.CFrame) * CFrame.new(0, 1.593, 0)
						local cframe = api.PredictCFrame(arg)

						if not featureState.DirectGrab then
							cframe = CFrame.new(num36.Position, cframe.Position)
						end

						remotes.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(arg, cframe)
					end

					api.SNO = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
						local value24, value25, value26 = api.Char()
						local head = value24 and value24:FindFirstChild("Head")
						local ragdolled = value26 and value26:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
						if not (head and ragdolled and value25 and value26 and value26.Health > 0) then
							return false
						end

						if not arg or not arg:IsA("BasePart") or not arg.Parent or api.GetMagnitude(arg, workspace.SpawnLocation) > 10000000 then
							return false
						end

						if api.IsPartOwner(arg) then
							return true
						end

						if sharedState.States.Owning or arg3 and not arg3() then
							return false
						end
						local state12 = false
						local cframe = CFrame.new()
						local cframe2 = CFrame.new()
						local cframe3 = CFrame.new()
						local connection = nil

						if (value25.Position - arg.Position).Magnitude >= 31 then
							state12 = true
						end

						local state13 = arg.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" or arg.Name == "Torso"
						local value27 = nil

						if state13 then
							local value28 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg.Parent.Name)
							value27 = nil

							if value28 then
								local value29, value30, value31 = api.Char(value28)
								if not value31 or value31.Health == 0 then
									return false
								end
								local inPlot2 = value28:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
								inPlot2 = inPlot2 and inPlot2.Value == true
								value27 = nil

								if inPlot2 then
									value27 = api.BreakPlot(value28)
									if not value27 then
										return false
									end
								end
							end
						end

						sharedState.States.Owning = true

						local function func21()
							cframe2 = value25.CFrame
							cframe = head.CFrame
							cframe3 = value25.CFrame

							RunService:BindToRenderStep("RSCClient", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
								value25.CFrame = cframe2
								head.CFrame = cframe
							end)

							connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
								cframe2 = value25.CFrame
								cframe = head.CFrame
								value25.CFrame = cframe3
								head.CFrame = cframe3
							end)
						end

						local function func22()
							RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RSCClient")

							if connection then
								connection:Disconnect()
								connection = nil
							end

							head.CFrame = cframe
							value25.CFrame = cframe2
						end

						local ok, result = pcall(function()
							if state12 then
								func21()
							end

							arg2 = arg2 or 0.65
							local now3 = tick()

							while not unloaded and arg.Parent and not api.IsPartOwner(arg) and value26.Health ~= 0 and value25.Parent and (not arg3 or arg3()) and tick() - now3 < arg2 do
								if state12 then
									cframe3 = api.PredictCFrame(arg) - Vector3.new(0, 22, 0)
								end

								RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
								local grab = api.Grab
								local value28

								if state12 then
									value28 = cframe3
								else
									value28 = nil
								end

								grab(arg, value28)
							end
						end)

						if state12 then
							pcall(func22)
						end

						if value27 then
							task.delay(1, api.DestroyToy, value27)
						end

						sharedState.States.Owning = false
						if not ok then
							return warn(result)
						end
						return api.IsPartOwner(arg)
					end

					api.RNO = function(arg)
						remotes.DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(arg)
					end

					api.DisableEndGrabEarly = function()
						local grabEvents = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents")
						if not grabEvents then
							return
						end
						local endGrabEarly = grabEvents:FindFirstChild("EndGrabEarly")
						if endGrabEarly == remoteEvent then
							return endGrabEarly
						end

						if endGrabEarly then
							endGrabEarly:Destroy()
						end

						remoteEvent = Instance.new("RemoteEvent")
						remoteEvent.Name = "EndGrabEarly"
						remoteEvent.Parent = grabEvents
						return remoteEvent
					end

					do
						local function func21(arg)
							return #tostring(arg) / 1000000
						end

						api.FormatPlayer = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
							local value24

							if type(arg) ~= "string" then
								value24 = arg
							else
								value24 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg)
							end

							if value24 == nil then
								return "???"
							end

							if value24 == localPlayer and arg3 then
								return "You"
							end
							local text6 = "%*%* (@%*)"
							local format = text6.format
							local displayName = value24.DisplayName
							local text7

							if arg2 then
								text7 = "\n"
							else
								text7 = ""
							end

							return (format(text6, displayName, text7, value24.Name))
						end

						local baseFormatPlayer = api.FormatPlayer
						local streamerNameCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
						local streamerNameCount = 0
						local streamerFriends = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

						local function streamerTargetActive(value24)
							if not value24 or not toggles.StreamerMode or not toggles.StreamerMode.Value then
								return false
							end

							local streamerScope = configValues.StreamerScope

							if streamerScope == "only You" then
								return value24 == localPlayer
							end

							return true
						end

						api.FormatPlayer = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
							local value24

							if type(arg) ~= "string" then
								value24 = arg
							else
								value24 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg)
							end

							if streamerTargetActive(value24) then
								local streamerName = streamerNameCache[value24]

								if not streamerName then
									streamerNameCount += 1
									local streamerNick = configValues.StreamerNickname
								if streamerNick == nil or streamerNick == "" then
									streamerNick = "redacted"
								end
								streamerName = streamerNick .. streamerNameCount
									streamerNameCache[value24] = streamerName
								end

								return ("%s%s (@%s)"):format(streamerName, arg2 and "\n" or "", streamerName)
							end

							return baseFormatPlayer(arg, arg2, arg3)
						end

						task.spawn(function()
							while task.wait(5) do
								if unloaded then
									return
								end

								if not toggles.StreamerMode or not toggles.StreamerMode.Value or configValues.StreamerScope ~= "Friends" then
									continue
								end

								for _, value25 in Players:GetPlayers() do
									if value25 ~= localPlayer and streamerFriends[value25] == nil then
										local ok, isFriend = pcall(localPlayer.IsFriendsWith, localPlayer, value25.UserId)

										if ok then
											streamerFriends[value25] = isFriend and true or false
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end)

						api.PacketHandler = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
							if arg == localPlayer then
								return
							end

							if typeof(arg2) ~= "string" and typeof(arg2) ~= "number" then
								return
							end
							local text6 = tostring(arg2)
							if #text6 < 400 then
								return
							end
							local table16 = sharedState.PacketData[arg]

							if not table16 then
								table16 = {}
								sharedState.PacketData[arg] = table16
							end

							local now3 = tick()
							local lastSize = #text6
							local preview = string.sub(text6, 1, 15)
							local value24 = api.FormatPlayer(arg)
							local text7 = string.format("%.2f", func21(text6))
							local text8

							if arg3 == "CGL" then
								text8 = "Silent"
							else
								text8 = "Normal"
							end

							local method = text8

							if not table16.IsLagging then
								table16.IsLagging = true
								table16.SizeChanged = false
								notify("Player: " .. value24 .. "\n" .. "Status: Started packet lagging\n" .. "Method: " .. method .. "\n" .. "Packet Size: " .. text7 .. " MB\n" .. "Preview: " .. preview, "Packet Lag Detected", nil, 7)
							end

							if table16.LastSize then
								local num36 = math.abs(lastSize - table16.LastSize)

								if num36 >= 25000 and not table16.SizeChanged then
									table16.SizeChanged = true
									notify("Player: " .. value24 .. "\n" .. "Status: Packet size changed\n" .. "Method: " .. method .. "\n" .. "New Packet Size: " .. text7 .. " MB\n" .. "Difference: " .. math.floor(num36 / 1024) .. " KB\n" .. "Preview: " .. preview, "Packet Size Changed", nil, 6)
								end
							end

							table16.LastTime = now3
							table16.LastSize = lastSize

							if table16.LagCheck then
								task.cancel(table16.LagCheck)
							end

							table16.LagCheck = task.delay(8, function()
								if not sharedState.PacketData[arg] then
									return
								end
								local lastTime = table16.LastTime

								if tick() - lastTime >= 8 then
									table16.IsLagging = false
									table16.SizeChanged = false
									table16.LagCheck = nil
									table16.LastSize = nil
									notify("Player: " .. value24 .. "\n" .. "Status: Stopped packet lagging\n" .. "Method: " .. method, "Packet Lag Ended")
								end
							end)
						end
					end

					api.LineLagHandler = function(arg, lastPart, arg2)
						if typeof(lastPart) ~= "Instance" or typeof(arg2) ~= "CFrame" or arg == localPlayer then
							return
						end
						local table16 = sharedState.LineData[arg]

						if not table16 then
							table16 = {
								Count = 0,
								FirstTime = 0,
								LastPart = nil,
								PartSwitches = 0,
								IsLagging = false,
								LastTime = 0,
								Initialized = false,
								NotificationCooldown = 0,
							}

							sharedState.LineData[arg] = table16
						end

						local num36 = 2
						local num37 = 3
						local now3 = tick()
						local value24 = api.FormatPlayer(arg)

						if not table16.Initialized then
							table16.FirstTime = now3
							table16.Initialized = true
							return
						end

						if now3 - table16.FirstTime < 1 then
							return
						end
						local position = arg2.Position
						local num38 = math.abs(position.X) + math.abs(position.Y) + math.abs(position.Z)
						local state12 = lastPart and lastPart:IsA("BasePart") and lastPart.CanQuery == false

						if table16.LastPart and table16.LastPart ~= lastPart then
							table16.PartSwitches = table16.PartSwitches + 1
						end

						table16.LastPart = lastPart

						if now3 - table16.FirstTime > 2 then
							table16.FirstTime = now3
							table16.Count = 0
							table16.PartSwitches = 0
						end

						table16.Count = table16.Count + 1
						table16.LastTime = now3

						if table16.Count >= 20 and (num38 > 10000 or table16.PartSwitches >= 10 or state12) and not table16.IsLagging and now3 - table16.NotificationCooldown >= num37 then
							table16.IsLagging = true
							table16.NotificationCooldown = now3
							notify("Player: " .. value24 .. "\nStatus: Line lag started", "Line Lag Detected")
						end

						if table16.LagCheck then
							task.cancel(table16.LagCheck)
							table16.LagCheck = nil
						end

						if table16.IsLagging then
							table16.LagCheck = task.delay(2, function()
								if not sharedState.LineData[arg] then
									return
								end

								if not table16.IsLagging then
									return
								end

								if tick() - (table16.LastTime or 0) >= num36 then
									table16.IsLagging = false
									table16.Count = 0
									table16.PartSwitches = 0
									local notificationCooldown = table16.NotificationCooldown

									if tick() - notificationCooldown >= num37 then
										table16.NotificationCooldown = tick()
										notify("Player: " .. value24 .. "\nStatus: Line lag stopped", "Line Lag Ended")
									end
								end
							end)
						end
					end

                    api.QueueTeleport = function(antiLag, reload)
                        local queue = queue_on_teleport or queueonteleport or (syn and syn.queue_on_teleport)
                        if type(queue) ~= "function" then
                            if reload then notify("This executor does not support queue_on_teleport") end
                            return false
                        end
                        if reload and not reloadPath then
                            notify("Local reload is unavailable: file persistence failed")
                            return false
                        end
                        local source = string.format([[
repeat task.wait() until game:IsLoaded()
local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
if not player then return end
local scripts = player:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 15)
if scripts then
    for _, name in ipairs({"CharacterAndBeamMove", "StickyPartsTouchDetection"}) do
        local item = scripts:WaitForChild(name, 10)
        if item then item.Enabled = not %s end
    end
end
if %s then
    local env = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
    env.ResonanceRunning = false
    env.ResonanceStarting = false
    local source = assert(readfile, "readfile unavailable")(%q)
    local run, message = loadstring(source, "@Resonance")
    assert(run, message)
    run()
end
]], tostring(antiLag == true), tostring(reload == true), reloadPath or "")
                        local ok, err = pcall(queue, source)
                        if not ok then notify("Unable to queue teleport: " .. tostring(err)) end
                        return ok
                    end

					api.Rejoin = function(arg, arg2)
						if not jobId or jobId == "" then
							notify("Job ID not found! This might be because your executor is protecting you from other people joining your server. Couldn't rejoin")
							return
						end
						api.QueueTeleport(arg, arg2)

						if #sharedState.PlayerList <= 1 then
							localPlayer:Kick("\nRejoining...")
							task.wait()
							TeleportService:Teleport(placeId, localPlayer)
						else
							TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, jobId, localPlayer)
						end
					end

					methodQueue.Compact = function(arg)
						local items = {}
						local tail = 0

						for i = arg.Head, arg.Tail do
							local value24 = arg.Items[i]

							if value24 then
								tail += 1
								items[tail] = value24
							end
						end

						arg.Items = items
						arg.Head = 1
						arg.Tail = tail
					end

					methodQueue.Enqueue = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
						if not (arg and arg2 and arg3) then
							return
						end
						arg4 = arg4 or arg
						local table16 = methodQueue.Workers[arg]

						if not table16 then
							table16 = { Items = {}, Pending = {}, Head = 1, Tail = 0, Running = false }
							methodQueue.Workers[arg] = table16
						end

						local num36 = methodQueue.Generations[arg4] or 0
						local value24 = table16.Pending[arg3]
						if value24 and value24.Owner == arg4 and value24.Epoch == methodQueue.Epoch and value24.Generation == num36 then
							return
						end
						local table17 = { Callback = arg2, Player = arg3, Owner = arg4, Epoch = methodQueue.Epoch, Generation = num36 }
						table16.Tail = table16.Tail + 1
						table16.Items[table16.Tail] = table17
						table16.Pending[arg3] = table17
						if table16.Running then
							return
						end
						table16.Running = true

						task.spawn(function()
							while not unloaded do
								local value25 = table16.Items[table16.Head]

								if value25 then
									table16.Items[table16.Head] = nil
									table16.Head = table16.Head + 1
									local state12 = value25.Epoch == methodQueue.Epoch

									if state12 then
										state12 = value25.Generation == (methodQueue.Generations[value25.Owner] or 0)
									end

									if state12 and value25.Player.Parent then
										local ok, result = pcall(value25.Callback, value25.Player)

										if not ok then
											warn(("[Resonance] %s failed: %s"):format(arg, tostring(result)))
										end

										task.wait()
									end

									if table16.Pending[value25.Player] == value25 then
										table16.Pending[value25.Player] = nil
									end

									if table16.Head > 128 and table16.Head > table16.Tail / 2 then
										methodQueue.Compact(table16)
									end

									continue
								end

								break
							end

							table16.Items = {}
							table16.Pending = {}
							table16.Head = 1
							table16.Tail = 0
							table16.Running = false
						end)
					end

					methodQueue.Cancel = function(arg)
						methodQueue.Generations[arg] = (methodQueue.Generations[arg] or 0) + 1
					end

					methodQueue.CancelAll = function()
						methodQueue.Epoch = methodQueue.Epoch + 1

						for _, worker in pairs(methodQueue.Workers) do
							worker.Pending = {}
						end
					end

					api.IsTerminalDisconnect = function()
						local ok, result = pcall(GuiService.GetErrorCode, GuiService)

						if not ok then
							ok, result = pcall(GuiService.GetErrorType, GuiService)
						end

						if not ok then
							return false
						end
						local value

						if typeof(result) == "EnumItem" then
							value = result.Value
						else
							value = tonumber(result)
						end

						return value ~= nil and value >= Enum.ConnectionError.DisconnectErrors.Value and value < Enum.ConnectionError.PlacelaunchErrors.Value
					end

					api.AimRaycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
					api.AimRaycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

					api.GetAimTarget = function()
						local aimSettings = sharedState.AimSettings
						local character = localPlayer.Character
						local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if not humanoidRootPart then
							return nil
						end
						local position = currentCamera.CFrame.Position
						local position2 = humanoidRootPart.Position
						local x = mouse.X
						local y = mouse.Y

						if uiLib.IsMobile then
							x = currentCamera.ViewportSize.X / 2
							y = currentCamera.ViewportSize.Y / 2
						end

						local worldToViewportPoint

						if uiLib.IsMobile then
							worldToViewportPoint = currentCamera.WorldToViewportPoint
						else
							worldToViewportPoint = currentCamera.WorldToScreenPoint
						end

						local num36 = aimSettings.FOVRadius * aimSettings.FOVRadius
						local num37 = aimSettings.SADist * aimSettings.SADist
						local aimRaycastFilter = sharedState.AimRaycastFilter
						local aimRaycastParams = api.AimRaycastParams
						local huge = math.huge
						local value24 = nil

						for _, value25 in pairs(sharedState.PlayerList) do
							if value25 ~= localPlayer and value25.Character and not api.IsProtectedPlayer(value25, true) then
								local inPlot2 = value25:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

								if not (inPlot2 and inPlot2.Value) then
									local value26 = value25.Character:FindFirstChild(aimSettings.TargetPart)
									local humanoid = value25.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

									if value26 and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
										local value27, value28 = worldToViewportPoint(currentCamera, value26.Position)

										if value28 then
											local num38 = value27.X - x
											local num39 = value27.Y - y

											if num38 * num38 + num39 * num39 <= num36 then
												local character2 = value25.Character
												aimRaycastFilter[1] = character
												aimRaycastFilter[2] = character2
												aimRaycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = aimRaycastFilter
												local position3 = value26.Position

												if not workspace:Raycast(position, position3 - position, aimRaycastParams) then
													local num40 = position2 - position3
													local value29 = num40:Dot(num40)

													if value29 < huge and value29 <= num37 then
														huge = value29
														value24 = value25
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end

						return value24
					end

					api.Click = function()
						if not uiLib.IsMobile then
							if mouse1click then
								return mouse1click()
							end
							mouse1press()
							task.wait()

							if mouse1release then
								mouse1release()
							end

							return
						end

						if pcall(function()
							ContextActionService:CallFunction("Grab", Enum.UserInputState.Begin, game)
						end) then
							return
						end
						local button = ContextActionService:GetButton("Grab")
						if not button or not button.Visible then
							return
						end
						local num36 = button.AbsolutePosition + button.AbsoluteSize / 2
						VirtualInputManager:SendTouchEvent(1, 0, num36.X, num36.Y)
						RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
						VirtualInputManager:SendTouchEvent(1, 2, num36.X, num36.Y)
					end

					api.TweenFOV = function(arg, arg2)
						if tween then
							tween:Cancel()
							tween = nil
						end

						local num36 = math.clamp(arg, 1, 120)
						tween = TweenService:Create(currentCamera, TweenInfo.new(arg2 or 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { FieldOfView = num36 })
						tween:Play()
						return tween
					end
				end

				do
					api.SetFOV = function(arg, arg2)
						if arg2 then
							configValues.ZoomFOV = math.clamp(arg, configValues.ZoomMinFOV, configValues.ZoomMaxFOV)
							return api.TweenFOV(configValues.ZoomFOV, 0.2)
						end
						fieldOfView = math.clamp(arg, 30, 120)
						if not featureState.Zoom then
							return api.TweenFOV(fieldOfView, 0.25)
						end
					end

					api.UpdateZoomCamera = function(arg)
						if unloaded then
							pcall(function()
								RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RSCZoom")
							end)

							return
						end

						if not featureState.Zoom or currentCamera.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
							cFrame = nil
							return
						end
						local cFrame2 = currentCamera.CFrame
						if not cFrame then
							cFrame = cFrame2
							return
						end
						cFrame = cFrame:Lerp(cFrame2, math.clamp(1 - math.exp(-(configValues.ZoomCameraSmoothness or 14) * (arg or 0.016666666666666666)), 0, 1))
						currentCamera.CFrame = cFrame
					end

					pcall(function()
						RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RSCZoom")
					end)

					do
						local value24 = nil
						local value25 = nil
						local value26 = nil
						local value27 = nil
						local value28 = nil
						local value29 = nil

						local function createPart(name)
							local part4 = Instance.new("Part")
							part4.Name = name
							part4.Anchored = true
							part4.CanCollide = false
							part4.CanQuery = false
							part4.CanTouch = false
							local smoothPlastic = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
							part4.CastShadow = false
							part4.Transparency = 0.5
							part4.Material = smoothPlastic
							part4.Parent = workspace
							return part4
						end

						local function func21(arg, arg2, arg3)
							local value30 = createPart(arg)
							value30.Size = Vector3.one * 0.05
							value30.Transparency = 1
							local table16 = {}
							local table17 = {}

							for i = 1, 3 do
								local table18 = {}

								for i2 = 1, 24 do
									local num36 = (i2 - 1) / 24 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
									local num37 = math.cos(num36) * arg2
									local num38 = math.sin(num36) * arg2
									local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", value30)
									local vector

									if i == 1 then
										vector = Vector3.new(num37, num38, 0)
									elseif i == 2 then
										vector = Vector3.new(num37, 0, num38)
									else
										vector = Vector3.new(0, num37, num38)
									end

									attachment.Position = vector
									table18[i2] = attachment
									table16[#table16 + 1] = { attachment, i, num36 }
								end

								for i2 = 1, 24 do
									local beam = Instance.new("Beam", value30)
									local value31 = table18[i2]
									local value32 = table18[i2 % 24 + 1]
									beam.Attachment0 = value31
									beam.Attachment1 = value32
									local colorSequence = ColorSequence.new(arg3)
									local numberSequence = NumberSequence.new(0.5)
									beam.Color = colorSequence
									beam.Transparency = numberSequence
									local wrap = Enum.TextureMode.Wrap
									beam.Texture = sharedState.LineTypes.Dots
									beam.TextureMode = wrap
									beam.TextureLength = 0.75
									beam.Width0 = 0.12
									beam.Width1 = 0.12
									beam.FaceCamera = true
									table17[#table17 + 1] = beam
								end
							end

							return value30, table16, table17
						end

						api.UpdateAuraVisuals = function()
							local value30
							value30, value30 = api.Char()
							if not value30 then
								return
							end

							if value24 then
								value24.CFrame = CFrame.new(value30.Position)
							end

							if value25 then
								value25.CFrame = CFrame.new(value30.Position)
							end
						end

						api.UpdateAuraVisualSettings = function()
							local function func22(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
								local value30 = ipairs
								arg2 = arg2 or {}

								for _, value31 in value30(arg2) do
									value31.Color = ColorSequence.new(arg4)
								end

								local value31 = ipairs
								arg = arg or {}

								for _, value32 in value31(arg) do
									local num36 = math.cos(value32[3]) * arg3
									local num37 = math.sin(value32[3]) * arg3
									local value33 = value32[1]
									local vector

									if value32[2] == 1 then
										vector = Vector3.new(num36, num37, 0)
									elseif value32[2] == 2 then
										vector = Vector3.new(num36, 0, num37)
									else
										vector = Vector3.new(0, num36, num37)
									end

									value33.Position = vector
								end
							end

							func22(value26, value27, configValues.AuraDistance, sharedState.AuraVisualSettings.AuraColor)
							func22(value28, value29, configValues.ToyAuraDistance, sharedState.AuraVisualSettings.ToyAuraColor)
						end

						api.RefreshAuraVisuals = function()
							if value24 then
								value24:Destroy()
								value24 = nil
							end

							if value25 then
								value25:Destroy()
								value25 = nil
							end

							value26 = nil
							value27 = nil
							value28 = nil
							value29 = nil

							if featureState.AuraVisualizer and featureState.Aura then
								local AuraRadius, value30, value31 = func21("AuraRadius", configValues.AuraDistance, sharedState.AuraVisualSettings.AuraColor)
								value24 = AuraRadius
								value26 = value30
								value27 = value31
							end

							if featureState.ToyAuraVisualizer and featureState.ToyAuras then
								local ToyAuraRadius, value30, value31 = func21("ToyAuraRadius", configValues.ToyAuraDistance, sharedState.AuraVisualSettings.ToyAuraColor)
								value25 = ToyAuraRadius
								value28 = value30
								value29 = value31
							end

							api.UpdateAuraVisualSettings()
							api.UpdateAuraVisuals()
							local setRuntimeConnection = api.SetRuntimeConnection
							local renderStepped = RunService.RenderStepped
							local value30 = value24
							local value31

							if value24 then
								value31 = value30
							else
								value31 = value25
							end

							setRuntimeConnection("AuraVisuals", renderStepped, value31, api.UpdateAuraVisuals)
						end
					end
				end

				api.ClearAuraVisuals = function()
					local value24 = featureState
					featureState.AuraVisualizer = false
					value24.ToyAuraVisualizer = false
					api.RefreshAuraVisuals()
				end

				api.GetTriggerTarget = function()
					local aimSettings = sharedState.AimSettings
					local silentAimState = sharedState.SilentAimState
					if unloaded or not aimSettings.TriggerbotEnabled or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() or workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts") then
						return
					end
					local value24, value25, value26 = api.Char()
					if not (value25 and value26 and value26.Health > 0) then
						return
					end
					local instance

					if featureState.SilentAim and silentAimState.RaycastActive then
						local hit = silentAimState.Hit

						if hit then
							hit = os.clock() - (silentAimState.HitTime or 0) < 0.15
						end

						if hit then
							instance = silentAimState.Hit.Instance
						else
							instance = nil
						end
					else
						instance = api.GetTarget()
					end

					if not (instance and instance.Parent) or (instance.Position - value25.Position).Magnitude > aimSettings.TriggerDist then
						return
					end
					local model = instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
					local playerFromCharacter = model and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
					if not playerFromCharacter or playerFromCharacter == localPlayer or api.IsProtectedPlayer(playerFromCharacter, true) or featureState.SilentAim and not api.IsSilentAimTarget(playerFromCharacter) then
						return
					end
					local value27, value28, value29 = api.Char(playerFromCharacter)
					if value29 and value29.Health > 0 then
						return playerFromCharacter
					end
				end

				api.PlayTriggerTrick = function(arg, arg2)
					local aimSettings = sharedState.AimSettings
					local value24, value25, value26 = api.Char()
					local value27, value28, value29 = api.Char(arg2)
					local currentCamera2 = workspace.CurrentCamera
					local triggerTrick = aimSettings.TriggerTrick
					if not (value25 and value26 and value26.Health > 0 and currentCamera2 and value28 and value29 and value29.Health > 0) then
						return false
					end
					local cFrame2 = currentCamera2.CFrame
					local cameraType = currentCamera2.CameraType
					local num36 = 0
					local position = value25.Position
					local rotation = value25.CFrame.Rotation
					local autoRotate = value26.AutoRotate
					local num37

					if triggerTrick == "720 Spin" then
						num37 = 2
					else
						num37 = 1
					end

					local value30 = num37
					local num38 = 0.175 * value30 / math.clamp(aimSettings.TriggerTrickSpeed, 0.75, 2)

					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						aimSettings.TriggerSpinning = true
						local value31 = value26
						currentCamera2.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
						value31.AutoRotate = false

						repeat
							num36 += RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
							if unloaded or value26.Health <= 0 or not aimSettings.TriggerbotEnabled or not aimSettings.TriggerTricks or aimSettings.TriggerVersion ~= arg or localPlayer.Character ~= value24 or workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts") then
								return false
							end

							if workspace.CurrentCamera ~= currentCamera2 or arg2.Character ~= value27 or not value28.Parent or value29.Health <= 0 then
								return false
							end

							if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() or api.IsProtectedPlayer(arg2, true) or featureState.SilentAim and not api.IsSilentAimTarget(arg2) or (value28.Position - value25.Position).Magnitude > aimSettings.TriggerDist then
								return false
							end
							local num39 = math.min(num36 / num38, 1)
							local num40 = cFrame2.Position + value25.Position - position
							if (value28.Position - num40).Magnitude < 0.001 then
								return false
							end
							local value32 = cFrame2.Rotation:Lerp(CFrame.lookAt(num40, value28.Position).Rotation, num39)
							local num41

							if triggerTrick == "180 Flick" then
								num41 = math.sin(num39 * 3.1415926535897931) * 3.1415926535897931
							else
								num41 = num39 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2 * value30
							end

							currentCamera2.CFrame = CFrame.new(num40) * CFrame.Angles(0, num41, 0) * value32
							local num42 = currentCamera2.CFrame.LookVector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)

							if num42.Magnitude > 0.001 then
								value25.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(value25.Position, value25.Position + num42)
							end
						until num36 >= num38

						return true
					end)

					aimSettings.TriggerSpinning = false

					if value26.Parent then
						value26.AutoRotate = autoRotate
					end

					if not (ok and result) and value25.Parent then
						value25.CFrame = CFrame.new(value25.Position) * rotation
					end

					if currentCamera2.Parent then
						if not (ok and result) then
							currentCamera2.CFrame = cFrame2 + value25.Position - position
						end

						currentCamera2.CameraType = cameraType
					end

					return ok and result
				end

				api.UpdateTriggerbot = function()
					local aimSettings = sharedState.AimSettings
					if aimSettings.TriggerBotDebounce then
						return
					end
					local value24 = api.GetTriggerTarget()
					if not value24 then
						return
					end
					local character = localPlayer.Character
					local triggerVersion = aimSettings.TriggerVersion
					aimSettings.TriggerBotDebounce = true

					task.spawn(function()
						pcall(function()
							task.wait(aimSettings.TriggerDelay)
							if aimSettings.TriggerVersion ~= triggerVersion or localPlayer.Character ~= character or api.GetTriggerTarget() ~= value24 then
								return
							end

							if aimSettings.TriggerTricks then
								if not api.PlayTriggerTrick(triggerVersion, value24) then
									return
								end
								RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
							end

							if aimSettings.TriggerVersion ~= triggerVersion or localPlayer.Character ~= character or api.GetTriggerTarget() ~= value24 then
								return
							end
							local triggerHitchance = aimSettings.TriggerbotHitchance or 100

						if triggerHitchance < 100 and math.random(1, 100) > triggerHitchance then
							task.wait(aimSettings.TriggerCooldown or 0.4)
							return
						end

						api.Click()
						task.wait(0.2)

							while not unloaded and aimSettings.TriggerbotEnabled and aimSettings.TriggerVersion == triggerVersion and localPlayer.Character == character and workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts") do
								task.wait()
							end

							if not unloaded and aimSettings.TriggerVersion == triggerVersion then
								task.wait(aimSettings.TriggerCooldown)
							end
						end)

						aimSettings.TriggerBotDebounce = false
					end)
				end

				api.UpdateAimAssist = function()
					local aimSettings = sharedState.AimSettings
					local grabParts = workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")
					local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
					local vector2 = Vector2.new(viewportSize.X / 2, viewportSize.Y / 2)
					local frame2 = zoomOverlay.Frame

					if aimSettings.FOVEnabled then
						local num36 = aimSettings.FOVRadius * 2
						local udim2 = UDim2.fromOffset(num36, num36)
						local udim22 = UDim2.fromOffset(vector2.X, vector2.Y)

						if not frame2.Visible then
							frame2.Visible = true
						end

						if frame2.Size ~= udim2 then
							frame2.Size = udim2
						end

						if frame2.Position ~= udim22 then
							frame2.Position = udim22
						end
					elseif frame2.Visible then
						frame2.Visible = false
					end

					if grabParts and not aimSettings.AimDebounce then
						local grabPart = grabParts:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
						grabPart = grabPart and grabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")
						grabPart = grabPart and grabPart.Part1

						if grabPart and grabPart.Parent and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(grabPart.Parent) then
							aimSettings.AimDebounce = true

							task.spawn(function()
								while true do
									task.wait()
									if not (unloaded or not grabParts.Parent) then
										continue
									end
									break
								end

								task.wait(0.3)
								aimSettings.AimDebounce = false
							end)
						end

						return
					end

					if aimSettings.AimbotEnabled and not aimSettings.AimDebounce and not aimSettings.TriggerSpinning then
						local alwaysOn = aimSettings.AlwaysOn

						if not alwaysOn then
							local aimbotKeybind = options.AimbotKeybind

							if aimbotKeybind and aimbotKeybind.Toggled then
								alwaysOn = true
							elseif typeof(aimSettings.AimKey) == "EnumItem" then
								if aimSettings.AimKey.EnumType == Enum.KeyCode then
									alwaysOn = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(aimSettings.AimKey)
								elseif aimSettings.AimKey.EnumType == Enum.UserInputType then
									alwaysOn = UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(aimSettings.AimKey)
								end
							end
						end

						local value24 = alwaysOn and api.GetAimTarget()

						if value24 and value24.Character and value24.Character:FindFirstChild(aimSettings.TargetPart) then
							local smoothness = aimSettings.Smoothness
							currentCamera.CFrame = currentCamera.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.new(currentCamera.CFrame.Position, value24.Character[aimSettings.TargetPart].Position), smoothness)
						end
					end

					if aimSettings.TriggerbotEnabled and not grabParts then
						api.UpdateTriggerbot()
					end
				end

				api.UpdateAimConnection = function()
					local aimSettings = sharedState.AimSettings
					local fovEnabled = aimSettings.FOVEnabled or aimSettings.AimbotEnabled or aimSettings.TriggerbotEnabled
					api.SetRuntimeConnection("AimAssist", RunService.RenderStepped, fovEnabled, api.UpdateAimAssist)

					if not fovEnabled and zoomOverlay.Frame.Visible then
						zoomOverlay.Frame.Visible = false
					end
				end

				api.IsSilentAimTarget = function(arg)
					return arg and arg ~= localPlayer and not api.IsProtectedPlayer(arg, featureState.SilentAimIgnoreWhitelist) and (not featureState.SilentAimTargetsOnly or sharedState.SelectedSilentAimPlayers[arg])
				end

				api.ApplySilentAimPlayer = function(arg)
					if arg == localPlayer then
						return
					end
					local value24, value25 = api.Char(arg)
					local silentAimState = sharedState.SilentAimState
					local value26 = silentAimState.Players[arg]
					local state12 = featureState.SilentAim and not silentAimState.RaycastActive and api.IsSilentAimTarget(arg)

					if value26 and (not state12 or value26.Root ~= value25) then
						if value26.Root.Parent then
							value26.Root.Size = value26.Size
							value26.Root.CustomPhysicalProperties = value26.Physics
							value26.Root.CanCollide = value26.CanCollide
						end

						silentAimState.Players[arg] = nil
						value26 = nil
					end

					if not value25 or not state12 then
						return
					end

					if not value26 or value26.Root ~= value25 then
						silentAimState.Players[arg] = {
							Root = value25,
							Size = value25.Size,
							Physics = value25.CustomPhysicalProperties,
							CanCollide = value25.CanCollide,
						}
					end

					local saimHitbox = configValues.SAIMHitbox

					if silentAimState.Size ~= saimHitbox then
						silentAimState.Size = saimHitbox
						silentAimState.HitboxSize = Vector3.new(saimHitbox, saimHitbox, saimHitbox)
						silentAimState.PhysicalProperties = PhysicalProperties.new(2.8 / saimHitbox ^ 3, 0.3, 0.5, 1, 1)
					end

					value25.Size = silentAimState.HitboxSize
					value25.CustomPhysicalProperties = silentAimState.PhysicalProperties
					value25.CanCollide = false
				end

				api.UpdateSilentAimFOV = function()
					local silentAimState = sharedState.SilentAimState
					silentAimState.Circle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(configValues.SAIMRadius * 2, configValues.SAIMRadius * 2)
					silentAimState.Circle.Visible = not unloaded and featureState.SilentAim == true and silentAimState.RaycastActive == true and featureState.SilentAimFOV == true
				end

				api.RefreshSilentAim = function()
					for _, value24 in ipairs(sharedState.PlayerList) do
						api.ApplySilentAimPlayer(value24)
					end

					local state12 = sharedState.SilentAimState.RaycastActive == true
					local silentAimMethod = uiLib.Labels and uiLib.Labels.SilentAimMethod

					if silentAimMethod then
						local value24 = silentAimMethod
						local setText = value24.SetText
						local method

						if state12 then
							method = "Game Raycast"
						else
							method = "Hitbox (Compatibility)"
						end

						setText(value24, "Method: " .. method)
					end

					if options.SilentAimRadius then
						options.SilentAimRadius:SetVisible(state12)
					end

					if options.SilentAimSize then
						options.SilentAimSize:SetVisible(not state12)
					end

					if toggles.SilentAimFOV then
						toggles.SilentAimFOV:SetVisible(state12)
					end

					api.UpdateSilentAimFOV()
				end

				api.GetSilentAimHit = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
					local currentCamera2 = workspace.CurrentCamera
					if not currentCamera2 or type(arg) ~= "number" or arg <= 0 then
						return
					end
					arg3 = arg3 or currentCamera2.CFrame.Position
					local num36 = currentCamera2.ViewportSize / 2
					local num37 = configValues.SAIMRadius ^ 2
					local value24 = nil

					for _, value25 in ipairs(sharedState.PlayerList) do
						if api.IsSilentAimTarget(value25) then
							local value26, value27, value28 = api.Char(value25)

							if value27 and value28 and value28.Health > 0 then
								local value29, value30 = currentCamera2:WorldToViewportPoint(value27.Position)

								if value30 then
									local num38 = value29.X - num36.X
									local num39 = value29.Y - num36.Y
									local num40 = num38 * num38 + num39 * num39
									local num41 = value27.Position - arg3
									local magnitude = num41.Magnitude

									if num40 <= num37 and magnitude > 0.001 and magnitude <= arg + value27.Size.Magnitude / 2 then
										local hit = workspace:Raycast(arg3, num41.Unit * math.min(arg, magnitude + value27.Size.Magnitude / 2), arg2)

										if hit and hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(value26) then
											num37 = num40
											value24 = hit
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					return value24
				end

				api.SetSilentAim = function(arg)
					local silentAimState = sharedState.SilentAimState
					arg = arg and not unloaded

					if arg then
						silentAimState.Failed = nil
					end

					silentAimState.Hit = nil
					silentAimState.HitTime = nil
					api.SetRuntimeToggle("SilentAim", arg)

					api.SetRuntimeConnection("SilentAimStatus", RunService.Heartbeat, not unloaded, function()
						if silentAimState.Dirty then
							silentAimState.Dirty = nil
							api.RefreshSilentAim()
						end
					end)

					if unloaded then
						local patch = silentAimState.Patch

						if patch and getrawmetatable then
							local ok, result = pcall(getrawmetatable, game)

							if ok and result.__namecall == patch.Wrapper then
								pcall(hookmetamethod, game, "__namecall", patch.Original)
							end
						end

						silentAimState.Patch = nil
						silentAimState.Dirty = nil
						silentAimState.Failed = nil
					elseif not silentAimState.Patch and type(hookmetamethod) == "function" and type(getnamecallmethod) == "function" then
						local patch = {}

						local function wrapper(arg2, ...)
							local value24 = table.pack(...)
							if unloaded or not featureState.SilentAim or not silentAimState.RaycastActive or arg2 ~= workspace or getnamecallmethod() ~= "Raycast" or checkcaller and checkcaller() then
								return patch.Original(arg2, ...)
							end
							local value25 = ...
							local value = select(2, ...)
							local value2 = select(3, ...)
							local currentCamera2 = workspace.CurrentCamera
							if not currentCamera2 or typeof(value25) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(value) ~= "Vector3" or value.Magnitude <= 0 or (value25 - currentCamera2.CFrame.Position).Magnitude >= 0.1 or value.Unit:Dot(currentCamera2.CFrame.LookVector) <= 0.999 then
								return patch.Original(arg2, table.unpack(value24, 1, value24.n))
							end
							local ok, result = pcall(getcallingscript)
							if ok and result and result.Name ~= "GrabbingScript" and result.Name ~= "RaycastModule" then
								return patch.Original(arg2, table.unpack(value24, 1, value24.n))
							end
							local ok2, hit = pcall(api.GetSilentAimHit, value.Magnitude, value2, value25)

							if ok2 and hit then
								local value26 = silentAimState
								local value27 = silentAimState
								local now3 = os.clock()
								value26.Hit = hit
								value27.HitTime = now3
								return hit
							end

							if not ok2 then
								silentAimState.Failed = true
								silentAimState.RaycastActive = false
								silentAimState.Dirty = true
							end

							local value26 = workspace.Raycast(arg2, table.unpack(value24, 1, value24.n))
							local value27 = silentAimState
							local value28 = silentAimState
							local now3 = os.clock()
							value27.Hit = value26
							value28.HitTime = now3
							return value26
						end

						if newcclosure then
							wrapper = newcclosure(wrapper)
						end

						patch.Wrapper = wrapper
						local ok, original = pcall(hookmetamethod, game, "__namecall", patch.Wrapper)

						if ok and type(original) == "function" then
							patch.Original = original
							silentAimState.Patch = patch
						end
					end

					silentAimState.RaycastActive = not unloaded and silentAimState.Patch ~= nil and not silentAimState.Failed
					api.RefreshSilentAim()
				end

				api.TrackSilentAimPlayer = function(arg)
					uiLib:GiveSignal(arg.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
						local value24 = unloaded
						local state12

						if unloaded then
							state12 = value24
						else
							state12 = not featureState.SilentAim
						end

						if state12 then
							return
						end

						if not character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5) then
							return
						end

						if not unloaded then
							api.ApplySilentAimPlayer(arg)
						end
					end))
				end

				api.Ragdoll = function(arg)
					if not arg or not arg.Parent or sharedState.States.Ragdolling then
						return
					end
					local head = arg:FindFirstChild("Head")
					local torso = head and arg:FindFirstChild("Torso")
					local humanoid = torso and arg:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
					humanoid = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")

					if humanoid and humanoid.Value == false then
						sharedState.States.Ragdolling = true
						local ragdollRSC = toysFolder:FindFirstChild("RagdollRSC") or api.SpawnToy("PalletLightBrown")
						local soundPart = ragdollRSC and ragdollRSC:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 0.5)
						local main = soundPart and ragdollRSC:WaitForChild("Main", 0.5)
						if not main or not api.SNO(soundPart) then
							sharedState.States.Ragdolling = false
							return api.DestroyToy(ragdollRSC)
						end
						ragdollRSC.Name = "RagdollRSC"

						for _, child in pairs(ragdollRSC:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("BasePart") then
								child.CanCollide = false
							end
						end

						if not main:FindFirstChild("RagdollBP") then
							local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
							bodyPosition.Name = "RagdollBP"
							bodyPosition.D = 1500
							bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
							bodyPosition.Position = Vector3.new(0, 100000, 0)
							bodyPosition.Parent = main
						end

						local now3 = tick()

						while true do
							soundPart.Position = torso.Position
							soundPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, -10, 0)
							task.wait(0.1)
							if not (humanoid.Value or not main.Parent or not arg.Parent or not head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") or tick() - now3 > 0.5) then
								continue
							end
							break
						end

						sharedState.States.Ragdolling = false
						return humanoid.Value
					end
				end

				api.RemoveSticky = function(arg, arg2)
					if not arg or not arg.Parent then
						return
					end

					for _, value24 in pairs(arg:QueryDescendants("BasePart")) do
						if value24.Parent then
							for _, value25 in pairs(value24:GetConnectedParts()) do
								if value25.Name == "StickyPart" then
									for _, child in pairs(value25:GetChildren()) do
										if child:IsA("TouchInterest") then
											child:Destroy()
										end
									end

									local parent2 = value25.Parent and api.FindNicePart(value25.Parent)

									if parent2 then
										if not parent2:IsDescendantOf(workspace.PlotItems) then
											api.Grab(parent2)
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if arg2 then
						return
					end
					local playerFromCharacter = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(arg)
					if not playerFromCharacter then
						return
					end
					local value24 = api.GetInventory(playerFromCharacter)
					if not value24 then
						return
					end
					local humanoidRootPart = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or arg:FindFirstChild("Torso")
					if not humanoidRootPart then
						return
					end
