				api.BreakLimb = function(arg, arg2)
					local state12 = arg == localPlayer
					if state12 and featureState.GucciAntiGrab then
						return notify("Can't break limbs while in Gucci!")
					end

								state13 = true
								break
							end

					end

					if not state13 then
						return
					end

								continue
							end
							break
						end

						return
					end

					if not ragdolled.Value then
						return
					end

					for _, child in ipairs(children) do
						if child:IsA("BasePart") then
							if child.Name:find("Arm") and (arg2 == "Arms" or arg2 == "All") then
								api.Teleport(child, CFrame.new(0, -100000, 0))
							elseif child.Name:find("Leg") and (arg2 == "Legs" or arg2 == "All") then
								api.Teleport(child, CFrame.new(0, -100000, 0))
							end
						end
					end

						return
					end

						api.Grab(value25)
						task.wait()

							if value27.Parent then
								api.ResetVelocity(value27)

								end
							end

								if value28.Parent then
									api.ResetVelocity(value28)
								end
							end

					local num37 = 0

					end

						num38 = 2
					end

							num38 += 2
						end
					end

											if table16 == value27 then
												occupant = seat.Occupant
												playerFromCharacter = occupant and occupant ~= value26 and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(occupant.Parent)

												end

													func23()
												end
											end

											func21(value26)
											continue
										elseif exitTo == 24 then
											if table16 == value27 then
												func23()
											end

											func21(value26)
											continue
										elseif exitTo == 25 then
											if value23 ~= gucciType then

												local text5

													text5 = "Train doesn't have seats!"
												else
													text5 = "Train doesn't exist!"
												end

												value23 = gucciType
											end

											continue
										elseif exitTo == 26 then
											state21 = object and gucciType ~= "Train"

											if state21 then
												sharedState.ToyAuraReserved[object] = nil
												api.DestroyToy(object)
											end

										elseif exitTo == 27 then
											func23()
											continue
										elseif exitTo == 28 then

												func22()
											end

											continue

												task.wait()
											end

											continue

											end

											continue

											state20 = not state19

											end

												func21(value26)
											end

						end

						api.UpdatePCLDAssignments = function()
							local pcldTracker = sharedState.PCLDTracker
							local players = pcldTracker.Players
							local positions = pcldTracker.Positions
							table.clear(players)
							table.clear(positions)
							local state12 = false

							for k in sharedState.PCLDParts, nil, nil do

							end

								else

									if not table18 then
										table18 = { Hits = 0 }
										pcldTracker.PartStates[k] = table18
									end

									if api.IsWaitingPCLD(k) then
										if table18.Player or table18.Candidate or table18.Hits ~= 0 or k:GetAttribute("CurrentlyAssignedTo") ~= nil then
											api.ClearPCLDAssignment(k, table18)
										end
									else
										if featureState.PCLDESP and api.CreatePCLDESP then
											api.CreatePCLDESP(k)
										end

										local position, huge, huge2, value23, num36, value24, value25, value26, state13, pcldesp, updatePCLDESP

										if table18.Player then
											if table18.Player.Parent == Players then
												sharedState.PCLD[table18.Player] = k

												if featureState.PCLDESP and api.UpdatePCLDESP then
													api.UpdatePCLDESP(k)
												end
											else
												api.ClearPCLDAssignment(k, table18)
												position = k.Position
												huge = math.huge
												huge2 = math.huge
												value23 = nil

												for i, player in ipairs(players) do
													num36 = position - positions[i]
													value24 = num36:Dot(num36)

													if value24 < huge then
														huge2 = huge
														value23 = player
														huge = value24
													elseif value24 < huge2 then
														huge2 = value24
													end
												end

												value26 = math.sqrt(huge2)

													else
														table18.Candidate = value23
														table18.Hits = 1
													end

													if table18.Hits >= pcldTracker.Confirmations then
														table18.Player = value23
														table18.Candidate = nil
														table18.Hits = 0
														sharedState.PCLD[value23] = k
														k:SetAttribute("CurrentlyAssignedTo", value23.Name)
														pcldesp = featureState.PCLDESP
														updatePCLDESP = pcldesp and api.UpdatePCLDESP

														if updatePCLDESP then
															api.UpdatePCLDESP(k)
														end
													end
												else
													table18.Candidate = nil
													table18.Hits = 0
												end
											end
										else
											position = k.Position
											huge = math.huge
											huge2 = math.huge
											value23 = nil

								if unloaded or not clone.Parent then

								end

								table18.Waiting = value23

								if value23 then
									table18.Moving = false
									table17[table18] = nil
								else
									if not featureState.PCLDESP then
										api.UpdatePCLDESP(arg)
										return
									end

									if not featureState.SmoothPCLD then
										local cFrame2 = arg.CFrame
										table18.Moving = false
										table17[table18] = nil
										clone.CFrame = cFrame2
										table18.From = cFrame2
										table18.Target = cFrame2
										table18.LastUpdate = tick()
									elseif arg.CFrame ~= table18.Target then
										local now4 = tick()
										local num36 = now4 - table18.LastUpdate
										table18.From = clone.CFrame
										table18.Target = arg.CFrame
										table18.StartedAt = now4
										table18.LastUpdate = now4
										table18.Duration = math.clamp(num36 * 1.05, 0.08, 0.5)
										table18.Moving = true
										table17[table18] = true
										api.SetSmoothPCLDConnection()
									end
								end

								api.UpdatePCLDESP(arg)
							end)

							table18.Connections[2] = arg.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(child, parent2)
								if not parent2 then
									func23(arg, table18)
								end
							end)

							api.UpdatePCLDESP(arg)
						end

						api.SnapPCLDESP = function()
							for k, pclD in pairs(table16.PCLDs) do
								if k.Parent and pclD.Clone.Parent then
									pclD.Moving = false

									if not api.IsWaitingPCLD(k) then
										pclD.Clone.CFrame = k.CFrame
										pclD.From = k.CFrame
										pclD.Target = k.CFrame
										pclD.LastUpdate = tick()
									end

									api.UpdatePCLDESP(k)
								else
									func23(k, pclD)
								end
							end

							table.clear(table17)
							api.SetSmoothPCLDConnection()
						end

						func24 = function(arg, arg2)
							func21(table16.Stickies, arg, arg2)
						end

						do
							local function func25(arg, adornee)
								if not adornee:IsA("BasePart") or adornee.Transparency >= 1 or adornee.LocalTransparencyModifier >= 1 or arg.Parts[adornee] then
									return
								end
								local boxHandleAdornment = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
								local table18 = {}
								local num36 = adornee.Size * 0.5

								local function func26(arg2, arg3)
									local lineHandleAdornment = Instance.new("LineHandleAdornment")
									local value23 = adornee
									local cframe = CFrame.lookAt(arg2, arg3)
									local magnitude = (arg3 - arg2).Magnitude
									lineHandleAdornment.Adornee = value23
									lineHandleAdornment.CFrame = cframe
									lineHandleAdornment.Length = magnitude
									local value24 = screenGui
									lineHandleAdornment.Thickness = 2
									lineHandleAdornment.AlwaysOnTop = true
									lineHandleAdornment.ZIndex = 2
									lineHandleAdornment.Parent = value24
									table.insert(table18, lineHandleAdornment)
									table.insert(arg.Instances, lineHandleAdornment)
								end

								for i = -1, 1, 2 do
									for i2 = -1, 1, 2 do
										local vector = Vector3.new
										local x = num36.X
										local num37 = i * num36.Y
										local num38 = i2 * num36.Z
										func26(Vector3.new(-num36.X, i * num36.Y, i2 * num36.Z), vector(x, num37, num38))
										local vector2 = Vector3.new
										local num39 = i * num36.X
										local y = num36.Y
										local num40 = i2 * num36.Z
										func26(Vector3.new(i * num36.X, -num36.Y, i2 * num36.Z), vector2(num39, y, num40))
										local vector3 = Vector3.new
										local num41 = i * num36.X
										local num42 = i2 * num36.Y
										local z = num36.Z
										func26(Vector3.new(i * num36.X, i2 * num36.Y, -num36.Z), vector3(num41, num42, z))
									end
								end

								local size = adornee.Size
								boxHandleAdornment.Adornee = adornee
								boxHandleAdornment.Size = size
								boxHandleAdornment.AlwaysOnTop = true
								boxHandleAdornment.ZIndex = 1
								boxHandleAdornment.Parent = screenGui
								arg.Parts[adornee] = { table18, boxHandleAdornment }
								table.insert(arg.Instances, boxHandleAdornment)
							end

							api.UpdateStickyESP = function(arg)
								local value23 = table16.Stickies[arg]
								local sticky = espConfig.Sticky
								if not value23 then
									return
								end

								if not (arg.Parent and value23.StickyWeld.Parent) then
									return func24(arg, value23)
								end
								local part1 = value23.StickyWeld.Part1
								local character = localPlayer.Character
								local state12 = featureState.StickyESP and part1 ~= nil

								if state12 then
									state12 = not (character and part1:IsDescendantOf(character))
								end

								for k, part4 in pairs(value23.Parts) do
									if not k.Parent then
										value23.Parts[k] = nil
									else
										local visible = state12 and k.Transparency < 1 and k.LocalTransparencyModifier < 1
										local value24 = part4[1]
										local value25 = part4[2]
										local visible2 = visible and sticky.OutlineTransparency < 1
										visible = visible and sticky.FillTransparency < 1

										for _, value26 in ipairs(value24) do
											if value26.Visible ~= visible2 then
												value26.Visible = visible2
											end

											if value26.Color3 ~= sticky.OutlineColor then
												value26.Color3 = sticky.OutlineColor
											end

											if value26.Transparency ~= sticky.OutlineTransparency then
												value26.Transparency = sticky.OutlineTransparency
											end

											if value26.Thickness ~= sticky.OutlineThickness then
												value26.Thickness = sticky.OutlineThickness
											end
										end

										if value25.Visible ~= visible then
											value25.Visible = visible
										end

										if value25.Color3 ~= sticky.FillColor then
											value25.Color3 = sticky.FillColor
										end

										if value25.Transparency ~= sticky.FillTransparency then
											value25.Transparency = sticky.FillTransparency
										end
									end
								end
							end

							api.CreateStickyESP = function(arg)
								if table16.Stickies[arg] then
									return
								end
								local stickyPart = arg:WaitForChild("StickyPart", 0.15)
								stickyPart = stickyPart and stickyPart:WaitForChild("StickyWeld", 0.15)
								if not stickyPart or unloaded or not featureState.StickyESP or not arg.Parent or table16.Stickies[arg] then
									return
								end
								local table18 = { StickyWeld = stickyPart, Parts = {}, Connections = {}, Instances = {} }
								table16.Stickies[arg] = table18

								for _, value23 in arg:QueryDescendants("BasePart") do
									func25(table18, value23)
								end

								table18.Connections[1] = stickyPart:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Part1"):Connect(function()
									api.UpdateStickyESP(arg)
								end)

								table18.Connections[2] = arg.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(child, parent2)
									if not parent2 then
										func24(arg, table18)
									end
								end)

								table18.Connections[3] = stickyPart.Destroying:Connect(function()
									func24(arg, table18)
								end)

								table18.Connections[4] = arg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
									func25(table18, descendant)
									api.UpdateStickyESP(arg)
								end)

								api.UpdateStickyESP(arg)
							end
						end
					end

					do
						local function func25(arg, arg2)
							func21(table16.BlackHoles, arg, arg2)
						end

						api.UpdateBlackHoleESP = function(arg)
							local value23 = table16.BlackHoles[arg]
							local blackhole = espConfig.Blackhole
							if not value23 then
								return
							end

							if not (arg.Parent and value23.Hole.Parent and value23.Highlight.Parent and value23.Billboard.Parent) then
								return func25(arg, value23)
							end
							value23.Highlight.FillColor = blackhole.FillColor
							value23.Highlight.OutlineColor = blackhole.OutlineColor
							value23.Highlight.FillTransparency = blackhole.FillTransparency
							value23.Highlight.OutlineTransparency = blackhole.OutlineTransparency
							value23.Highlight.Enabled = featureState.BlackholeESP
							value23.Billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, blackhole.NameYOffset, 0)
							value23.Billboard.Enabled = featureState.BlackholeESP and blackhole.NameEnabled
							value23.Label.TextSize = blackhole.NameSize
							value23.Label.TextColor3 = blackhole.NameColor
							value23.Label.TextTransparency = blackhole.NameTransparency
							value23.Label.TextStrokeColor3 = blackhole.NameOutlineColor
							value23.Label.TextStrokeTransparency = blackhole.NameOutlineTransparency
							value23.Label.FontFace = api.GetFont(blackhole.NameFont)
						end

						api.CreateBlackHoleESP = function(arg, arg2)
							if unloaded or not arg then
								return
							end

							if table16.BlackHoles[arg] then
								return table16.BlackHoles[arg]
							end
							local hole = arg:WaitForChild("Hole", 1.5)
							if not hole then
								return
							end
							local clone = highlight:Clone()
							clone.Name = "Blackhole"
							clone.Adornee = hole
							clone.Parent = hole
							local billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
							billboardGui.Name = "BlackholeName"
							billboardGui.Enabled = false
							billboardGui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(220, 60)
							billboardGui.SizeOffset = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
							billboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
							billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
							billboardGui.Adornee = hole
							billboardGui.Parent = screenGui
							local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
							textLabel.Name = "Label"
							textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
							textLabel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
							textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
							textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
							textLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
							textLabel.Text = "Kicked: " .. api.FormatPlayer(arg2, true)
							textLabel.Parent = billboardGui

							local table18 = {
								Hole = hole,
								Player = arg2,
								Highlight = clone,
								Billboard = billboardGui,
								Label = textLabel,
								Connections = {},
								Instances = { billboardGui, clone },
							}

							table16.BlackHoles[arg] = table18

							table18.Connections[1] = arg.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(child, parent2)
								if not parent2 then
									func25(arg, table18)
								end
							end)

							api.UpdateBlackHoleESP(arg)
							return table18
						end

						api.RefreshESP = function(arg)
							task.spawn(function()
								if arg == "Player" then
									for _, value23 in ipairs(sharedState.PlayerList) do
										if value23 ~= localPlayer then
											api.CreatePlayerESP(value23)
										end
									end
								elseif arg == "Server" then
									for k in pairs(sharedState.PCLDParts) do
										if not k.Parent or k.Name ~= "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" then
											sharedState.PCLDParts[k] = nil
										elseif not api.IsWaitingPCLD(k) then
											api.CreatePCLDESP(k)
										end
									end
								elseif arg == "Sticky" then
									for _, value23 in ipairs(workspace:QueryDescendants("BasePart#StickyPart")) do
										if value23.Parent then
											api.CreateStickyESP(value23.Parent)
										end
									end
								end

								api.UpdateESP(arg)
							end)
						end

						api.UpdateESP = function(arg)
							local value23 = espConfig[arg]
							if not value23 then
								return
							end

							for k in pairs(value23.Objects) do
								if arg == "Player" then
									api.UpdatePlayerESP(k)
								elseif arg == "Server" then
									api.UpdatePCLDESP(k)
								elseif arg == "Sticky" then
									api.UpdateStickyESP(k)
								elseif arg == "Blackhole" then
									api.UpdateBlackHoleESP(k)
								end
							end
						end

						api.UpdateSmoothPCLD = function()
							local now3 = tick()

							for k in pairs(table17) do
								if k.Moving and k.Part.Parent and k.Clone.Parent then
									local num36 = math.clamp((now3 - k.StartedAt) / k.Duration, 0, 1)
									k.Clone.CFrame = k.From:Lerp(k.Target, num36)

									if num36 >= 1 then
										k.Moving = false
										table17[k] = nil
									end
								else
									table17[k] = nil
								end
							end

							if not next(table17) then
								api.SetSmoothPCLDConnection()
							end
						end

						api.SetSmoothPCLDConnection = function()
							if not featureState.PCLDESP or not featureState.SmoothPCLD then
								for k in pairs(table17) do
									k.Moving = false
								end

								table.clear(table17)
							end

							api.SetRuntimeConnection("SmoothPCLD", RunService.RenderStepped, featureState.PCLDESP and featureState.SmoothPCLD and next(table17) ~= nil, api.UpdateSmoothPCLD)
						end

						api.CleanupESP = function()
							table.clear(table17)
							api.SetSmoothPCLDConnection()

							for k in pairs(weakCache) do
								api.RemovePlayerESP(k)
							end

							for k, player in pairs(table16.Players) do
								func22(k, player)
							end

							for k, pclD in pairs(table16.PCLDs) do
								func23(k, pclD)
							end

							for k, sticky in pairs(table16.Stickies) do
								func24(k, sticky)
							end

							for k, blackHole in pairs(table16.BlackHoles) do
								func25(k, blackHole)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			do
				api.UpdateCustomCrosshair = function()
					local customCrosshair = sharedState.CustomCrosshair
					local state12 = featureState.CustomCrosshair and type(configValues.CustomCrosshairImage) == "string" and configValues.CustomCrosshairImage ~= ""

					api.SetRuntimeConnection("CustomCrosshairAdded", playerGui.ChildAdded, state12, function(arg)
						if arg:IsA("ScreenGui") and arg.Name == "Crosshairs" then
							api.UpdateCustomCrosshair()
						end
					end)

					if not state12 then
						api.SetRuntimeConnection("CustomCrosshairEnabled", playerGui.ChildAdded, false)
						customCrosshair.GameScreen = nil

						if customCrosshair.Gui then
							customCrosshair.Gui:Destroy()
							customCrosshair.Gui = nil
							customCrosshair.Image = nil
						end

						for k, value23 in customCrosshair.Hidden, nil, nil do
							if k.Parent then
								k.Enabled = value23
							end

							customCrosshair.Hidden[k] = nil
						end

						return
					end

					if not (customCrosshair.Gui and customCrosshair.Gui.Parent) then
						local screenGui2 = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
						local imageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
						screenGui2.Name = "RSCCustomCrosshair"
						screenGui2.IgnoreGuiInset = true
						screenGui2.ResetOnSpawn = false
						screenGui2.DisplayOrder = 100
						local udim2 = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
						imageLabel.Name = "Crosshair"
						imageLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
						imageLabel.Position = udim2
						local fit = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
						imageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
						imageLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
						imageLabel.ScaleType = fit
						imageLabel.Parent = screenGui2
						screenGui2.Parent = playerGui
						customCrosshair.Gui = screenGui2
						customCrosshair.Image = imageLabel
					end

					customCrosshair.Image.Image = configValues.CustomCrosshairImage
					customCrosshair.Image.ImageColor3 = configValues.CustomCrosshairColor
					customCrosshair.Image.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(configValues.CustomCrosshairSize, configValues.CustomCrosshairSize)
					local crosshairs = playerGui:FindFirstChild("Crosshairs")

					if crosshairs and crosshairs:IsA("ScreenGui") then
						if customCrosshair.Hidden[crosshairs] == nil then
							customCrosshair.Hidden[crosshairs] = crosshairs.Enabled
						end

						crosshairs.Enabled = false

						if customCrosshair.GameScreen ~= crosshairs then
							api.SetRuntimeConnection("CustomCrosshairEnabled", crosshairs:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"), false)
							customCrosshair.GameScreen = crosshairs

							api.SetRuntimeConnection("CustomCrosshairEnabled", crosshairs:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"), true, function()
								if featureState.CustomCrosshair and crosshairs.Enabled then
									crosshairs.Enabled = false
								end
							end)
						end
					elseif customCrosshair.GameScreen then
						api.SetRuntimeConnection("CustomCrosshairEnabled", playerGui.ChildAdded, false)
						customCrosshair.GameScreen = nil
					end
				end

				api.SetCustomCrosshair = function(customCrosshair)
					featureState.CustomCrosshair = customCrosshair
					api.UpdateCustomCrosshair()
				end

				local table16 = {}

				local function func21(arg, arg2)
					sharedState.CharacterHighlightParts[arg] = nil
					arg2.MaterialConnection:Disconnect()
					arg2.ColorConnection:Disconnect()

					if arg.Parent then
						local color = arg2.Color
						local physical = arg2.Physical
						arg.Material = arg2.Material
						arg.Color = color
						arg.CustomPhysicalProperties = physical
					end
				end

				api.ApplyCharacterHighlightPart = function(arg, affect)
					if not (arg and arg:IsA("BasePart")) then
						return
					end
					local table17 = sharedState.CharacterHighlightParts[arg]

					if arg.Parent and not unloaded and featureState.ForcefieldChar and sharedState.CharacterHighlightAffects[affect] then
						if not table17 then
							table17 = {
								Material = arg.Material,
								Color = arg.Color,
								Affect = affect,
								Physical = arg.CustomPhysicalProperties,
								CurrentPhysical = arg.CurrentPhysicalProperties,
							}

							sharedState.CharacterHighlightParts[arg] = table17

							table17.MaterialConnection = arg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Material"):Connect(function()
								if arg.Material ~= Enum.Material.ForceField and sharedState.CharacterHighlightParts[arg] == table17 and not unloaded and featureState.ForcefieldChar and sharedState.CharacterHighlightAffects[table17.Affect] then
									arg.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
								end
							end)

							table17.ColorConnection = arg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Color"):Connect(function()
								if arg.Color ~= configValues.ForcefieldColor and sharedState.CharacterHighlightParts[arg] == table17 and not unloaded and featureState.ForcefieldChar and sharedState.CharacterHighlightAffects[table17.Affect] then
									arg.Color = configValues.ForcefieldColor
								end
							end)
						end

						table17.Affect = affect

						if affect == "Character" and arg.CustomPhysicalProperties ~= table17.CurrentPhysical then
							arg.CustomPhysicalProperties = table17.CurrentPhysical
						end

						if arg.Material ~= Enum.Material.ForceField then
							arg.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
						end

						if arg.Color ~= configValues.ForcefieldColor then
							arg.Color = configValues.ForcefieldColor
						end
					elseif table17 then
						func21(arg, table17)
					end
				end

				api.UpdateCharacterHighlightAffects = function()
					for k, characterHighlightPart in pairs(sharedState.CharacterHighlightParts) do
						if not k.Parent or unloaded or not featureState.ForcefieldChar or not sharedState.CharacterHighlightAffects[characterHighlightPart.Affect] then
							func21(k, characterHighlightPart)
						end
					end

					if not unloaded and featureState.ForcefieldChar and sharedState.CharacterHighlightAffects["Coconut Forms"] then
						for _, value23 in sharedState.CoconutForms.Models, nil, nil do
							for _, value24 in value23:QueryDescendants("BasePart") do
								api.ApplyCharacterHighlightPart(value24, "Coconut Forms")
							end
						end
					end

					if not unloaded and featureState.ForcefieldChar and sharedState.CharacterHighlightAffects["Anti Kick"] and value18 then
						for _, value23 in value18:GetChildren() do
							api.ApplyCharacterHighlightPart(value23, "Anti Kick")
						end
					end

					api.UpdateChinaHat()
				end

				api.ClearChinaHat = function()
					if table16.Hat then
						table16.Hat:Destroy()
						table16.Hat = nil
					end
				end

				api.UpdateChinaHat = function()
					local hat = table16.Hat
					if not (hat and hat.Parent) then
						return
					end
					local chinaHat = featureState.ForcefieldChar and sharedState.CharacterHighlightAffects["China Hat"]
					local forceField

					if chinaHat then
						forceField = Enum.Material.ForceField
					else
						forceField = Enum.Material.Neon
					end

					local forcefieldColor

					if chinaHat then
						forcefieldColor = configValues.ForcefieldColor
					else
						forcefieldColor = configValues.ChinaHatColor
					end

					local transparency

					if chinaHat then
						transparency = 0
					else
						transparency = 0.75
					end

					if hat.Material ~= forceField then
						hat.Material = forceField
					end

					if hat.Color ~= forcefieldColor then
						hat.Color = forcefieldColor
					end

					if hat.Transparency ~= transparency then
						hat.Transparency = transparency
					end
				end

				api.CreateChinaHat = function(arg)
					local head = arg and arg:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
					if unloaded or not featureState.ChinaHat or arg ~= localPlayer.Character or not head then
						return
					end
					api.ClearChinaHat()
					local meshPart = Instance.new("MeshPart")
					table16.Hat = meshPart
					meshPart.Name = "RSCChinaHat"
					meshPart.MeshId = "rbxassetid://1778999"
					meshPart.Size = Vector3.new(3, 0.7, 3)
					meshPart.Transparency = 0.75
					meshPart.CanCollide = false
					meshPart.CanTouch = false
					meshPart.CanQuery = false
					meshPart.Massless = true
					meshPart.CastShadow = false
					meshPart.DoubleSided = true
					local value23 = currentCamera
					meshPart.CFrame = head.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0)
					meshPart.Parent = value23
					local weldConstraint = Instance.new("WeldConstraint", meshPart)
					weldConstraint.Part0 = meshPart
					weldConstraint.Part1 = head
					api.UpdateChinaHat()
				end

				api.SetChinaHat = function(chinaHat)
					featureState.ChinaHat = chinaHat

					api.SetRuntimeConnection("ChinaHatRespawn", localPlayer.CharacterAdded, chinaHat, function(arg)
						task.defer(api.CreateChinaHat, arg)
					end)

					api.SetRuntimeConnection("ChinaHatVisibility", RunService.RenderStepped, chinaHat, function()
						if table16.Hat then
							local hat = table16.Hat
							local localTransparencyModifier

							if (currentCamera.CFrame.Position - currentCamera.Focus.Position).Magnitude <= 0.6 then
								localTransparencyModifier = 1
							else
								localTransparencyModifier = 0
							end

							hat.LocalTransparencyModifier = localTransparencyModifier
						end
					end)

					if chinaHat then
						task.defer(api.CreateChinaHat, localPlayer.Character)
					else
						api.ClearChinaHat()
					end
				end

				local function func22()
					if table16.Connection then
						table16.Connection:Disconnect()
						table16.Connection = nil
					end

					table16.Character = nil

					for k, characterHighlightPart in pairs(sharedState.CharacterHighlightParts) do
						if characterHighlightPart.Affect == "Character" then
							func21(k, characterHighlightPart)
						end
					end
				end

				local function func23(descendant)
					if not table16.Character or not descendant:IsDescendantOf(table16.Character) then
						return
					end
					api.ApplyCharacterHighlightPart(descendant, "Character")
				end

				local function func24(character)
					func22()

							while true do
								if not unloaded and featureState.CoconutForms and generation == coconutForms.Generation and #coconutForms.Models < #coconutForms.Offsets then
									local FoodCoconut = api.SpawnToy("FoodCoconut", nil, 1, true)
									local soundPart = FoodCoconut and FoodCoconut:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 0.5)
									local holdPart = FoodCoconut and FoodCoconut:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 0.5)
									holdPart = holdPart and holdPart:WaitForChild("RigidConstraint", 0.5)

									if not (soundPart and holdPart and api.BreakHoldable(FoodCoconut) and api.SNO(soundPart, 0.4)) then
										api.DestroyToy(FoodCoconut)
										break
									else
										for _, value22 in FoodCoconut:QueryDescendants("BasePart") do
											value22.CanCollide = false
											value22.CanQuery = false
											value22.CanTouch = false
											value22.LocalTransparencyModifier = coconutForms.Transparency

											if api.ApplyCharacterHighlightPart then
												api.ApplyCharacterHighlightPart(value22, "Coconut Forms")
											end
										end

										if featureState.CoconutForms and generation == coconutForms.Generation and #coconutForms.Models < #coconutForms.Offsets then
											coconutForms.Parts[FoodCoconut] = { Root = soundPart, Rigid = holdPart }
											coconutForms.Models[#coconutForms.Models + 1] = FoodCoconut
										else
											api.DestroyToy(FoodCoconut)
										end

										continue
									end
								end

								break
							end

							coconutForms.Spawning = false

							if featureState.CoconutForms and #coconutForms.Models < #coconutForms.Offsets then
								task.delay(0.5, api.SpawnCoconutForms)
							end
						end)
					end

					api.UpdateCoconutForms = function()
						local coconutForms = sharedState.CoconutForms
						local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
						if not (value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0) then
							return
						end
						local flight = featureState.Flight
						local now3 = os.clock()
						local num37 = math.min(now3 - (coconutForms.WingClock or now3), 0.1)
						coconutForms.WingClock = now3
						local wingSpeed = coconutForms.WingSpeed
						local num38

						if flight then
							num38 = 7.5
						else
							num38 = 1.4
						end

						coconutForms.WingSpeed = wingSpeed + (num38 - coconutForms.WingSpeed) * math.min(num37 * 5, 1)
						coconutForms.WingPhase = coconutForms.WingPhase + num37 * coconutForms.WingSpeed
						local wingPhase = coconutForms.WingStart and coconutForms.WingPhase
						local num39

						if not wingPhase then
							num39 = nil
						elseif flight then
							num39 = -0.08 + math.sin(wingPhase) * 0.52 + math.sin(wingPhase * 2 - 0.6) * 0.07
						else
							num39 = -0.3 + math.sin(wingPhase) * 0.06
						end

						local cFrame2 = (value22:FindFirstChild("Torso") or value23).CFrame
						local num40 = nil
						local num41 = nil

						if wingPhase then
							local num42 = cFrame2 * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0.55)
							local cframe = CFrame.Angles
							local num43

							if flight then
								num43 = -0.12
							else
								num43 = 0
							end

							num40 = num42 * cframe(num43, 0, -num39)
							local cframe2 = CFrame.Angles
							local num44

							if flight then
								num44 = -0.12
							else
								num44 = 0
							end

							num41 = num42 * cframe2(num44, 0, num39)

							for i = 1, 6 do
								local wingRipple = coconutForms.WingRipple
								local sin = math.sin
								local num45 = i - 1
								local num46

								if flight then
									num46 = 0.22
								else
									num46 = 0.35
								end

								local value25 = sin(wingPhase - num45 * num46)
								local num47

								if flight then
									num47 = 0.18
								else
									num47 = 0.035
								end

								wingRipple[i] = value25 * num47
							end
						end

						local num42 = 0
						local vector3 = Vector3.zero

						if coconutForms.CapeStart then
							local value25 = api.GetCustomVelocity(value23)

							if not flight and value24.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.01 then
								local num43 = value24.MoveDirection * value24.WalkSpeed
								value25 = value25:Lerp(Vector3.new(num43.X, value25.Y, num43.Z), 0.45)
							end

							local value26 = cFrame2:VectorToObjectSpace(value25)
							local magnitude = Vector3.new(value26.X, 0, value26.Z).Magnitude
							local num43 = math.sin(now3 * 0.72) + math.sin(now3 * 1.31 + 0.8) * 0.45
							num42 = value26.Magnitude
							local vector4 = Vector3.new(num43 * 0.18, 0.08 + math.sin(now3 * 0.58 + 1.4) * 0.06, 0.38 + num43 * 0.14)
							local vector5 = Vector3.new
							local num44 = math.clamp(-value26.X * 0.05, -2.25, 2.25)
							local clamp = math.clamp
							local num45

							if flight then
								num45 = 0.045
							else
								num45 = 0.018
							end

							local num46 = magnitude * num45 - value26.Y * 0.025
							local num47

							if flight then
								num47 = 0.55
							else
								num47 = 0
							end

							local num48 = num46 + num47
							local num49

							if flight then
								num49 = 2.8
							else
								num49 = 1.3
							end

							local value27 = clamp(num48, -0.9, num49)
							local clamp2 = math.clamp
							local num50 = -value26.Z * 0.06 + magnitude * 0.015
							local num51

							if flight then
								num51 = 0.8
							else
								num51 = 0
							end

							local num52 = num50 + num51
							local num53

							if flight then
								num53 = 5
							else
								num53 = 3.5
							end

							vector3 = vector4 + vector5(num44, value27, clamp2(num52, -0.15, num53))
						end

						coconutForms.CapeSpeed = coconutForms.CapeSpeed + (num42 - coconutForms.CapeSpeed) * math.min(num37 * 8, 1)

						if coconutForms.CapeStart then
							coconutForms.CapePhase = coconutForms.CapePhase + num37 * (2.2 + math.min(coconutForms.CapeSpeed * 0.055, 5))
						end

						local capeWind = coconutForms.CapeWind
						local lerp = capeWind.Lerp
						local min = math.min
						local num43

						if flight then
							num43 = 12
						else
							num43 = 10
						end

						coconutForms.CapeWind = lerp(capeWind, vector3, min(num37 * num43, 1))
						local num44 = coconutForms.Counts.Cape / 3
						local num45 = 0.09 + math.min(coconutForms.CapeSpeed * 0.004, 0.28)
						local num46

						if flight then
							num46 = 0.1
						else
							num46 = 0
						end

						local num47 = num45 + num46
						local num48 = now3 * 1.17

						for i = #coconutForms.Models, 1, -1 do
							local value25 = coconutForms.Models[i]
							local rigid = coconutForms.Parts[value25]
							local root = rigid and rigid.Root
							rigid = rigid and rigid.Rigid

							if not (value25.Parent and root and root.Parent and rigid and rigid.Parent) or rigid.Attachment1 then
								if value25 and value25.Parent then
									api.DestroyToy(value25)
								end

								coconutForms.Parts[value25] = nil
								table.remove(coconutForms.Models, i)
							elseif api.IsPartOwner(root) then
								local num49 = coconutForms.Offsets[i]
								local value26

								if wingPhase and i >= coconutForms.WingStart and i < coconutForms.WingStart + 12 then
									local num50 = i - coconutForms.WingStart
									local num51

									if num50 < 6 then
										num51 = -1
									else
										num51 = 1
									end

									local num52 = num50 % 6 + 1
									local num53 = (num52 - 1) * coconutForms.WingSpacing
									local value27 = coconutForms.WingRipple[num52]

									if num51 ~= -1 then
										value26 = num41
									else
										value26 = num40
									end

									local cframe = CFrame.new
									local num54 = num51 * (0.55 + num53)
									local num55 = num53 / 1.1
									local num56

									if flight then
										num56 = 0.1
									else
										num56 = 0.16
									end

									local value28 = cframe(num54, 0.05 - num55 * num56 + value27, num53 / 1.1 * 0.06 + value27 * 0.35)
									local cframe2 = CFrame.Angles
									local num57 = value27 * 0.6
									local num58

									if flight then
										num58 = -0.1
									else
										num58 = -0.18
									end

									num49 = value28 * cframe2(num57, num51 * num58, num51 * (value27 * 0.8 - 0.1))
								elseif coconutForms.CapeStart and i >= coconutForms.CapeStart + num44 and i < coconutForms.CapeStart + coconutForms.Counts.Cape then
									local num50 = i - coconutForms.CapeStart
									local num51 = num50 % num44 - (num44 - 1) / 2
									local num52 = math.floor(num50 / num44)
									local num53 = num52 / 2
									local num54 = coconutForms.CapeWind * num53
									local num55 = (math.sin(coconutForms.CapePhase - num52 * 0.9 + num51 * 0.48) + math.sin(num48 + num52 * 0.55 - num51 * 0.75) * 0.45) * num47 * num53
									local clamp = math.clamp
									local num56 = -num54.X * 0.14 + num51 * num55 * 0.06
									num49 = CFrame.new(num51 * (1.3 + num52 * 0.12) + num54.X, 0.55 - num52 * 1.65 + num54.Y + num55 * 0.28, 0.55 + num53 * 0.65 + num54.Z + num55 + math.abs(num51) * 0.08 * num53) * CFrame.Angles(0.1 + num53 * 0.18 + math.clamp(-num54.Y * 0.16 + num55 * 0.3, -0.65, 0.65), math.clamp(num51 * num55 * 0.08, -0.18, 0.18), clamp(num56, -0.5, 0.5))
									value26 = cFrame2
								else
									value26 = cFrame2
								end

								local cFrame3 = value26 * num49

								if root.CFrame ~= cFrame3 then
									root.CFrame = cFrame3
								end

								api.ResetVelocity(root)
							elseif not coconutForms.Acquiring[root] then
								coconutForms.Acquiring[root] = true

								task.spawn(function()
									api.SNO(root, 0.25)
									coconutForms.Acquiring[root] = nil
								end)
							end
						end

						if #coconutForms.Models < #coconutForms.Offsets then
							api.SpawnCoconutForms()
						end
					end

					api.RefreshCoconutForms = function()
						local coconutForms = sharedState.CoconutForms
						local selected = sharedState.CoconutForms.Selected
						local offsets = {}
						local counts = coconutForms.Counts
						local cape

						if coconutForms.CapeMode == "2x3" then
							cape = 6
						else
							cape = 12
						end

						counts.Cape = cape
						coconutForms.WingStart = nil
						coconutForms.CapeStart = nil

						for _, value22 in coconutForms.Order, nil, nil do
							if selected[value22] and #offsets + coconutForms.Counts[value22] <= coconutForms.Limit then
								if value22 == "Boobs" then
									local num37 = #offsets + 1
									local num38 = #offsets + 2
									local cframe = CFrame.new(-0.6, 0.5, -0.9)
									local cframe2 = CFrame.new(0.6, 0.5, -0.9)
									offsets[num37] = cframe
									offsets[num38] = cframe2
								elseif value22 == "Ass" then
									local num37 = #offsets + 1
									local num38 = #offsets + 2
									local cframe = CFrame.new(-0.55, -1, 0.75)
									local cframe2 = CFrame.new(0.55, -1, 0.75)
									offsets[num37] = cframe
									offsets[num38] = cframe2
								elseif value22 == "Penis" then
									local num37 = #offsets + 1
									local num38 = #offsets + 2
									local cframe = CFrame.new(-0.55, -1.2, -0.8)
									local cframe2 = CFrame.new(0.55, -1.2, -0.8)
									offsets[num37] = cframe
									offsets[num38] = cframe2

									for i = 1, coconutForms.ShaftLength do
										offsets[#offsets + 1] = CFrame.new(0, -1.2, -0.8 - i * coconutForms.ShaftSpacing)
									end
								elseif value22 == "Wings" then
									coconutForms.WingStart = #offsets + 1

									for i = 1, 12 do
										offsets[#offsets + 1] = CFrame.identity
									end
								elseif value22 == "Cape" then
									coconutForms.CapeStart = #offsets + 1
									local num37 = coconutForms.Counts.Cape / 3

									for i = 0, coconutForms.Counts.Cape - 1 do
										local num38 = i % num37 - (num37 - 1) / 2
										local num39 = #offsets + 1
										local num40

										if i < num37 then
											num40 = CFrame.new(num38 * 1.3, 0.55, 0.55) * CFrame.Angles(0.1, 0, 0)
										else
											num40 = CFrame.identity
										end

										offsets[num39] = num40
									end
								elseif value22 == "67" then
									local num37

									if coconutForms.Display67 == "Show to Front" then
										num37 = -1
									else
										num37 = 1
									end

									for k, value23 in coconutForms.Points67, nil, nil do
										local num38

										if k <= 7 then
											num38 = -1
										else
											num38 = 1
										end

										offsets[#offsets + 1] = CFrame.new((value23.X + num38 * coconutForms.Spacing67 * 0.5) * num37, value23.Y, 0.75)
									end
								end
							end
						end

						if not coconutForms.CapeStart then
							coconutForms.CapeSpeed = 0
							coconutForms.CapeWind = Vector3.zero
						end

						local generation = coconutForms.Generation + 1
						coconutForms.Offsets = offsets
						coconutForms.Generation = generation
						local table17 = {}

						for _, value22 in coconutForms.Names, nil, nil do
							if not selected[value22] and #offsets + coconutForms.Counts[value22] > coconutForms.Limit then
								table17[#table17 + 1] = value22
							end
						end

						if options.CoconutFormsDropdown then
							options.CoconutFormsDropdown:SetDisabledValues(table17)
						end

						if uiLib.Labels.CoconutFormsLimit then
							local coconutFormsLimit = uiLib.Labels.CoconutFormsLimit
							local setText = coconutFormsLimit.SetText
							local text5 = ("%*/%* coconuts used"):format(#offsets, coconutForms.Limit)
							setText(coconutFormsLimit, text5)
						end

						for i = #coconutForms.Models, #offsets + 1, -1 do
							local value22 = table.remove(coconutForms.Models, i)
							coconutForms.Parts[value22] = nil
							api.DestroyToy(value22)
						end

						api.SetRuntimeConnection("CoconutForms", RunService.Heartbeat, featureState.CoconutForms and #offsets > 0, api.UpdateCoconutForms)
						api.SpawnCoconutForms()
					end

					api.SetCoconutTransparency = function(transparency)
						local coconutForms = sharedState.CoconutForms
						coconutForms.Transparency = transparency

						for _, value22 in coconutForms.Models, nil, nil do
							for _, value23 in value22:QueryDescendants("BasePart") do
								value23.LocalTransparencyModifier = transparency
							end
						end
					end

					api.SetCoconutShaftLength = function(arg)
						local coconutForms = sharedState.CoconutForms
						local num37 = 11

						if coconutForms.Selected.Penis then
							local num38 = 0

							for k, value22 in coconutForms.Selected, nil, nil do
								value22 = value22 and k ~= "Penis"

								if value22 then
									num38 += coconutForms.Counts[k]
								end
							end

							num37 = math.max(1, coconutForms.Limit - num38 - 2)
						end

						local shaftLength = math.min(arg, num37)
						local counts = coconutForms.Counts
						coconutForms.ShaftLength = shaftLength
						counts.Penis = shaftLength + 2

						if shaftLength ~= arg and options.CoconutShaftLength then
							task.defer(function()
								options.CoconutShaftLength:SetValue(shaftLength)
							end)
						end

						api.RefreshCoconutForms()
					end

					api.ClearCoconutForms = function()
						local coconutForms = sharedState.CoconutForms
						featureState.CoconutForms = false
						coconutForms.Generation = coconutForms.Generation + 1
						api.SetRuntimeConnection("CoconutForms", RunService.Heartbeat, false)

						for _, value22 in coconutForms.Models, nil, nil do
							api.DestroyToy(value22)
						end

						table.clear(coconutForms.Models)
						table.clear(coconutForms.Parts)
						table.clear(coconutForms.WingRipple)
						table.clear(coconutForms.Acquiring)
					end

					api.SetCoconutForms = function(coconutForms)
						if featureState.CoconutForms == coconutForms then
							return
						end
						featureState.CoconutForms = coconutForms

						if coconutForms then
							api.RefreshCoconutForms()
						else
							api.ClearCoconutForms()
						end
					end

					api.BlackHole = function(arg, arg2)
						local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()

						if value23 and value24.Health ~= 0 and arg then
							local table17 = {}

							for i = 1, 12 do
								local NinjaShuriken = api.SpawnToy("NinjaShuriken")
								local stickyPart = NinjaShuriken and NinjaShuriken:WaitForChild("StickyPart", 0.25)
								local soundPart = NinjaShuriken and NinjaShuriken:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 0.25)

								if stickyPart and soundPart and api.SNO(soundPart) then
									soundPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 1e10, 0)
									table.insert(table17, NinjaShuriken)
								end
							end

							for _, value25 in pairs(table17) do
								if value25.Parent and value25:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") and value25:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") then
									remotes.StickyPartEvent:FireServer(value25.StickyPart, arg, arg2 or CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))
								end
							end
						end
					end

					api.FindNicePart = function(arg)
						if not arg or not arg:IsA("Model") then
							return
						end
						local value22 = sharedState.GrabbableParts[arg]
						if value22 and value22:IsDescendantOf(arg) and value22.CanQuery and not value22.Anchored then
							return value22
						end
						sharedState.GrabbableParts[arg] = nil
						local value23 = nil

						for _, value24 in ipairs(arg:QueryDescendants("BasePart")) do
							if value24.CanQuery and not value24.Anchored and value24.Name ~= "Button" then
								value23 = value23 or value24
								if (value24.AssemblyRootPart or value24) == value24 then
									sharedState.GrabbableParts[arg] = value24
									return value24
								end
							end
						end

						sharedState.GrabbableParts[arg] = value23
						return value23
					end

					api.MakeFoodEaten = function(arg, arg2)
						local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
						local holdPart = arg and arg:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 0.15)
						local ediblePart = arg and arg:WaitForChild("EdiblePart", 0.15)

						if holdPart and value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0 then
							if not ediblePart then
								return arg
							end
							local holdItemRemoteFunction = holdPart:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 0.15)
							local dropItemRemoteFunction = holdPart:WaitForChild("DropItemRemoteFunction", 0.15)
							if not (holdItemRemoteFunction and dropItemRemoteFunction) then
								return
							end

							task.spawn(function()
								holdItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(arg, value22)
							end)

							local now3 = tick()

							while true do
								task.wait()
								if not (holdPart.AssemblyRootPart == value23 or not holdPart.Parent or value24.Health == 0 or tick() - now3 > 0.7) then
									continue
								end
								break
							end

							if not (holdPart.AssemblyRootPart == value23) then
								return
							end
							remotes.UseFood:FireServer(arg, value22)
							local now4 = tick()

							while true do
								task.wait()
								if not (not ediblePart.Parent or not holdPart.Parent or not (holdPart.AssemblyRootPart == value23) or value24.Health == 0 or tick() - now4 > 4) then
									continue
								end
								break
							end

							if not holdPart.Parent or not (holdPart.AssemblyRootPart == value23) or tick() - now4 > 4 then
								task.spawn(function()
									dropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(arg)
								end)

								return
							end

							task.spawn(function()
								if arg2 then
									dropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(arg)
								else
									dropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(arg, holdPart.CFrame, Vector3.new())
								end
							end)

							local now5 = tick()

							while true do
								task.wait()
								if not (not (holdPart.AssemblyRootPart == value23) or tick() - now5 > 0.7) then
									continue
								end
								break
							end

							for _, value25 in value24:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() do
								local match = value25.Animation and value25.Animation.AnimationId:match("%d+")

								if not (match and sharedState.AnimationWhitelist[match]) then
									value25:Stop()
								end
							end

							if not (holdPart.AssemblyRootPart == value23) then
								return arg
							end
						end
					end

					api.ExplodeMissile = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
						local value22
						value22, value22 = api.Char()
						if not value22 or not arg then
							return false
						end
						local table17 = {}
						arg3 = arg3 or 1

						for i = 1, arg3 do
							local value23 = api.SpawnToy(arg2)

							if value23 then
								local partHitDetector, wedge

								if arg2 == "BombMissile" or arg2 == "FireworkMissile" then
									partHitDetector = value23:WaitForChild("PartHitDetector", 0.15)
									wedge = partHitDetector and value23:WaitForChild("Wedge", 0.15)
								elseif arg2 == "BombDarkMatter" then
									partHitDetector = value23:WaitForChild("PartHitDetector", 0.15)
									wedge = partHitDetector and value23:WaitForChild("Spinner", 0.15)
								elseif arg2 == "BombBalloon" then
									partHitDetector = value23:WaitForChild("Balloon", 0.15)
									wedge = partHitDetector
								else
									partHitDetector = nil
									wedge = nil

									if arg2 == "BallSnowball" then
										partHitDetector = value23:WaitForChild("SnowRagdollPart", 0.15)
										wedge = partHitDetector and value23:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 0.15)
									end
								end

								if partHitDetector and wedge and api.SNO(wedge) then
									local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
									bodyPosition.Parent = wedge
									bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
									local random = math.random
									bodyPosition.Position = Vector3.new(math.random(-1000, 1000), 1000000, random(-1000, 1000))
									table.insert(table17, value23)
								else
									api.DestroyToy(value23)
								end
							end
						end

						for _, value23 in pairs(table17) do
							local partHitDetector, wedge

							if arg2 == "BombMissile" or arg2 == "FireworkMissile" then
								partHitDetector = value23:WaitForChild("PartHitDetector", 0.15)
								wedge = partHitDetector and value23:WaitForChild("Wedge", 0.15)
							elseif arg2 == "BombDarkMatter" then
								partHitDetector = value23:WaitForChild("PartHitDetector", 0.15)
								wedge = partHitDetector and value23:WaitForChild("Spinner", 0.15)
							elseif arg2 == "BombBalloon" then
								partHitDetector = value23:WaitForChild("Balloon", 0.15)
								wedge = partHitDetector
							else
								partHitDetector = nil
								wedge = nil

								if arg2 == "BallSnowball" then
									partHitDetector = value23:WaitForChild("SnowRagdollPart", 0.15)
									wedge = partHitDetector and value23:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 0.15)
								end
							end

							wedge = partHitDetector and wedge

							if wedge then
								if arg2 ~= "BallSnowball" then
									if typeof(arg) == "Instance" then
										remotes.Explode:FireServer({ Hitbox = partHitDetector, PositionPart = arg }, arg.Position)
									elseif typeof(arg) == "Vector3" then
										remotes.Explode:FireServer({
											Hitbox = partHitDetector,
											PositionPart = { Position = arg, AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero },
										}, arg)
									end
								else
									local position = partHitDetector.Position
									tick()

									for i = 1, 2 do
										for _, value24 in value23:GetChildren() do
											if value24:IsA("BasePart") then
												value24.CanCollide = false
												value24.CanTouch = true
												value24.Position = arg.Position
											end
										end

										partHitDetector.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
										RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
									end

									partHitDetector.Position = position
								end
							end

							task.delay(0.25, api.DestroyToy, value23)
						end
					end

					api.IsBarrierBroken = function()
						if configValues.IsBarrierBroken then
							return true
						end
						local Boombox = api.SpawnToy("Boombox", workspace.Waypoints.BlueHouse.CFrame, 4)
						task.wait(2)

						if Boombox and Boombox.Parent then
							api.DestroyToy(Boombox)
							configValues.IsBarrierBroken = true

							task.spawn(function()
								while true do
									task.wait()
									if not (uiLib.Labels and uiLib.Labels.BarrierStatus) then
										continue
									end
									break
								end

								uiLib.Labels.BarrierStatus:SetText("Current barrier status: 🔴")
							end)

							return true
						end

						api.DestroyToy(Boombox)
					end

					task.spawn(api.IsBarrierBroken)

					api.ExtraEscape = function()
						local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
						if not value23 or not value24 or value24.Health == 0 or sharedState.States.Escaping or localPlayer.InPlot.Value then
							return
						end
						sharedState.States.Escaping = true
						local TractorGreen = api.SpawnToy("TractorGreen", workspace.Waypoints.TudorHouse.CFrame)
						if not TractorGreen then
							sharedState.States.Escaping = false
							return
						end
						local vehicleSeat = TractorGreen:WaitForChild("VehicleSeat", 1)
						if not vehicleSeat then
							sharedState.States.Escaping = false
							return api.DestroyToy(TractorGreen)
						end
						local now3 = tick()
						local state12 = false
						value24.Sit = false
						vehicleSeat:Sit(value24)

						local connection = vehicleSeat.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
							if child.Name == "SeatWeld" then
								child.Enabled = false
								state12 = true
								child:Destroy()
							end
						end)

						while true do
							waitAtLeast()
							if not (state12 or tick() - now3 > 0.7) then
								continue
							end
							break
						end

						connection:Disconnect()
						value24.Sit = false
						sharedState.States.Escaping = false
						return api.DestroyToy(TractorGreen)
					end

					api.DestroyTrain = function()
						if featureState.GucciAntiGrab then
							return
						end
						local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
						if not value23 or not value24 then
							return
						end
						local leftGripAttachment = value22 and value22:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") and value22["Left Arm"]:FindFirstChild("LeftGripAttachment")
						if not leftGripAttachment then
							return
						end
						local objectModel = alwaysHereTweenedObjects.Train.Object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
						if not objectModel then
							return notify("Train doesn't exist!")
						end
						local seat = objectModel:FindFirstChild("Seat")
						if not seat then
							return notify("Train seat doesn't exist!")
						end

						if value24.Sit then
							value24.Sit = false
						end

						local cFrame2 = value23.CFrame
						seat:Sit(value24)
						local now3 = tick()

						while true do
							task.wait()
							if not (seat.Occupant == value24 or tick() - now3 > 1.5) then
								continue
							end
							break
						end

						if tick() - now3 > 1.5 then
							return
						end
						local FoodHamburger = api.SpawnToy("FoodHamburger")
						local holdPart = FoodHamburger and FoodHamburger:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 0.25)
						if not holdPart then
							return api.DestroyToy(FoodHamburger)
						end
						local holdItemRemoteFunction = holdPart:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 0.45)
						local dropItemRemoteFunction = holdItemRemoteFunction and holdPart:WaitForChild("DropItemRemoteFunction", 0.45)
						local rigidConstraint = dropItemRemoteFunction and holdPart:WaitForChild("RigidConstraint", 0.45)
						if not rigidConstraint then
							return api.DestroyToy(FoodHamburger)
						end
						local now4 = tick()

						while value22.Parent and leftGripAttachment.Parent and rigidConstraint.Parent and rigidConstraint.Attachment1 ~= leftGripAttachment and value24.Health ~= 0 and tick() - now4 < 1 do
							task.spawn(function()
								holdItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(FoodHamburger, value22)
							end)

							waitAtLeast()
						end

						task.spawn(function()
							local vector3 = Vector3.new
							dropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(FoodHamburger, CFrame.new(0, -150, 0), vector3())
						end)

						if tick() - now4 > 1 then
							return
						end
						task.wait(0.3)
						workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = fallenPartsDestroyHeight
						api.Teleport(value23, CFrame.new(0, -250, 0))
						task.wait(0.3)
						value24.Sit = false
						task.wait(0.15)
						api.Teleport(value23, cFrame2)
						local value25 = workspace
						local fallenPartsDestroyHeight2

						if featureState.DestroyVoid then
							fallenPartsDestroyHeight2 = (0/0)
						else
							fallenPartsDestroyHeight2 = -100
						end

						value25.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = fallenPartsDestroyHeight2
						api.DestroyToy(FoodHamburger)
					end

					api.DestroyObject = function(arg, arg2)
						if not arg or not arg:IsA("Model") then
							return
						end
						arg2 = arg2 or api.FindNicePart(arg)
						if not arg2 or arg2.Anchored or not api.SNO(arg2) then

							task.cancel(configValues.DestroyThread)

							workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -10000

							local value22 = workspace
							local fallenPartsDestroyHeight2

								fallenPartsDestroyHeight2 = (0/0)

								toggles.DisblVoid:SetDisabled(false)

					api.ClearDestroyObjects = function()
						if configValues.DestroyThread then

					func21 = function(arg)

					end

					func22 = function(arg)
						if not (arg and arg.Parent) then
							return
						end

						if arg:IsA("Player") then
							return arg
						end
						local model

						if arg:IsA("Model") then
							model = arg
						else
							model = arg:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
						end

						local value22 = objectStates[arg]
						local parent2

							parent2 = model
							value22 = arg
						else
							parent2 = model
						end

								if playerFromCharacter then
									return playerFromCharacter
								end

								if objectStates[parent2] then
									value22 = parent2
								end
							end

							parent2 = parent2.Parent
						end

						if arg == map or arg:IsDescendantOf(map) then
							return
						end
						value22 = value22 or model

						if value22 then
							arg = value22
						elseif not arg:IsA("BasePart") then
							arg = nil
						end

						return arg
					end

					func23 = function(arg)
						if not (arg and arg.Parent) then
							return
						end

						if arg:IsA("Player") then
							local value22, value23 = api.Char(arg)
							if value22 == arg.Character then
								return value22, value23
							end
							return
						end

						if arg:IsA("BasePart") then
							return arg, arg
						end
						local primaryPart = arg.PrimaryPart

						if not (primaryPart and primaryPart:IsDescendantOf(arg) and primaryPart.CanQuery and not primaryPart.Anchored) then
							primaryPart = api.FindNicePart(arg)
						end

						if not (primaryPart and primaryPart:IsDescendantOf(arg)) then
							sharedState.GrabbableParts[arg] = nil
							primaryPart = arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
						end

						return arg, primaryPart
					end

					do
						local function func30(arg)
							local value22 = pairs
							local collisions = arg.Collisions or {}

							for k, collision in value22(collisions) do
								collision.Connection:Disconnect()

								if k.Parent then
									k.CanCollide = collision.Value
								end
							end

							arg.Collisions = nil
						end

						func24 = function(arg)
							local group = arg.Group

							if group and group.Noclip then
								arg.Collisions = arg.Collisions or {}
								local value22 = ipairs
								local parts = arg.Parts or {}

								for _, part4 in value22(parts) do
									if not (not part4.Parent or arg.Collisions[part4]) then
										arg.Collisions[part4] = {
											Value = part4.CanCollide,
											Connection = part4:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanCollide"):Connect(function()
												if part4.CanCollide then
													part4.CanCollide = false
												end
											end),
										}

										part4.CanCollide = false
									end
								end
							else
								func30(arg)
							end

							if not (group and group.Highlight) then
								if arg.Anchored then
									group = anchorSettings
								else
									group = nil
								end
							end

							if group and arg.Model and arg.Model.Parent then
								if not (arg.Highlight and arg.Highlight.Parent) then
									arg.Highlight = Instance.new("Highlight", arg.Model)
									arg.Highlight.Name = "AnchorHighlight"
									arg.Highlight.Adornee = arg.Model
								end

								arg.Highlight.FillColor = group.FillColor
								arg.Highlight.OutlineColor = group.OutlineColor
							elseif arg.Highlight then
								arg.Highlight:Destroy()
								arg.Highlight = nil
							end
						end

						local function func31(arg)
							arg.Position:Destroy()
							arg.Orientation:Destroy()
							arg.Attachment:Destroy()

							if arg.Target then
								arg.Target:Destroy()
							end

							if arg.Part.Parent then
								arg.Part.CustomPhysicalProperties = arg.Physics
								arg.Part.Massless = arg.Massless
								api.ResetVelocity(arg.Part)
							end
						end

						func25 = function(arg, parent2, cFrame2)
							if parent2 then
								local target = arg.Target

								if not target then
									target = Instance.new("Attachment")
									target.Name = "GroupAttachment"
									arg.Target = target
								end

								target.Parent = parent2
								target.CFrame = cFrame2
								arg.Position.Attachment1 = target
								arg.Position.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment
								arg.Orientation.Attachment1 = target
								arg.Orientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment
							elseif arg.Target then
								arg.Position.Attachment1 = nil
								arg.Position.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
								arg.Orientation.Attachment1 = nil
								arg.Orientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
								arg.Target:Destroy()
								arg.Target = nil
							end
						end

						local function func32(arg)
							local assemblyRootPart = arg.Root.AssemblyRootPart or arg.Root
							local value22 = ipairs
							local parts = arg.Parts or {}
							local num37 = nil

							for _, part4 in value22(parts) do
								local canCollide = part4.Parent and part4.CanCollide

								if canCollide then
									canCollide = (part4.AssemblyRootPart or part4) == assemblyRootPart
								end

								if canCollide then
									local num38 = part4.Size * 0.5
									local cFrame2 = part4.CFrame
									local x = num38.X
									local y = num38.Y
									local z = num38.Z
									num37 = math.min(num37 or math.huge, cFrame2.Position.Y - math.abs(cFrame2.RightVector.Y) * x + math.abs(cFrame2.UpVector.Y) * y + math.abs(cFrame2.LookVector.Y) * z)
								end
							end

							return num37
						end

						func26 = function(arg, arg2)
							local value22 = nil
							local value23 = nil

							for k in pairs(arg.Members) do
								if k.Root and k.Root.Parent then
									local value24 = func32(k)

									if value24 and (not value22 or value24 < value22) then
										value22 = value24
										value23 = k
									end
								end
							end

							return value23 or arg2
						end

						func27 = function(arg)
							local assemblyLinearVelocity = arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity
							local assemblyAngularVelocity = arg.AssemblyAngularVelocity

							if assemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 50 then
								arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = assemblyLinearVelocity.Unit * 50
							end

							if assemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude > 15 then
								arg.AssemblyAngularVelocity = assemblyAngularVelocity.Unit * 15
							end
						end

						func28 = function(arg)
							local value22 = ipairs
							local grabConnections = arg.GrabConnections or {}

							for _, grabConnection in value22(grabConnections) do
								grabConnection:Disconnect()
							end

							arg.GrabConnections = nil
							arg.Held = nil
							arg.HeldRoot = nil
							func30(arg)

							for _, connection in ipairs(arg.Connections) do
								connection:Disconnect()
							end

							for _, node in pairs(arg.Nodes) do
								func31(node)
							end

							table.clear(arg.Connections)
							table.clear(arg.Nodes)

							if arg.Highlight then
								arg.Highlight:Destroy()
								arg.Highlight = nil
							end

							arg.Root = nil
							arg.Model = nil
							arg.Parts = nil
						end

						func29 = function(arg, model, root)
							if arg.Model ~= model then
								func28(arg)
								arg.Model = model

								local function func33(descendant)
									if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
										arg.Dirty = true
									end
								end

								arg.Connections[1] = model.DescendantAdded:Connect(func33)
								arg.Connections[2] = model.DescendantRemoving:Connect(func33)
							end

							arg.Root = root
							arg.Dirty = false
							local parts

							if model:IsA("BasePart") then
								parts = { model }
							else
								parts = model:QueryDescendants("BasePart")
							end

							arg.Parts = parts
							local value22 = pairs
							local collisions = arg.Collisions or {}

							for k, collision in value22(collisions) do
								if k ~= model and not k:IsDescendantOf(model) then
									collision.Connection:Disconnect()

									if k.Parent then
										k.CanCollide = collision.Value
									end

									arg.Collisions[k] = nil
								end
							end

							local table17 = {}
							local grabParts = workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")
							grabParts = grabParts and grabParts:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
							grabParts = grabParts and grabParts:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")
							grabParts = grabParts and grabParts.Part1
							local assemblyRootPart

							if grabParts and (grabParts == model or grabParts:IsDescendantOf(model)) then
								assemblyRootPart = grabParts.AssemblyRootPart
							else
								assemblyRootPart = nil
							end

							for _, part4 in ipairs(parts) do
								local assemblyRootPart2 = part4.AssemblyRootPart or part4

								if not assemblyRootPart2.Anchored then
									if assemblyRootPart2 == model or assemblyRootPart2:IsDescendantOf(model) then
										table17[assemblyRootPart2] = true
									elseif assemblyRootPart2 == assemblyRootPart then
										table17[root] = true
									end
								end
							end

							for k, node in pairs(arg.Nodes) do
								if not table17[k] then
									func31(node)
									arg.Nodes[k] = nil
								end
							end

							for k in pairs(table17) do
								if not arg.Nodes[k] then
									local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", k)
									attachment.Name = "AnchorAttachment"
									local alignPosition = Instance.new("AlignPosition", k)
									alignPosition.Name = "AnchorPosition"
									alignPosition.Attachment0 = attachment
									alignPosition.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
									alignPosition.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = true
									alignPosition.RigidityEnabled = true
									alignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
									alignPosition.MaxVelocity = math.huge
									alignPosition.Enabled = false
									local alignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation", k)
									alignOrientation.Name = "AnchorOrientation"
									alignOrientation.Attachment0 = attachment
									alignOrientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
									alignOrientation.RigidityEnabled = true
									alignOrientation.MaxTorque = math.huge
									alignOrientation.MaxAngularVelocity = math.huge
									alignOrientation.Enabled = false

									arg.Nodes[k] = {
										Part = k,
										Attachment = attachment,
										Position = alignPosition,
										Orientation = alignOrientation,
										Offset = root.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(k.CFrame),
										Physics = k.CustomPhysicalProperties,
										Massless = k.Massless,
									}
								end
							end

							if arg.Group then
								local group = arg.Group
								group.CollisionDirty = true

								if not arg.Held and (not group.Driver or group.Driver == arg) then
									local value23 = func26(group, arg)
									group.Leader = value23
									group.Driver = value23
								end
							end

							func24(arg)
						end
					end
				end

				do
					local func30, func31, func32

					do
						do
							local function func33()
								api.SetRuntimeConnection("Anchors", RunService.Heartbeat, not unloaded and next(objectStates) ~= nil, api.UpdateAnchors)
							end

							func30 = function(arg)
								local value22 = objectStates[arg]

								if value22 then
									if not (value22.Root and value22.Root.Parent) then
										value22 = nil
									end

									return value22
								end

								if arg:IsA("Player") and table16[arg.UserId] then
									return
								end
								local value23, value24 = func23(arg)
								if not (value23 and value24 and value24.Parent) or value24.Anchored then
									return

								if api.ClearObjectAura then
									api.ClearObjectAura(value23)
								end

								objectStates[arg] = table17
								func29(table17, value23, value24)

								if not next(table17.Nodes) then
									objectStates[arg] = nil
									func28(table17)
									return
								end

								local grabPart = grabParts and grabParts:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")

								if weldConstraint and func22(weldConstraint.Part1) == arg then
									api.HandleAnchorGrab(weldConstraint.Part1, grabParts)
								end

								func33()
								return table17
							end

							func31 = function(arg, anchored)
								if arg.Anchored == anchored then
									return
								end
								arg.Anchored = anchored
								local anchored2 = sharedState.Anchored
								local target = arg.Target
								local value22

								if anchored then
									value22 = arg
								else
									value22 = nil
								end
