									local userId = arg.Target.UserId
									local value24

									if anchored then
										value24 = arg
									else
										value24 = nil
									end

									value23[userId] = value24
								end

								if anchored and arg.Root and arg.Root.Parent then
									arg.CFrame = arg.Root.CFrame
								end

								if not anchored then
									for _, node in pairs(arg.Nodes) do
										node.Position.Enabled = false
										node.Orientation.Enabled = false

										if node.Part.Parent then
											node.Part.CustomPhysicalProperties = node.Physics
											node.Part.Massless = node.Massless
											api.ResetVelocity(node.Part)
										end
									end
								end

								func24(arg)
							end

							func32 = function(arg)
								objectStates[arg.Target] = nil
								sharedState.Anchored[arg.Target] = nil

								if arg.Target:IsA("Player") then
									table16[arg.Target.UserId] = nil
								end

								func28(arg)
								func33()
							end
						end
					end

					api.GetObjectTarget = function()
						local grabParts = workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")
						local grabPart = grabParts and grabParts:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
						grabPart = grabPart and grabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")
						local part1 = grabPart and grabPart.Part1
						return func22(part1 or api.GetTarget()), part1 and grabParts
					end

					api.SetAnchored = function(arg, anchored)
						local value22

						if objectStates[arg] then
							value22 = arg
						else
							value22 = func22(arg)
						end

						if not value22 or anchored and unloaded then
							return false
						end
						local value23 = objectStates[value22]
						local value24

						if value23 then
							value24 = value23
						elseif anchored then
							value24 = func30(value22)
						else
							value24 = nil
						end

						if not value24 then
							return false
						end
						local group = value24.Group

						if group then
							group.Anchored = anchored

							for k in pairs(group.Members) do
								func31(k, anchored)
							end
						else
							func31(value24, anchored)

							if not anchored then
								func32(value24)
							end
						end

						return true
					end

					api.ToggleAnchor = function()
						local value22 = api.GetObjectTarget()
						if value22 then
							return api.SetAnchored(value22, not sharedState.Anchored[value22])
						end
					end

					do
						local function func33(arg, arg2, arg3)
							if objectStates[arg.Target] ~= arg or arg.Held ~= arg2 then
								return
							end

							if arg.HeldRoot and arg.HeldRoot.Parent then
								arg.CFrame = arg.HeldRoot.CFrame * arg.HeldOffset:Inverse()
							end

							if arg.Group then
								local group = arg.Group
								local num37 = arg.CFrame * group.Members[arg]:Inverse()

								for k, member in pairs(group.Members) do
									k.CFrame = num37 * member
								end

								local value22 = func26(group, arg)
								group.Leader = value22
								group.Driver = value22
							end

							arg.Held = nil
							arg.HeldRoot = nil
							arg.Dirty = true
							arg.RetryAt = 0
							local value22 = ipairs
							local grabConnections = arg.GrabConnections or {}

							for _, grabConnection in value22(grabConnections) do
								grabConnection:Disconnect()
							end

							arg.GrabConnections = nil

							if not unloaded and not arg3 then
								api.UpdateAnchors()
							end
						end

						api.HandleAnchorGrab = function(heldRoot, held)
							local value22 = objectStates[func22(heldRoot)]
							if not value22 or value22.Held == held then
								return
							end
							api.DisableEndGrabEarly()
							local value23 = ipairs
							local grabConnections = value22.GrabConnections or {}

							for _, grabConnection in value23(grabConnections) do
								grabConnection:Disconnect()
							end

							value22.Held = held
							value22.HeldRoot = heldRoot
							value22.HeldOffset = value22.Root.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(heldRoot.CFrame)

							if value22.Group then
								value22.Group.Leader = value22
								value22.Group.Driver = value22
							end

							for _, node in pairs(value22.Nodes) do
								node.Position.Enabled = false
								node.Orientation.Enabled = false

								if node.Part.Parent then
									node.Part.CustomPhysicalProperties = node.Physics
									node.Part.Massless = node.Massless
								end
							end

							value22.GrabConnections = {
								held.Destroying:Connect(function()
									func33(value22, held)
								end),
							}

							local grabPart = held:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
							local weldConstraint = grabPart and grabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")

							if weldConstraint then
								value22.GrabConnections[2] = weldConstraint:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Part1"):Connect(function()
									local target = value22.Target

									if func22(weldConstraint.Part1) ~= target then
										func33(value22, held)
									end
								end)
							end
						end

						local function func34(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
							local state12 = arg.HeldRoot and arg4.AssemblyRootPart == arg.HeldRoot.AssemblyRootPart

							if arg.Held then
								arg2 = not state12
							else
								arg2 = arg.Anchored or arg2 and arg2.Driver ~= arg

								if not arg2 then
									arg2 = arg4 ~= (arg3.AssemblyRootPart or arg3)
								end
							end

							return state12, arg2 and not arg4.Anchored
						end

						api.UpdateAnchors = function()
							local now3 = os.clock()

							for k, value22 in pairs(table16) do
								if not value22.Target.Parent then
									local playerByUserId = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(k)

									if playerByUserId then
										objectStates[value22.Target] = nil
										sharedState.Anchored[value22.Target] = nil
										func28(value22)
										value22.Target = playerByUserId
										value22.RetryAt = 0
										objectStates[playerByUserId] = value22
										sharedState.Anchored[playerByUserId] = value22
									end
								end
							end

							for k, objectState in pairs(objectStates) do
								local value22, value23 = func23(k)

								if not (value22 and value23 and value23.Parent) then
									if k:IsA("Player") and (k.Parent or objectState.Anchored) then
										if objectState.Root then
											func28(objectState)
										end
									else
										api.RemoveObjectFromGroup(k)

										if objectStates[k] then
											func32(objectState)
										end
									end
								else
									if value22 ~= objectState.Model or value23 ~= objectState.Root or objectState.Dirty and not objectState.Held then
										func29(objectState, value22, value23)
									end

									if objectState.Held then
										local grabPart = objectState.Held:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
										grabPart = grabPart and grabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")

										if objectState.HeldRoot and objectState.HeldRoot.Parent then
											objectState.CFrame = objectState.HeldRoot.CFrame * objectState.HeldOffset:Inverse()
										end

										if not objectState.Held.Parent or not grabPart or func22(grabPart.Part1) ~= k then
											func33(objectState, objectState.Held, true)
										end
									end
								end
							end

							for _, objectGroup in pairs(objectGroups) do
								if objectGroup.CollisionDirty then
									api.UpdateGroupCollisions(objectGroup)
								end

								local leader = objectGroup.Leader

								for k in pairs(objectGroup.Members) do
									if k.Held and k.Root and k.Root.Parent then
										leader = k
										break
									end
								end

								if leader and leader.Root and leader.Root.Parent and (not objectGroup.Anchored or leader.Held) then
									local cFrame2

									if leader.Held then
										cFrame2 = leader.CFrame
									else
										cFrame2 = leader.Root.CFrame
									end

									local num37 = cFrame2 * objectGroup.Members[leader]:Inverse()

									for k, member in pairs(objectGroup.Members) do
										k.CFrame = num37 * member
									end
								end

								if leader and not leader.Held and not objectGroup.Anchored then
									for k in pairs(objectGroup.Members) do
										for k2 in pairs(k.Nodes) do
											if k2.Parent then
												func27(k2)
											end
										end
									end
								end

								objectGroup.Driver = leader
							end

							for k, objectState in pairs(objectStates) do
								local root = objectState.Root

								if root and root.Parent then
									local group = objectState.Group

									if group and group.Driver == objectState and not objectState.Anchored and not objectState.Held then
										objectState.CFrame = root.CFrame
									end

									local anchored = objectState.Anchored or group and group.Highlight

									if anchored then
										anchored = not (objectState.Highlight and objectState.Highlight.Parent)
									end

									if anchored then
										func24(objectState)
									end

									local table17 = nil

									for k2 in pairs(objectState.Nodes) do
										if k2.Parent then
											local value22, state12 = func34(objectState, group, root, k2)
											local state13 = (k2.AssemblyRootPart or k2) == k2 or value22

											if state13 then
												state12 = group ~= nil or state12
											else
												state12 = state13
											end

											if state12 and not func21(k2) then
												table17 = table17 or {}
												table17[#table17 + 1] = k2
											end
										end
									end

									if table17 then
										for _, node in pairs(objectState.Nodes) do
											node.Position.Enabled = false
											node.Orientation.Enabled = false
										end

										if not objectState.Acquiring and now3 >= objectState.RetryAt then
											objectState.Acquiring = true

											task.spawn(function()
												local model = objectState.Model

												local function func35(arg)
													return objectStates[k] == objectState and objectState.Model == model and (not arg or objectState.Nodes[arg] ~= nil)
												end

												for _, value22 in ipairs(table17) do
													if func35() then
														if value22.Parent and func35(value22) and not func21(value22) then
															if pcall(api.SNO, value22, 0.75, function()
																return func35(value22)
															end) then
																continue
															end
														else
															continue
														end
													end

													break
												end

												objectState.Acquiring = false
												objectState.RetryAt = os.clock() + 0.25
											end)
										end
									else
										for k2, node in pairs(objectState.Nodes) do
											if not k2.Parent then
												objectState.Dirty = true
											else
												local value22, value23 = func34(objectState, group, root, k2)

												if (k2.AssemblyRootPart or k2) ~= k2 and not value22 then
													objectState.Dirty = true
												elseif not value23 then
													node.Position.Enabled = false
													node.Orientation.Enabled = false
													func25(node)

													if k2.CustomPhysicalProperties ~= node.Physics then
														k2.CustomPhysicalProperties = node.Physics
													end

													if k2.Massless ~= node.Massless then
														k2.Massless = node.Massless
													end
												else
													local physics = node.Physics
													local massless = node.Massless

													if k2.CustomPhysicalProperties ~= physics then
														k2.CustomPhysicalProperties = physics
													end

													if k2.Massless ~= massless then
														k2.Massless = massless
													end

													local num37 = objectState.CFrame * node.Offset
													local state12 = objectState.Anchored == true

													if state12 then
														state12 = not (group and group.Driver and group.Driver.Held)
													end

													local driver = not state12 and group and group.Driver
													local heldRoot

													if not driver then
														heldRoot = nil
													elseif driver.HeldRoot and driver.HeldRoot.Parent then
														heldRoot = driver.HeldRoot
													else
														heldRoot = driver.Root
													end

													if heldRoot and heldRoot.Parent then
														local value24 = group.Members[objectState]
														local num38 = group.Members[driver]:Inverse() * value24 * node.Offset

														if heldRoot == driver.HeldRoot then
															num38 = driver.HeldOffset:Inverse() * num38
														end

														func25(node, heldRoot, num38)
													else
														func25(node)
														node.Position.Position = num37.Position
														node.Orientation.CFrame = num37.Rotation
													end

													node.Position.Enabled = value23
													node.Orientation.Enabled = value23

													if state12 then
														local state13 = math.floor(now3 * 15) % 2 == 0
														local vector3

														if state13 then
															vector3 = Vector3.new(0, 0.002, 0)
														else
															vector3 = -vector
														end

														k2.CFrame = num37 + vector3
														local vector4

														if state13 then
															vector4 = Vector3.new(0, 0.05, 0)
														else
															vector4 = -vector2
														end

														k2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vector4
														k2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					api.ClearAnchors = function()
						for _, objectGroup in pairs(objectGroups) do
							objectGroup.Anchored = false
						end

						for _, objectState in pairs(objectStates) do
							func31(objectState, false)

					api.UpdateGroupCollisions = function(arg)
						if arg.CollisionFolder then
							arg.CollisionFolder:Destroy()
						end

						arg.CollisionFolder = nil
						arg.CollisionDirty = false
						if arg.Deleting or not next(arg.Members) then
							return
						end
						local folder2 = Instance.new("Folder", workspace)
						local table17 = {}
						folder2.Name = "ObjectGroupCollisions"
						arg.CollisionFolder = folder2

						for k in pairs(arg.Members) do
							local value22 = ipairs
							local parts = k.Parts or {}

							for _, part4 in value22(parts) do
								if part4.Parent then
									for _, value23 in ipairs(table17) do
										if value23.State ~= k then
											local noCollisionConstraint = Instance.new("NoCollisionConstraint")
											noCollisionConstraint.Part0 = part4
											noCollisionConstraint.Part1 = value23.Part
											noCollisionConstraint.Parent = folder2
										end
									end

									table17[#table17 + 1] = { Part = part4, State = k }
								end
							end
						end
					end

					api.UpdateObjectGroup = function(arg)
						for k in pairs(arg.Members) do
							func24(k)
						end
					end

					api.RefreshObjectGroups = function()
						if unloaded then
							return
						end
						local table17 = {}

						for k in pairs(objectGroups) do
							table17[#table17 + 1] = k
						end

						table.sort(table17, function(arg, arg2)
							return objectGroups[arg].Index < objectGroups[arg2].Index
						end)

						local objectGroup = options.ObjectGroup
						local objectGroup2 = configValues.ObjectGroup

						if objectGroup then
							objectGroup:SetValues(table17)
							local value22 = objectGroup
							local setValue = value22.SetValue

							if not objectGroups[objectGroup2] then
								objectGroup2 = nil
							end

							setValue(value22, objectGroup2)
						end

						local value22 = objectGroups[configValues.ObjectGroup]
						local objects = 0

						if value22 then
							for k in pairs(value22.Members) do
								objects += 1
							end
						end

						if configValues.ObjectGroupLabel then
							configValues.ObjectGroupLabel:SetText("Objects: " .. objects)
						end
					end

					api.CreateObjectGroup = function()
						num36 += 1
						local objectGroup = "Group " .. num36

						objectGroups[objectGroup] = {
							Name = objectGroup,
							Index = num36,
							Members = {},
							Anchored = false,
							Noclip = false,
							Highlight = false,
							FillColor = anchorSettings.FillColor,
							OutlineColor = anchorSettings.OutlineColor,
						}

						configValues.ObjectGroup = objectGroup
						api.RefreshObjectGroups()
					end

					api.RemoveObjectFromGroup = function(arg)
						if not objectStates[arg] then
							arg = func22(arg) or arg
						end

						local value22 = objectStates[arg]
						local group = value22 and value22.Group
						if not group then
							return false
						end
						group.Members[value22] = nil
						value22.Group = nil
						api.UpdateGroupCollisions(group)
						func24(value22)

						if group.Leader == value22 then
							group.Leader = next(group.Members)
						end

						if group.Driver == value22 then
							group.Driver = group.Leader
						end

						if not value22.Anchored then
							func32(value22)
						end

						api.RefreshObjectGroups()
						return true
					end

					api.AddObjectToGroup = function()
						if unloaded then
							return
						end
						local value22 = api.GetObjectTarget()
						local value23 = objectGroups[configValues.ObjectGroup]
						if not value23 then
							return notify("Select an object group first.")
						end

						if not value22 or value22:IsA("Player") then
							return notify("Grab a movable toy first.")
						end
						local value24 = func30(value22)
						if not value24 then
							return notify("This object cannot be grouped.")
						end

						if value24.Group == value23 then
							return
						end

						if value24.Group then
							return notify("Remove this object from its current group first.")
						end
						local leader = value23.Leader

						if leader then
							leader = not (value23.Leader.Root and value23.Leader.Root.Parent)
						end

						if leader then
							api.RemoveObjectFromGroup(value23.Leader.Target)
						end

						value23.Leader = value23.Leader or value24
						local leader2 = value23.Leader
						local num37

						if value23.Members[leader2] then
							num37 = leader2.Root.CFrame * value23.Members[leader2]:Inverse()
						else
							num37 = value24.Root.CFrame
						end

						value23.Members[value24] = num37:ToObjectSpace(value24.Root.CFrame)
						value24.Group = value23

						if not value23.Driver then
							local value25 = func26(value23, value24)
							value23.Leader = value25
							value23.Driver = value25
						end

						api.UpdateGroupCollisions(value23)

						if value23.Anchored or value24.Anchored then
							value23.Anchored = true

							for k in pairs(value23.Members) do
								func31(k, true)
							end
						end

						func24(value24)
						api.RefreshObjectGroups()
					end

					api.DeleteObjectGroup = function(arg)
						local value22 = objectGroups[arg]
						if not value22 then
							return
						end
						value22.Deleting = true

						while next(value22.Members) do
							api.RemoveObjectFromGroup(next(value22.Members).Target)
						end

						if value22.CollisionFolder then
							value22.CollisionFolder:Destroy()
						end

						objectGroups[arg] = nil
						api.RefreshObjectGroups()
					end

					api.ReleaseObject = function(arg)
						if not objectStates[arg] then
							arg = func22(arg) or arg
						end

						api.RemoveObjectFromGroup(arg)
						local value22 = objectStates[arg]

						if value22 then
							func32(value22)
						end
					end
				end

					do
						api.ClearObjectGroups = function()
							for k in pairs(objectGroups) do
								api.DeleteObjectGroup(k)
							end
						end

						api.BananaRagdoll = function(arg)
							local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
							local rightLeg, value25, value26 = api.Char(arg)
							rightLeg = rightLeg and rightLeg:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")

							if rightLeg and value25 and value26 and value26.Health > 0 and value23 and value24.Health > 0 and api.GetMagnitude(value25, workspace.SpawnLocation) < 3000 then
								local rscBanana = toysFolder:FindFirstChild("RSCBanana") or api.SpawnToy("FoodBanana")
								local weight = rscBanana and rscBanana:WaitForChild("Weight", 0.25)
								local hitboxPart = weight and rscBanana:WaitForChild("HitboxPart", 0.25)
								local main = hitboxPart and rscBanana:WaitForChild("Main", 0.25)
								if not main or not api.MakeFoodEaten(rscBanana, true) or not value25 then
									return api.DestroyToy(rscBanana)
								end

								if weight.ReceiveAge ~= 0 then
									if not api.SNO(hitboxPart) then
										return api.DestroyToy(rscBanana)
									end
									api.RNO(hitboxPart)
								end

								if rscBanana.Name ~= "RSCBanana" then
									for _, child in pairs(rscBanana:GetChildren()) do
										if child:IsA("BasePart") then
											child.CanCollide = false
											child.CanTouch = true
											child.Transparency = 1
										end
									end

									rscBanana.Name = "RSCBanana"
								end

								local now3 = tick()

								while tick() - now3 <= 0.075 do
									main.CFrame = rightLeg.CFrame
									main.Velocity = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1)
									RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
									api.ResetVelocity(main)
								end
							end
						end

						api.MakeBlack = function(arg)
							local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
							local value25, value26, value27 = api.Char(arg)

							if value24 and value24.Health > 0 and value23 and value26 and value27 and value27.Health > 0 and api.GetMagnitude(value26, workspace.SpawnLocation) < 3000 and (not localPlayer.InPlot.Value or configValues.IsBarrierBroken) then
								local rscOven = toysFolder:FindFirstChild("RSCOven") or api.SpawnToy("OvenDarkGray")
								if not rscOven then
									return
								end
								local buttonOven = rscOven:WaitForChild("ButtonOven", 0.25)
								if not buttonOven then
									return api.DestroyToy(rscOven)
								end
								local main = rscOven:WaitForChild("Main", 0.25)
								if not main then
									return api.DestroyToy(rscOven)
								end
								local value28 = api.FindNicePart(rscOven)
								if not api.SNO(value28) then
									return api.DestroyToy(rscOven)
								end

								if buttonOven.Transparency ~= 1 then
									main.CFrame = value23.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 0)
									task.wait(0.15)
									api.Grab(buttonOven)
									task.wait(0.15)
								end

								if rscOven.Name ~= "RSCOven" then
									for _, value29 in rscOven:GetChildren() do
										if value29:IsA("BasePart") or value29:IsA("MeshPart") then
											value29.CanCollide = false
											value29.CanTouch = true
											value29.CanQuery = true
											value29.Transparency = 1
										end
									end

									rscOven.Name = "RSCOven"
								end

								local now3 = tick()

								while tick() - now3 <= 0.1 do
									api.Teleport(main, value26)
									RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
								end
							end
						end

						api.Microwave = function(arg)
							local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
							local value25, value26, value27 = api.Char(arg)

							if value24 and value24.Health > 0 and value23 and value26 and value27 and value27.Health > 0 and api.GetMagnitude(value26, workspace.SpawnLocation) < 3000 and (not localPlayer.InPlot.Value or configValues.IsBarrierBroken) then

									task.wait(0.15)
								end

								if rscMicrowave.Name ~= "RSCMicrowave" then
									for _, value29 in rscMicrowave:GetChildren() do

											value29.CanCollide = false
											value29.CanTouch = true
											value29.CanQuery = true
											value29.Transparency = 1
										end

								end

								local now3 = tick()

									api.Teleport(primaryPart, value26)
									RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
								end
							end
						end

						api.Campfire = function(arg)
							local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
							local value25, value26, value27 = api.Char(arg)

							if value26 and value27 and value27.Health > 0 and value23 and value24.Health > 0 and api.GetMagnitude(value26, workspace.SpawnLocation) < 3000 then
								local rscCampfire = toysFolder:FindFirstChild("RSCCampfire") or api.SpawnToy("Campfire")
								local soundPart = rscCampfire and rscCampfire:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 0.25)
								local main = soundPart and rscCampfire:WaitForChild("Main", 0.25)
								if not main or not value26 or not api.SNO(soundPart) then
									return api.DestroyToy(rscCampfire)
								end

								if rscCampfire.Name ~= "RSCCampfire" then
									for _, child in pairs(rscCampfire:GetChildren()) do
										if child:IsA("BasePart") then
											child.CanCollide = false
											child.CanTouch = true
											child.Transparency = 1
										end
									end

									rscCampfire.Name = "RSCCampfire"
								end

								local now3 = tick()

								while tick() - now3 <= 0.1 do
									api.Teleport(main, value26.CFrame - Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
									RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
								end
							end
						end

						do
							local flingState = sharedState.FlingState

							api.ClearFling = function()
								local toy = flingState.Toy
								local part4 = flingState.Part
								local value22 = flingState
								local value23 = flingState
								flingState.Toy = nil
								value22.Part = nil
								value23.ActiveMethod = nil

								if part4 and part4.Parent then
									api.ResetVelocity(part4)
								end

								if toy and toy.Parent then
									api.DestroyToy(toy)
								end
							end

							api.Fling = function(arg)
								if flingState.Busy then
									return
								end
								flingState.Busy = true

								local ok, result = pcall(function()
									local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
									local value25, value26, value27 = api.Char(arg)
									if not (value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0 and value26 and value27 and value27.Health > 0 and api.GetMagnitude(value26, workspace.SpawnLocation) < 3000) then
										return
									end
									local method = flingState.Method
									local toy = flingState.Toy
									local part4 = flingState.Part

									if not (toy and toy.Parent and part4 and part4.Parent and flingState.ActiveMethod == method) then
										api.ClearFling()
										toy = api.SpawnToy(method, nil, 1)
										local value28

										if toy then
											local value29 = toy
											local waitForChild = value29.WaitForChild
											local text5

											if method == "YouDecoy" then
												text5 = "HumanoidRootPart"
											else
												text5 = "Main"
											end

											value28 = waitForChild(value29, text5, 0.5)
										else
											value28 = toy
										end

										if toy then
											local value29 = toy
											local waitForChild = value29.WaitForChild
											local text5

											if method == "YouDecoy" then
												text5 = "HumanoidRootPart"
											else
												text5 = "SoundPart"
											end

											part4 = waitForChild(value29, text5, 0.5)
										else
											part4 = toy
										end

										if not (value28 and part4 and api.SNO(part4)) or flingState.Method ~= method or unloaded then
											api.DestroyToy(toy)
											return
										end
										local value29 = flingState
										local value30 = flingState
										flingState.Toy = toy
										value29.Part = part4
										value30.ActiveMethod = method
									elseif not api.IsPartOwner(part4) and not api.SNO(part4, 0.35) then
										api.ClearFling()
										return
									end

									local now3 = os.clock()
									local BasePart = toy:QueryDescendants("BasePart")
									local table17 = {}
									local num37 = 1

									while true do
										local state12 = unloaded or flingState.Toy ~= toy or flingState.Method ~= method

										if not state12 then
											state12 = not (toy.Parent and value26.Parent and value27.Health > 0)
										end

										if not state12 then
											num37 = -num37
											api.PivotTo(toy, api.PredictCFrame(value26) * CFrame.new(num37 * 0.75, 0, 0), BasePart, table17)
											part4.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(num37 * 250, 120, -num37 * 250)
											part4.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(num37 * 1500, 1500, -num37 * 1500)
											RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
											if not (os.clock() - now3 >= 0.2) then
												continue
											end
										end

										break
									end

									if flingState.Toy == toy and toy.Parent and part4.Parent then
										api.ResetVelocity(part4)
										api.PivotTo(toy, CFrame.new(0, 100000, 0), BasePart, table17)
									end

									return true
								end)

								flingState.Busy = false

								if not ok then
									error(result, 0)
								end

								return result
							end
						end
					end

					do
						local toysAuraSettings

						do
							charRegistry = {
								Shapes = {},
								ShapeNames = {},
								ShapeCache = {},
								Models = {},
								Movers = {},
								Candidates = {},
								Inventories = {},
								ActiveInventories = {},
								CharacterParts = {},
								CharacterRoots = {},
							}

							toysAuraSettings = sharedState.ToysAuraSettings

							charRegistry.ClearCharacterRoots = function()
								api.SetRuntimeConnection("ToyAuraCharacterAdded", nil, false)
								api.SetRuntimeConnection("ToyAuraCharacterRemoved", nil, false)
								charRegistry.Character = nil
								table.clear(charRegistry.CharacterParts)
								table.clear(charRegistry.CharacterRoots)
							end

							charRegistry.UpdateCharacterRoots = function()
								local character = localPlayer.Character

								if character ~= charRegistry.Character then
									charRegistry.ClearCharacterRoots()
									charRegistry.Character = character

									if character then
										api.SetRuntimeConnection("ToyAuraCharacterAdded", character.DescendantAdded, true, function(arg)
											if arg:IsA("BasePart") then
												charRegistry.CharacterParts[arg] = true
											end
										end)

										api.SetRuntimeConnection("ToyAuraCharacterRemoved", character.DescendantRemoving, true, function(arg)
											charRegistry.CharacterParts[arg] = nil
										end)

										for _, value22 in character:QueryDescendants("BasePart") do
											charRegistry.CharacterParts[value22] = true
										end
									end
								end

								local characterRoots = charRegistry.CharacterRoots
								table.clear(characterRoots)

								for k in pairs(charRegistry.CharacterParts) do
									characterRoots[k.AssemblyRootPart or k] = true
								end
							end

							charRegistry.AddCandidate = function(arg, arg2)
								if not arg:IsA("Model") then
									arg = arg:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
								end

								while arg and arg.Parent and arg.Parent:IsA("Model") do
									arg = arg.Parent
								end

								if arg and (arg == arg2 or arg:IsDescendantOf(arg2)) then
									charRegistry.Candidates[arg] = arg2
								end
							end

							charRegistry.UntrackInventory = function(arg)
								local value22 = charRegistry.Inventories[arg]
								if not value22 then
									return
								end
								charRegistry.Inventories[arg] = nil

								for _, value23 in ipairs(value22) do
									value23:Disconnect()
								end

								for k, candidate in pairs(charRegistry.Candidates) do
									if candidate == arg then
										charRegistry.Candidates[k] = nil
										sharedState.ToyAuraRoots[k] = nil
									end
								end
							end

							charRegistry.TrackInventory = function(arg)
								if not (arg and arg.Parent) or charRegistry.Inventories[arg] then
									return
								end

								local table17 = {
									arg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
										charRegistry.AddCandidate(descendant, arg)
									end),
									arg.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(descendant)
										if charRegistry.Candidates[descendant] == arg then
											charRegistry.Candidates[descendant] = nil
											sharedState.ToyAuraRoots[descendant] = nil
										end
									end),
								}

								charRegistry.Inventories[arg] = table17

								task.spawn(function()
									for i, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
										if charRegistry.Inventories[arg] ~= table17 then
											return
										end
										charRegistry.AddCandidate(descendant, arg)

										if i % 100 == 0 then
											task.wait()
										end
									end
								end)
							end

							charRegistry.RefreshInventories = function(arg)
								local activeInventories = charRegistry.ActiveInventories
								table.clear(activeInventories)

								for _, inventory in ipairs(sharedState.Inventories) do
									if inventory and inventory.Parent then
										activeInventories[inventory] = true
										charRegistry.TrackInventory(inventory)
									end
								end

								if arg and arg.Parent then
									activeInventories[arg] = true
									charRegistry.TrackInventory(arg)
								end

								for k in pairs(charRegistry.Inventories) do
									if not activeInventories[k] then
										charRegistry.UntrackInventory(k)
									end
								end
							end

							charRegistry.CleanupCandidates = function()
								for k in pairs(charRegistry.Inventories) do
									charRegistry.UntrackInventory(k)
								end

								table.clear(charRegistry.Candidates)
								table.clear(charRegistry.ActiveInventories)

								if unloaded or not toysAuraSettings.Enabled then
									charRegistry.ClearCharacterRoots()
								end
							end

							charRegistry.GetRoot = function(arg)
								if not arg or not arg.Parent or sharedState.ToyAuraReserved[arg] or arg.Name:find("RSC", 1, true) then
									return
								end
								local value22 = sharedState.ToyAuraRoots[arg]
								local parent2 = value22 and value22.Parent
								local state12

								if parent2 then
									state12 = value22 == arg or value22:IsDescendantOf(arg)
								else
									state12 = parent2
								end

								local primaryPart

								if state12 then
									primaryPart = value22
								else
									primaryPart = nil
								end

								if not primaryPart then
									if arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
										return
									end

									if arg:IsA("BasePart") then
										primaryPart = arg
									elseif arg:IsA("Model") then
										primaryPart = api.FindNicePart(arg) or arg.PrimaryPart or arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
									end
								end

								if not primaryPart or not primaryPart.Parent then
									return
								end
								local assemblyRootPart = primaryPart.AssemblyRootPart or primaryPart
								if charRegistry.CharacterRoots[assemblyRootPart] or assemblyRootPart ~= arg and not assemblyRootPart:IsDescendantOf(arg) then
									return
								end

								if value22 and value22 ~= assemblyRootPart then
									charRegistry.ClearMovers(arg)
								end

								sharedState.ToyAuraRoots[arg] = assemblyRootPart
								return assemblyRootPart
							end

							charRegistry.SetCollisions = function(arg, canCollide)
								local value22 = charRegistry.Movers[arg]
								if not value22 then
									return
								end

								if canCollide == nil then
									for k, value23 in value22.Parts, nil, nil do
										if k.Parent then
											k.CanCollide = value23
										end
									end

									table.clear(value22.Parts)
									return
								end

								for _, value23 in arg:QueryDescendants("BasePart") do
									if value22.Parts[value23] == nil then
										value22.Parts[value23] = value23.CanCollide
									end

									value23.CanCollide = canCollide
								end

								if arg:IsA("BasePart") then
									if value22.Parts[arg] == nil then
										value22.Parts[arg] = arg.CanCollide
									end

									arg.CanCollide = canCollide
								end
							end

							charRegistry.ClearMovers = function(arg)
								if not arg then
									for k in charRegistry.Movers, nil, nil do
										charRegistry.ClearMovers(k)
									end

									return
								end

								local value22 = charRegistry.Movers[arg]
								if not value22 then
									return
								end
								charRegistry.SetCollisions(arg, nil)
								value22.Position:Destroy()
								value22.Orientation:Destroy()
								value22.Attachment:Destroy()
								charRegistry.Movers[arg] = nil
							end

							api.ClearObjectAura = function(arg)
								if arg then
									charRegistry.ClearMovers(arg)
								end
							end

							charRegistry.EnsureMovers = function(arg, arg2)
								if sharedState.ObjectStates[arg] then
									return
								end
								local value22 = arg2 or charRegistry.GetRoot(arg)
								if not value22 or value22.Anchored then
									return
								end
								local table17 = charRegistry.Movers[arg]
								local state12

								if table17 then
									state12 = table17.Root ~= value22 or table17.Attachment.Parent ~= value22 or table17.Position.Parent ~= value22 or table17.Orientation.Parent ~= value22
								else
									state12 = table17
								end

								if state12 then
									charRegistry.ClearMovers(arg)
									table17 = nil
								end

								if not table17 then
									local auraAttachment = value22:FindFirstChild("AuraAttachment") or Instance.new("Attachment")
									auraAttachment.Name = "AuraAttachment"
									auraAttachment.Parent = value22
									local auraAlignPos = value22:FindFirstChild("AuraAlignPos") or Instance.new("AlignPosition")
									auraAlignPos.Name = "AuraAlignPos"
									auraAlignPos.Attachment0 = auraAttachment
									auraAlignPos.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
									auraAlignPos.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = true
									auraAlignPos.MaxForce = math.huge
									auraAlignPos.MaxVelocity = math.huge
									auraAlignPos.RigidityEnabled = false
									auraAlignPos.Parent = value22
									local auraAlignOri = value22:FindFirstChild("AuraAlignOri") or Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
									auraAlignOri.Name = "AuraAlignOri"
									auraAlignOri.Attachment0 = auraAttachment
									auraAlignOri.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
									auraAlignOri.MaxTorque = math.huge
									auraAlignOri.MaxAngularVelocity = math.huge
									auraAlignOri.RigidityEnabled = false
									auraAlignOri.Parent = value22
									table17 = { Root = value22, Attachment = auraAttachment, Position = auraAlignPos, Orientation = auraAlignOri, Parts = {} }
									charRegistry.Movers[arg] = table17
								end

								local responsiveness = toysAuraSettings.Responsiveness or 55

								if table17.Responsiveness ~= responsiveness then
									table17.Position.Responsiveness = responsiveness
									table17.Orientation.Responsiveness = math.max(responsiveness - 10, 10)
									table17.Responsiveness = responsiveness
								end

								if table17.Collisions ~= toysAuraSettings.Collisions then
									charRegistry.SetCollisions(arg, toysAuraSettings.Collisions)
									table17.Collisions = toysAuraSettings.Collisions
								end

								return table17.Position, table17.Orientation, value22, table17
							end

							charRegistry.UpdatePhysics = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
								local value22, value23, value24, value25 = charRegistry.EnsureMovers(arg, arg2)
								if not value22 then
									return
								end
								local position = arg3.Position

								if value25.TargetPosition ~= position then
									value22.Position = position
									value25.TargetPosition = position
								end

								arg5 = arg5 and arg5 - position
								local cframe

								if arg4 and arg5 and arg5:Dot(arg5) > 0.0025 then
									cframe = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, arg5)
								else
									cframe = arg3.Rotation
								end

								if value25.TargetOrientation ~= cframe then
									value23.CFrame = cframe
									value25.TargetOrientation = cframe
								end
							end

							charRegistry.GetPath = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
								local table17 = {}
								local table18 = {}
								local num37

								if arg3 then
									num37 = #arg
								else
									num37 = #arg - 1
								end

								local num38 = 0

								for i = 1, num37 do
									local magnitude = (arg[i % #arg + 1] - arg[i]).Magnitude
									table18[i] = magnitude
									num38 += magnitude
								end

								if num38 == 0 then
									return table.create(arg2, arg[1] or Vector3.zero)
								end

								for i = 1, arg2 do
									local num39 = i - 1
									local num40

									if arg3 then
										num40 = arg2
									else
										num40 = math.max(arg2 - 1, 1)
									end

									local num41 = num39 / num40 * num38
									local num42 = 1

									while num42 < num37 and num41 > table18[num42] do
										num41 -= table18[num42]
										num42 += 1
									end

									local num43

									if table18[num42] > 0 then
										num43 = math.clamp(num41 / table18[num42], 0, 1)
									else
										num43 = 0
									end

									table17[i] = arg[num42]:Lerp(arg[num42 % #arg + 1], num43)
								end

								return table17
							end

							charRegistry.AddShape = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
								if not charRegistry.Shapes[arg] then
									table.insert(charRegistry.ShapeNames, arg)
								end

								charRegistry.Shapes[arg] = { Generate = arg2, Closed = arg3 }
								table.clear(charRegistry.ShapeCache)
							end

							charRegistry.GetShape = function(count)
								local circle = charRegistry.Shapes[toysAuraSettings.Shape] or charRegistry.Shapes.Circle
								local rows = math.clamp(math.floor(toysAuraSettings.Rows or 1), 1, math.max(count, 1))
								local rotations = math.max(toysAuraSettings.Rotations or 1, 0)
								local shapeCache = charRegistry.ShapeCache
								if shapeCache.Shape == circle and shapeCache.Count == count and shapeCache.Rows == rows and shapeCache.Rotations == rotations then
									return shapeCache.Points
								end
								local value22

								if type(circle.Generate) == "table" then
									value22 = charRegistry.GetPath(circle.Generate, count, circle.Closed)
								else
									local num37

									if circle.Closed ~= nil then
										num37 = math.max(192, count * 4)
									else
										num37 = count
									end

									local value23 = table.create(num37)

									for i = 1, num37 do
										value23[i] = circle.Generate(i, num37, rows, rotations)
									end

									if circle.Closed == nil then
										value22 = value23
									else
										value22 = charRegistry.GetPath(value23, count, circle.Closed)
									end
								end

								shapeCache.Shape = circle
								shapeCache.Count = count
								shapeCache.Rows = rows
								shapeCache.Rotations = rotations
								shapeCache.Points = value22
								return value22
							end

							charRegistry.GetRow = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
								local num37 = math.floor(arg2 / arg3)
								local num38 = arg2 % arg3
								local num39 = (num37 + 1) * num38
								if arg <= num39 then
									return math.floor((arg - 1) / (num37 + 1)), (arg - 1) % (num37 + 1), num37 + 1
								end
								local num40 = arg - num39 - 1
								return num38 + math.floor(num40 / num37), num40 % num37, num37
							end

							charRegistry.GetOffset = function(arg)
								local num37 = (toysAuraSettings.Radius or 20) * (toysAuraSettings.Spacing or 1)
								return Vector3.new(arg.X * num37, arg.Y * (toysAuraSettings.Height or toysAuraSettings.Radius or 20), arg.Z * num37)
							end

							charRegistry.GetCenter = function(arg)
								local center = toysAuraSettings.Center or CFrame.new(0, 50, 0)

								if toysAuraSettings.PosType == "You" and arg then
									center = arg.CFrame * toysAuraSettings.Offset
								elseif toysAuraSettings.PosType == "Players" and configValues.SelectedTAPlayer then
									local value22, value23 = api.Char(configValues.SelectedTAPlayer)

									if value23 then
										center = value23.CFrame * toysAuraSettings.Offset
									end
								elseif toysAuraSettings.PosType == "Custom Pos" and toysAuraSettings.CustomPos then
									center = toysAuraSettings.CustomPos * toysAuraSettings.Offset
								end

								local rad = math.rad
								local z = toysAuraSettings.Rotation.Z
								return center * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(toysAuraSettings.Rotation.X), math.rad(toysAuraSettings.Rotation.Y), rad(z))
							end

							charRegistry.AddModel = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
								if unloaded or not arg or not arg.Parent or not arg:IsA("Model") or charRegistry.Models[arg] then
									return
								end

								if sharedState.ObjectStates[arg] then
									return
								end

								if toysAuraSettings.OwnInventoryOnly and not arg:IsDescendantOf(toysFolder) then
									return
								end

								if toysAuraSettings.LimitEnabled and #toysAuraSettings.Models >= toysAuraSettings.Limit then
									return
								end

								if not arg4 then
									charRegistry.UpdateCharacterRoots()
									arg4 = charRegistry.GetRoot(arg)
								end

								if not arg4 then
									return
								end

								if arg2 and not api.IsPartOwner(arg4) then
									if not api.SNO(arg4, arg3 or 0.35) then
										return
									end
									charRegistry.UpdateCharacterRoots()
									if charRegistry.GetRoot(arg) ~= arg4 then
										return
									end
								end

								if unloaded or not arg.Parent or charRegistry.Models[arg] or sharedState.ObjectStates[arg] then
									return
								end

								if toysAuraSettings.LimitEnabled and #toysAuraSettings.Models >= toysAuraSettings.Limit then
									return
								end
								table.insert(toysAuraSettings.Models, arg)
								charRegistry.Models[arg] = true
								return true
							end

							charRegistry.RemoveModel = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
								local value22 = arg3 or table.find(toysAuraSettings.Models, arg)

								if value22 then
									table.remove(toysAuraSettings.Models, value22)
								end

								charRegistry.Models[arg] = nil
								charRegistry.ClearMovers(arg)

								if arg2 then
									local value23 = charRegistry.GetRoot(arg)

									if value23 then
										task.delay(0.1, function()
											if value23.Parent and not charRegistry.Models[arg] and not sharedState.ObjectStates[arg] then
												api.RNO(value23)
											end
										end)
									end
								end

								sharedState.ToyAuraRoots[arg] = nil
							end

							charRegistry.TrimModels = function()
								while toysAuraSettings.LimitEnabled and #toysAuraSettings.Models > toysAuraSettings.Limit do
									charRegistry.RemoveModel(toysAuraSettings.Models[#toysAuraSettings.Models], true)
								end
							end

							charRegistry.ClearModels = function(arg)
								for i = #toysAuraSettings.Models, 1, -1 do
									charRegistry.RemoveModel(toysAuraSettings.Models[i], arg, i)
								end

								charRegistry.ClearMovers()
							end

							charRegistry.AddShape("Line", function(arg, arg2)
								local num37

								if arg2 == 1 then
									num37 = 0.5
								else
									num37 = (arg - 1) / (arg2 - 1)
								end

								return Vector3.new(-1 + 2 * num37, 0, 0)
							end)

							charRegistry.AddShape("Circle", function(arg, arg2)
								local num37 = (arg - 1) / arg2 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
								local sin = math.sin
								return Vector3.new(math.cos(num37), 0, sin(num37))
							end)

							for _, value22 in {
								{ "Triangle", 3 },
								{ "Square", 4 },
								{ "Pentagon", 5 },
								{ "Hexagon", 6 },
								{ "Octagon", 8 },
							}, nil, nil do
								local table17 = {}

								for i = 1, value22[2] do
									local num37 = (i - 1) / value22[2] * 3.1415926535897931 * 2 - 1.5707963267948966
									local sin = math.sin
									table17[i] = Vector3.new(math.cos(num37), 0, sin(num37))
								end

								charRegistry.AddShape(value22[1], table17, true)
							end

							charRegistry.AddShape("Diamond", {
								Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),
								Vector3.new(1, 0, 0),
								Vector3.new(0, 0, 1),
								Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0),
							}, true)

							do
								local table17 = {}

								for i = 1, 10 do
									local num37

									if i % 2 == 1 then
										num37 = 1
									else
										num37 = 0.42
									end

									local num38 = (i - 1) / 10 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2 - 1.5707963267948966
									table17[i] = Vector3.new(math.cos(num38) * num37, 0, math.sin(num38) * num37)
								end

								charRegistry.AddShape("Star", table17, true)
							end
						end

						charRegistry.AddShape("Heart", function(arg, arg2)
							local num37 = (arg - 1) / arg2 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
							return Vector3.new(16 * math.sin(num37) ^ 3 / 17, 0, -(13 * math.cos(num37) - 5 * math.cos(2 * num37) - 2 * math.cos(3 * num37) - math.cos(4 * num37)) / 17)
						end, true)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Penis", function(arg, arg2)
							local num37 = (arg - 1) / math.max(arg2 - 1, 1)
							if num37 < 0.22 then
								local num38 = num37 / 0.22 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
								return Vector3.new(-0.38 + math.cos(num38) * 0.34, -0.86 + math.sin(num38) * 0.18, math.sin(num38) * 0.28)
							end

							if num37 < 0.44 then
								local num38 = (num37 - 0.22) / 0.22 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
								return Vector3.new(0.38 + math.cos(num38) * 0.34, -0.86 + math.sin(num38) * 0.18, math.sin(num38) * 0.28)
							end

							if num37 < 0.8 then
								local num38 = (num37 - 0.44) / 0.36
								local num39 = num38 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2 * 3
								local num40 = 0.34 - num38 * 0.07 + math.sin(num38 * 3.1415926535897931) * 0.04
								return Vector3.new(math.cos(num39) * num40, -0.48 + num38 * 1.28, math.sin(num39) * num40)
							end

							local num38 = (num37 - 0.8) / 0.2
							local num39 = num38 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
							local num40 = 0.26 + math.sin(num38 * 3.1415926535897931) * 0.16
							return Vector3.new(math.cos(num39) * num40, 0.78 + num38 * 0.34, math.sin(num39) * num40)
						end)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Flower", function(arg, arg2)
							local num37 = (arg - 1) / arg2 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
							local num38 = 0.62 + math.cos(num37 * 6) * 0.32
							return Vector3.new(math.cos(num37) * num38, 0, math.sin(num37) * num38)
						end, true)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Clover", function(arg, arg2)
							local num37 = (arg - 1) / arg2 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
							local num38 = 0.35 + math.abs(math.sin(num37 * 2)) * 0.65
							return Vector3.new(math.cos(num37) * num38, 0, math.sin(num37) * num38)
						end, true)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Infinity", function(arg, arg2)
							local num37 = (arg - 1) / arg2 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
							return Vector3.new(math.sin(num37), 0, math.sin(num37) * math.cos(num37))
						end, true)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Spiral", function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
							local num37 = (arg - 1) / math.max(arg2 - 1, 1)
							local num38 = num37 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2 * arg4
							return Vector3.new(math.cos(num38) * num37, 0, math.sin(num38) * num37)
						end, false)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Wave", function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
							local num37 = (arg - 1) / math.max(arg2 - 1, 1)
							return Vector3.new(-1 + 2 * num37, math.sin(num37 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2 * arg4) * 0.5, 0)
						end, false)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Cross", {
							Vector3.new(0.24, 0, -1),
							Vector3.new(0.24, 0, -0.24),
							Vector3.new(1, 0, -0.24),
							Vector3.new(1, 0, 0.24),
							Vector3.new(0.24, 0, 0.24),
							Vector3.new(0.24, 0, 1),
							Vector3.new(-0.24, 0, 1),
							Vector3.new(-0.24, 0, 0.24),
							Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0.24),
							Vector3.new(-1, 0, -0.24),
							Vector3.new(-0.24, 0, -0.24),
							Vector3.new(-0.24, 0, -1),
						}, true)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Arrow", {
							Vector3.new(-1, 0, -0.25),
							Vector3.new(0.2, 0, -0.25),
							Vector3.new(0.2, 0, -0.65),
							Vector3.new(1, 0, 0),
							Vector3.new(0.2, 0, 0.65),
							Vector3.new(0.2, 0, 0.25),
							Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0.25),
						}, true)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Crown", function(arg, arg2)
							local num37 = (arg - 1) / arg2 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
							local vector3 = Vector3.new
							local num38 = 0.15 + (0.5 + 0.5 * math.cos(num37 * 5)) ^ 1.5 * 0.75
							local sin = math.sin
							return vector3(math.cos(num37), num38, sin(num37))
						end, true)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Sphere", function(arg, arg2)
							local num37 = 1 - 2 * (arg - 0.5) / arg2
							local value22 = math.sqrt(math.max(0, 1 - num37 * num37))
							local num38 = 2.3999632297286531 * arg
							return Vector3.new(math.cos(num38) * value22, num37, math.sin(num38) * value22)
						end)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Dome", function(arg, arg2)
							local num37 = (arg - 0.5) / arg2
							local value22 = math.sqrt(1 - num37 * num37)
							local num38 = 2.3999632297286531 * arg
							return Vector3.new(math.cos(num38) * value22, num37, math.sin(num38) * value22)
						end)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Box", {
							Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1),
							Vector3.new(1, -1, -1),
							Vector3.new(1, -1, 1),
							Vector3.new(-1, -1, 1),
							Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1),
							Vector3.new(-1, 1, -1),
							Vector3.new(1, 1, -1),
							Vector3.new(1, -1, -1),
							Vector3.new(1, 1, -1),
							Vector3.one,
							Vector3.new(1, -1, 1),
							Vector3.one,
							Vector3.new(-1, 1, 1),
							Vector3.new(-1, -1, 1),
							Vector3.new(-1, 1, 1),
							Vector3.new(-1, 1, -1),
						}, false)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Pyramid", {
							Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1),
							Vector3.new(1, -1, -1),
							Vector3.new(1, -1, 1),
							Vector3.new(-1, -1, 1),
							Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1),
							Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
							Vector3.new(1, -1, -1),
							Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
							Vector3.new(1, -1, 1),
							Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
							Vector3.new(-1, -1, 1),
						}, false)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Cone", function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
							local num37 = (arg - 1) / math.max(arg2 - 1, 1)
							local num38 = num37 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2 * arg4
							local num39 = 1 - num37
							return Vector3.new(math.cos(num38) * num39, -1 + 2 * num37, math.sin(num38) * num39)
						end, false)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Cylinder", function(arg, arg2, arg3)
							local num37 = math.min(arg3, math.max(1, math.floor(arg2 / 3)))
							local value22, value23, value24 = charRegistry.GetRow(arg, arg2, num37)
							local num38

							if num37 == 1 then
								num38 = 0
							else
								num38 = -1 + 2 * value22 / (num37 - 1)
							end

							local num39 = value23 / value24 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
							local sin = math.sin
							return Vector3.new(math.cos(num39), num38, sin(num39))
						end)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Helix", function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
							local num37 = (arg - 1) / math.max(arg2 - 1, 1)
							local num38 = num37 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2 * arg4
							local num39 = -1 + 2 * num37
							local sin = math.sin
							return Vector3.new(math.cos(num38), num39, sin(num38))
						end)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Double Helix", function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
							local num37 = math.ceil(arg2 / 2)
							local state12 = arg > num37

							if state12 then
								arg -= num37
							end

							if state12 then
								num37 = arg2 - num37
							end

							local num38 = (arg - 1) / math.max(num37 - 1, 1)
							local num39 = num38 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2 * arg4
							local num40

							if state12 then
								num40 = 3.1415926535897931
							else
								num40 = 0
							end

							local num41 = num39 + num40
							local num42 = -1 + 2 * num38
							local sin = math.sin
							return Vector3.new(math.cos(num41), num42, sin(num41))
						end)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Torus", function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
							local num37 = (arg - 1) / arg2 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
							local num38 = num37 * math.max(1, math.floor(arg4))
							local num39 = 0.72 + 0.28 * math.cos(num38)
							return Vector3.new(num39 * math.cos(num37), 0.28 * math.sin(num38), num39 * math.sin(num37))
						end, true)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Galaxy", function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
							local num37 = math.min(arg2, math.clamp(arg3, 2, 8))
							local num38 = (arg - 1) % num37
							local value22 = math.sqrt((math.floor((arg - 1) / num37) + 0.5) / (math.floor((arg2 - num38 - 1) / num37) + 1))
							local num39 = value22 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2 * arg4 + num38 / num37 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
							return Vector3.new(math.cos(num39) * value22, math.sin(value22 * 3.1415926535897931 * 4 + num38) * 0.12, math.sin(num39) * value22)
						end)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Grid", function(arg, arg2, arg3)
							local value22, value23, value24 = charRegistry.GetRow(arg, arg2, arg3)
							local num37

							if value24 == 1 then
								num37 = 0
							else
								num37 = -1 + 2 * value23 / (value24 - 1)
							end

							local num38

							if arg3 == 1 then
								num38 = 0
							else
								num38 = -1 + 2 * value22 / (arg3 - 1)
							end

							return Vector3.new(num37, 0, num38)
						end)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Wall", function(arg, arg2, arg3)
							local value22 = charRegistry.Shapes.Grid.Generate(arg, arg2, arg3)
							return Vector3.new(value22.X, value22.Z, 0)
						end)

						charRegistry.AddShape("Ring Stack", function(arg, arg2, arg3)
							local num37 = math.min(arg3, math.max(1, math.floor(arg2 / 3)))
							local value22, value23, value24 = charRegistry.GetRow(arg, arg2, num37)
							local num38

							if num37 == 1 then
								num38 = 0
							else
								num38 = -1 + 2 * value22 / (num37 - 1)
							end

							local num39 = value23 / value24 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
							local num40 = 1 - math.abs(num38) * 0.18
							return Vector3.new(math.cos(num39) * num40, num38, math.sin(num39) * num40)
						end)

						do
							local table17 = { ShapePhase = 0, MovePhase = 0, BobPhase = 0, Valid = {}, Roots = {} }

							api.UpdateToysAura = function(arg)
								local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
								local state12 = not toysAuraSettings.Enabled or #toysAuraSettings.Models == 0
								local state13

								if state12 then
									state13 = state12
								else
									state13 = not (value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0)
								end

								if state13 then
									charRegistry.ClearMovers()
									table17.MovePhase = 0
									return
								end

								local valid = table17.Valid
								local roots = table17.Roots
								table.clear(valid)
								table.clear(roots)
								charRegistry.UpdateCharacterRoots()
								local num37 = 1

								while num37 <= #toysAuraSettings.Models do
									local value25 = toysAuraSettings.Models[num37]
									local parent2 = value25.Parent and charRegistry.GetRoot(value25)

									if sharedState.ObjectStates[value25] then
										charRegistry.ClearMovers(value25)
										num37 += 1
										continue
									end

									local state14 = parent2 and api.IsPartOwner(parent2)

									if parent2 and not state14 and api.SNO(parent2, 0.18) then
										charRegistry.UpdateCharacterRoots()
										state14 = charRegistry.GetRoot(value25) == parent2
									end

									local value26 = unloaded
									local state15

									if unloaded then
										state15 = value26
									else
										state15 = not toysAuraSettings.Enabled
									end

									if state15 then
										charRegistry.ClearMovers()
										return
									end

									if toysAuraSettings.Models[num37] == value25 then
										if sharedState.ObjectStates[value25] then
											charRegistry.ClearMovers(value25)
											num37 += 1
										elseif state14 then
											valid[#valid + 1] = value25
											roots[#roots + 1] = parent2
											num37 += 1
										else
											charRegistry.RemoveModel(value25, false, num37)
										end
									end
								end

								local num38 = #valid
								if num38 == 0 then
									return
								end

								if toysAuraSettings.AutoRotate then
									table17.ShapePhase = table17.ShapePhase + (toysAuraSettings.RotationSpeed or 0) * arg
									table17.ShapePhase = table17.ShapePhase % 6.2831853071795862
								end

								if toysAuraSettings.AutoMove then
									table17.MovePhase = table17.MovePhase + (toysAuraSettings.MoveSpeed or 1) * arg
									table17.MovePhase = table17.MovePhase % num38
								else
									table17.MovePhase = 0
								end

								if toysAuraSettings.Bob then
									table17.BobPhase = (table17.BobPhase + (toysAuraSettings.BobSpeed or 0) * arg) % 6.2831853071795862
								else
									table17.BobPhase = 0
								end

								local value25 = charRegistry.GetCenter(value23)

								if toysAuraSettings.Bob then
									value25 *= CFrame.new(0, math.sin(table17.BobPhase) * (toysAuraSettings.BobHeight or 0), 0)
								end

								local value26 = charRegistry.GetShape(num38)
								local num39 = #value26
								if num39 == 0 then
									return
								end
								local cframe = CFrame.identity

								if toysAuraSettings.AutoRotate then
									cframe = CFrame.Angles(0, table17.ShapePhase, 0)
								end

								for i = 1, num38 do
									local num40

									if toysAuraSettings.AutoMove and num39 > 1 then
										local num41 = i + table17.MovePhase
										local num42 = math.floor(num41)
										num40 = value26[(num42 - 1) % num39 + 1]:Lerp(value26[(num42 + 1 - 1) % num39 + 1], num41 - num42)
									else
										num40 = value26[(i - 1) % num39 + 1]
									end

									if toysAuraSettings.AutoRotate then
										num40 = cframe * num40
									end

									local faceCenter = toysAuraSettings.FaceCenter
									local position = value25.Position
									charRegistry.UpdatePhysics(valid[i], roots[i], value25 * CFrame.new(charRegistry.GetOffset(num40)), faceCenter, position)
								end
							end

							api.StepToysAura = function(arg)
								if table17.Updating then
									return
								end
								table17.Updating = true
								local ok, result = pcall(api.UpdateToysAura, arg)
								table17.Updating = false

								if not ok then
									warn("Toys Aura Update failed: " .. tostring(result))
								end
							end

							api.SetToysAuraConnection = function(arg)
								api.SetRuntimeConnection("ToysAura", RunService.Heartbeat, arg, api.StepToysAura)
								if arg then
									return
								end

								if not api.IsToyAuraEffectsEnabled() then
									charRegistry.ClearCharacterRoots()
								end

								charRegistry.ClearMovers()
								table17.MovePhase = 0
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			do
				api.CaptureACPose = function(arg)
					local table16 = { BodyOffset = configValues.AutoClickerOffset, Rotation = {}, LimbOffsets = {} }

					for k, value22 in sharedState.ACRotation, nil, nil do
						table16.Rotation[k] = value22
					end

					for k, value22 in sharedState.ACOffset, nil, nil do
						table16.LimbOffsets[k] = value22
					end

					if arg then
						sharedState.AutoClickerTargetPoses[arg.UserId] = table16
					end

					return table16
				end

				api.GetACPose = function(arg)
					if not featureState.IndependentACPoses or arg == configValues.SelectedACPoseTarget then
						return configValues.AutoClickerOffset, sharedState.ACRotation, sharedState.ACOffset
					end
					local value22 = sharedState.AutoClickerTargetPoses[arg.UserId] or api.CaptureACPose(arg)
					return value22.BodyOffset, value22.Rotation, value22.LimbOffsets
				end

				api.LoadACEditorPose = function(arg)
					if not arg then
						return
					end

					if not options.ACCSecXO or not options.ACRSecXOTorso then
						return
					end
					local bodyOffset = arg.BodyOffset

					if not bodyOffset and arg.BodyPos then
						bodyOffset = CFrame.new(tonumber(arg.BodyPos[1]) or 0, tonumber(arg.BodyPos[2]) or 0, tonumber(arg.BodyPos[3]) or -5)
					end

					if bodyOffset then
						local accSecXO = options.ACCSecXO
						local setValue = accSecXO.SetValue
						local value22 = table.pack(tonumber(string.format("%.1f", bodyOffset.X)))
						setValue(accSecXO, table.unpack(value22, 1, value22.n))
						local accSecYO = options.ACCSecYO
						local setValue2 = accSecYO.SetValue
						local value23 = table.pack(tonumber(string.format("%.1f", bodyOffset.Y)))
						setValue2(accSecYO, table.unpack(value23, 1, value23.n))
						local accSecZO = options.ACCSecZO
						local setValue3 = accSecZO.SetValue
						local value24 = table.pack(tonumber(string.format("%.1f", bodyOffset.Z)))
						setValue3(accSecZO, table.unpack(value24, 1, value24.n))
					end

					local rotation = arg.Rotation or arg

					for k in sharedState.ACRotation, nil, nil do
						local value22 = rotation[k]

						if value22 then
							local x

							if typeof(value22) == "Vector3" then
								x = value22.X
							else
								x = value22[1]
							end

							local y

							if typeof(value22) == "Vector3" then
								y = value22.Y
							else
								y = value22[2]
							end

							local z

							if typeof(value22) == "Vector3" then
								z = value22.Z
							else
								z = value22[3]
							end

							local value23 = options["ACRSecXO" .. k]
							local setValue = value23.SetValue
							local value24 = table.pack(tonumber(string.format("%.1f", tonumber(x) or 0)))
							setValue(value23, table.unpack(value24, 1, value24.n))
							local value25 = options["ACRSecYO" .. k]
							local setValue2 = value25.SetValue
							local value26 = table.pack(tonumber(string.format("%.1f", tonumber(y) or 0)))
							setValue2(value25, table.unpack(value26, 1, value26.n))
							local value27 = options["ACRSecZO" .. k]
							local setValue3 = value27.SetValue
							local value28 = table.pack(tonumber(string.format("%.1f", tonumber(z) or 0)))
							setValue3(value27, table.unpack(value28, 1, value28.n))
						end
					end

					if arg.LimbOffsets then
						for k in sharedState.ACOffset, nil, nil do
							local value22 = arg.LimbOffsets[k]

							if value22 then
								local x

								if typeof(value22) == "Vector3" then
									x = value22.X
								else
									x = value22[1]
								end

								local y

								if typeof(value22) == "Vector3" then
									y = value22.Y
								else
									y = value22[2]
								end

								local z

								if typeof(value22) == "Vector3" then
									z = value22.Z
								else
									z = value22[3]
								end

								local value23 = options["ACOSecXO" .. k]
								local setValue = value23.SetValue
								local value24 = table.pack(tonumber(string.format("%.1f", tonumber(x) or 0)))
								setValue(value23, table.unpack(value24, 1, value24.n))
								local value25 = options["ACOSecYO" .. k]
								local setValue2 = value25.SetValue
								local value26 = table.pack(tonumber(string.format("%.1f", tonumber(y) or 0)))
								setValue2(value25, table.unpack(value26, 1, value26.n))
								local value27 = options["ACOSecZO" .. k]
								local setValue3 = value27.SetValue
								local value28 = table.pack(tonumber(string.format("%.1f", tonumber(z) or 0)))
								setValue3(value27, table.unpack(value28, 1, value28.n))
							end
						end
					else
						for k in sharedState.ACOffset, nil, nil do
							options["ACOSecXO" .. k]:SetValue(options["ACOSecXO" .. k].Default)
							options["ACOSecYO" .. k]:SetValue(options["ACOSecYO" .. k].Default)
							options["ACOSecZO" .. k]:SetValue(options["ACOSecZO" .. k].Default)
						end
					end

					return true
				end

				api.BringPlayer = function(arg)
					local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char(arg)
					local value25, value26, value27 = api.Char()
					local state12 = arg == localPlayer or api.IsProtectedPlayer(arg)

					if not state12 then
						state12 = not (value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0 and value26 and value27 and value27.Health > 0)
					end

					if state12 then
						return
					end

					if not api.IsNetworkOwner(value23) and not api.SNO(value23) then
						return
					end
					local num36 = value26.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -5)
					local BasePart = value22:QueryDescendants("BasePart")
					local table16 = {}

					for i = 1, 3 do
						local state13 = unloaded

						if not unloaded then
							state13 = not (value23.Parent and value22.Parent)
						end

						if not (state13 or value24.Health <= 0) then
							api.PivotTo(value22, num36 * value23.CFrame:Inverse() * value22:GetPivot(), BasePart, table16)

							for _, value28 in BasePart, nil, nil do
								api.ResetVelocity(value28)
							end

							RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
							continue
						end

						break
					end

					for _, value28 in BasePart, nil, nil do
						if value28.Parent then
							api.ResetVelocity(value28)
						end
					end

					if value23.Parent then
						api.RNO(value23)
					end

					return value23.Parent ~= nil and value24.Health > 0
				end

				do
					local table16 = {}

					api.Kill = function(arg)
						local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char(arg)

						if value24 and value24.Health ~= 0 and value23 and api.GetMagnitude(workspace.SpawnLocation, value23) < 15000 and not table16[value22] then
							value24.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
							value24:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
							api.DestroyObject(value22, value23)
							table16[value22] = true

							task.delay(1, function()
								table16[value22] = nil
							end)
						end
					end
				end
			end

			api.Void = function(arg)
				local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char(arg)

				if value24 and value24.Health ~= 0 and value23 and api.GetMagnitude(workspace.SpawnLocation, value23) < 15000 and api.SNO(value23) then
					local cframe = CFrame.new(0, 9e9, 0)
					api.PivotTo(value22, cframe * value23.CFrame:Inverse() * value22:GetPivot())
					api.Teleport(value23, cframe)
					value23.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 9e9, 0)
					task.delay(0.35, api.RNO, value23)
				end
			end

			api.ResolveDesync = function(arg)
				local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char(arg)

				if arg == localPlayer or api.IsProtectedPlayer(arg) then
					return
				end

				if not (value22 and value23 and value24 and value24.Health ~= 0 and api.GetMagnitude(workspace.SpawnLocation, value23) < 15000) then
					return
				end

				if not api.IsPartOwner(value23) and not api.SNO(value23) then
					return false
				end

				api.ResetVelocity(value23)
				task.delay(0.35, api.RNO, value23)
				return value23.Parent ~= nil
			end

					local leftArm = value22:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")

						for _, value26 in pairs(leftArm:GetConnectedParts()) do
							if value26.Name == "HoldPart" and not value26:IsDescendantOf(toysFolder) then
								api.BreakHoldable(value26.Parent, 1, true)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

		api.StopControlCharacter = function(arg)
			local controlCharacter = arg or sharedState.States.ControlCharacter
			if not controlCharacter then
				featureState.ControllingSomeone = false
				return
			end

			if controlCharacter.Cleaned then
				return
			end
			controlCharacter.Active = false
			controlCharacter.Cleaned = true

			if controlCharacter.JumpConnection then
				controlCharacter.JumpConnection:Disconnect()
			end

			for k, value22 in controlCharacter.Parts, nil, nil do
				if k.Parent then
					k.CanCollide = value22.CanCollide
					k.CanQuery = value22.CanQuery
				end
			end

			if controlCharacter.Humanoid.Parent then
				controlCharacter.Humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
				controlCharacter.Humanoid.Jump = false
				controlCharacter.Humanoid.AutoRotate = controlCharacter.AutoRotate
			end

			if controlCharacter.Root.Parent then
				api.RNO(controlCharacter.Root)
			end

			local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()

			if value24 then
				currentCamera.CameraSubject = value24
			elseif controlCharacter.CameraSubject and controlCharacter.CameraSubject.Parent then
				currentCamera.CameraSubject = controlCharacter.CameraSubject
			end

			if controlCharacter.LocalRoot.Parent and controlCharacter.LocalHumanoid.Parent and controlCharacter.LocalHumanoid.Health > 0 then
				api.Teleport(controlCharacter.LocalRoot, controlCharacter.ReturnCFrame)
			end

			if sharedState.States.ControlCharacter == controlCharacter then
				sharedState.States.ControlCharacter = nil
				featureState.ControllingSomeone = false
			end
		end

		api.ControlCharacter = function(arg)
			if sharedState.States.ControlCharacter then
				api.StopControlCharacter()
				return
			end
			local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
			if not (value22 and value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0) then
				return
			end

			if arg and not arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") then
				arg = arg:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			end

			if not arg or arg == value22 then
				return
			end
			local humanoidRootPart = arg and arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local humanoid = arg and arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
			if not (humanoidRootPart and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0) then
				return
			end

			local controlCharacter = {
				Active = true,
				Cleaned = false,
				Character = arg,
				Root = humanoidRootPart,
				Humanoid = humanoid,
				LocalRoot = value23,
				LocalHumanoid = value24,
				ReturnCFrame = value23.CFrame,
				CameraSubject = currentCamera.CameraSubject,
				AutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate,
				Parts = {},
			}

			sharedState.States.ControlCharacter = controlCharacter
			featureState.ControllingSomeone = true
			if not api.SNO(humanoidRootPart) or not controlCharacter.Active or sharedState.States.ControlCharacter ~= controlCharacter then
				api.StopControlCharacter(controlCharacter)
				return
			end

			local function func21(arg2, arg3)
				for _, value25 in arg2:QueryDescendants("BasePart") do
					controlCharacter.Parts[value25] = { CanCollide = value25.CanCollide, CanQuery = value25.CanQuery }
					value25.CanCollide = false

					if arg3 then
						value25.CanQuery = false
					end
				end
			end

			func21(value22, false)
			func21(arg, true)
			humanoid.AutoRotate = false
			currentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid

			controlCharacter.JumpConnection = UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
				if controlCharacter.Active and humanoid.Parent then
					humanoid.Jump = true
				end
			end)

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				while true do
					if controlCharacter.Active and not unloaded and sharedState.States.ControlCharacter == controlCharacter and arg.Parent and humanoidRootPart.Parent and humanoid.Health > 0 and value22.Parent and value23.Parent and value24.Health > 0 then
						if not (not api.IsPartOwner(humanoidRootPart) and not api.SNO(humanoidRootPart, 0.2)) then
							currentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
							humanoid.Sit = false
							local lookVector = currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
							local vector = Vector3.new(lookVector.X, 0, lookVector.Z)

							if vector.Magnitude > 0.001 then
								humanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(humanoidRootPart.Position, humanoidRootPart.Position + vector.Unit)
							end

							value23.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -10, 0)
							local moveDirection = value24.MoveDirection
							local value25 = humanoid
							local move = value25.Move

							if moveDirection.Magnitude > 1 then
								moveDirection = moveDirection.Unit
							end

							move(value25, moveDirection, false)
							RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
							continue
						end
					end

					break
				end
			end)

			api.StopControlCharacter(controlCharacter)

			if not ok then
				warn(result)
			end
		end

			blobApi = {

				SetFrozen = function(freezeBlobman)

					api.SetRuntimeConnection("FreezeBlobman", RunService.Heartbeat, freezeBlobman, blobApi.UpdateFreeze)
