		blobApi.Sit = function()
			local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
			local state12 = value24 and value24.Health > 0 and value23

			if state12 then
				state12 = not (parent or value24.Sit and value24.SeatPart or featureState.GucciAntiGrab)
			end

			if not state12 then
				return
			end
			local creatureBlobman = toysFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
			local vehicleSeat = creatureBlobman and creatureBlobman:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
			local state13 = vehicleSeat and (vehicleSeat.Occupant == nil or vehicleSeat.Occupant == value24)

			if vehicleSeat and not state13 then
				api.DestroyToy(creatureBlobman)
			end

			if not (state13 and creatureBlobman) then
				creatureBlobman = api.SpawnToy("CreatureBlobman")
			end

			if not (state13 and vehicleSeat) then
				vehicleSeat = creatureBlobman and creatureBlobman:WaitForChild("VehicleSeat", 0.5)
			end

			if not vehicleSeat then
				api.DestroyToy(creatureBlobman)
				return
			end
			local cFrame2 = value23.CFrame
			local now3 = tick()

			while true do
				vehicleSeat:Sit(value24)
				task.wait()
				if not (parent or tick() - now3 > 1) then
					continue
				end
				break
			end

			if parent then
				api.Teleport(value23, cFrame2)
			end
		end

		blobApi.Grab = function(arg, arg2)
			if not parent then
				return
			end
			local value22 = blobApi.GetBlobmanComponents()
			if not (value22.CreatureGrab and value22.RightWeld) then
				return
			end
			local value23, value24, value25 = api.Char()
			if not (value24 and value25 and value25.Health > 0) or not parent then
				return
			end
			local value26, value27, value28 = api.Char(arg)
			local rootAttachment = value27 and value27:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment")
			if not (rootAttachment and value28 and value28.Health > 0) or not parent then
				return
			end
			local now3 = tick()

			if api.GetMagnitude(value24, value27) > 40 then
				local cFrame2 = value24.CFrame
				currentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable

				while true do
					local state12 = parent and value27 and value27.Parent and value25.Health > 0 and value28.Health > 0 and rootAttachment and value22.RightWeld.Attachment0 ~= rootAttachment

					if state12 then
						state12 = tick() - now3 < (arg2 or 0.7)
					end

					if state12 then
						api.Teleport(value24, api.PredictCFrame(value27) - Vector3.new(0, 15, 0))
						task.wait()
						value22.CreatureGrab:FireServer(nil, value27, value22.RightWeld)
						continue
					end

					break
				end

				api.Teleport(value24, cFrame2, CFrame.new(0, 5, 0))
				currentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			else
				value22.CreatureGrab:FireServer(nil, value27, value22.RightWeld)
				task.wait(0.1)
			end

			if value22.RightWeld and value22.RightWeld.Attachment0 == rootAttachment then
				return true
			end
		end

		blobApi.Release = function(arg, arg2)
			if not parent then
				return
			end
			local value22 = blobApi.GetBlobmanComponents()
			if not (value22.CreatureGrab and value22.CreatureRelease and value22.RightWeld) then
				return
			end
			local value23, value24, value25 = api.Char()
			if not (value24 and value25 and value25.Health > 0) or not parent then
				return
			end
			local value26, value27, value28 = api.Char(arg)
			if not (value27 and value28 and value28.Health > 0) or not parent then
				return
			end

			if api.IsNetworkOwner(value27) then
				return true
			end
			local now3 = tick()
			local state12 = api.GetMagnitude(value24, value27) >= 40
			if state12 and sharedState.States.Owning then
				return
			end
			value22.RightWeld.Enabled = false
			local cframe = CFrame.new()
			local cframe2 = CFrame.new()
			local connection = nil
			sharedState.States.Owning = true

			local function func21()
				cframe = value24.CFrame
				cframe2 = value24.CFrame

				RunService:BindToRenderStep("ClientVision", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
					value24.CFrame = cframe
				end)

				connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					cframe = value24.CFrame
					value24.CFrame = cframe2
				end)
			end

			local function func22()
				RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("ClientVision")

				if connection then
					connection:Disconnect()
					connection = nil
				end

				if value24 then
					value24.CFrame = cframe
				end
			end

			if api.GetMagnitude(workspace.SpawnLocation, value27) < 15000 then
				pcall(function()
					if state12 then
						func21()
					end

					while true do
						local state13 = parent and value27 and value27.Parent and value25.Health > 0 and value28.Health > 0 and not api.IsNetworkOwner(value27)

						if state13 then
							state13 = tick() - now3 < (arg2 or 0.7)
						end

						if state13 then
							local value29 = api.PredictCFrame(value27)
							value22.RightWeld.Enabled = false

							if state12 then
								cframe2 = value29 - Vector3.new(0, 15, 0)
							end

							task.wait(0.011111111111111112)
							value22.CreatureGrab:FireServer(nil, value27, value22.RightWeld)
							task.wait(0.011111111111111112)
							value22.CreatureRelease:FireServer(value22.RightWeld)
							continue
						end

						break
					end
				end)

				if state12 then
					pcall(func22)
				end
			end

			task.delay(0.1, function()
				value22.RightWeld.Attachment0 = nil
				value22.RightWeld.Enabled = true
			end)

			sharedState.States.Owning = false
			task.wait()
			if value27 and api.IsNetworkOwner(value27) then
				return true
			end
		end

		blobApi.KickServerCooldowns = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

		blobApi.KickTargets = function(arg, arg2)
			local value22 = blobApi.GetBlobmanComponents()
			local value23, value24, value25 = api.Char()
			if not (value22 and value22.CreatureGrab and value22.CreatureDrop and value22.RightWeld and value24 and value25 and value25.Health > 0) then
				return
			end
			local table16 = {}
			local table17 = {}
			value22.CreatureDrop:FireServer(value22.RightWeld)

			for _, value26 in ipairs(arg) do
				local state12

				if arg2 then
					state12 = os.clock() - (blobApi.KickServerCooldowns[value26] or -math.huge) < 7
				else
					state12 = arg2
				end

				if not state12 then
					local value27, value28, value29 = api.Char(value26)
					local state13 = value26 ~= localPlayer and not api.IsProtectedPlayer(value26, arg2) and value28 and value29 and value29.Health > 0

					if state13 then
						state13 = not (value28.AssemblyRootPart or value28).Massless
					end

					if state13 and not value29.SeatPart and blobApi.Release(value26) then
						table16[#table16 + 1] = { Character = value27, Root = value28 }

						if arg2 then
							blobApi.KickServerCooldowns[value26] = os.clock()
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if #table16 == 0 then
				return false
			end
			value22.RightWeld.Enabled = false

			for i, value26 in ipairs(table16) do
				local cFrame2 = value24.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, i / #table16 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2, 0) * CFrame.new(0, 5, -20)
				value26.CFrame = cFrame2
				api.Teleport(value26.Root, cFrame2)
				local value27, value28 = api.CreateAlign(value26.Root, true)
				local position = cFrame2.Position
				local cFrame3 = cFrame2 - cFrame2.Position
				value27.Position = position
				value28.CFrame = cFrame3
				local num36 = #table17 + 1
				local num37 = #table17 + 2
				table17[num36] = value27
				table17[num37] = value28
			end

			task.wait(0.35)

			for _, value26 in ipairs(table16) do
				if value26.Root.Parent then
					api.Grab(value26.Root)
					api.RNO(value26.Root)
					value22.CreatureGrab:FireServer(nil, value26.Root, value22.RightWeld)

					if featureState.DeleteShurInBlobK then
						task.spawn(api.RemoveSticky, value26.Character)
					end

					task.wait()
				end
			end

			value22.CreatureGrab:FireServer(nil, value24, value22.RightWeld)

			local function func21()
				if value22.RightWeld.Parent then
					local rightWeld = value22.RightWeld
					value22.RightWeld.Attachment0 = nil
					rightWeld.Enabled = true
				end

				for _, value26 in ipairs(table17) do
					if value26.Parent then
						value26:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end

			local num36 = os.clock() + 2

			while true do
				for _, value26 in ipairs(table16) do
					if value26.Root.Parent then
						value26.Root.CFrame = value26.CFrame
						api.ResetVelocity(value26.Root)
					end
				end

				RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				if not (num36 <= os.clock()) then
					continue
				end
				break
			end

			func21()
			return true
		end

		blobApi.Kick = function(arg)
			return blobApi.KickTargets({ arg })
		end

		blobApi.KickServer = function()
			return blobApi.KickTargets(api.GetTargets(true, sharedState.SelectedPlayersBlob), true)
		end

		blobApi.Bring = function(arg)
			if not parent then
				return
			end
			local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
			if not (value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0) then
				return
			end
			local value25, value26, value27 = api.Char(arg)
			if not (value26 and value27 and value27.Health > 0) then
				return
			end

			if blobApi.Release(arg) then
				return api.BringPlayer(arg)
			end
		end

		blobApi.Kill = function(arg)
			if not parent then
				return
			end
			local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
			if not (value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0) then
				return
			end
			local value25, value26, value27 = api.Char(arg)
			if not (value26 and value27 and value27.Health > 0) then
				return
			end

			if not (value27.Sit and value27.SeatPart) and blobApi.Release(arg) then
				value27.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
				value27:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
				task.wait()
				return true
			end

			if value27.Sit and value27.SeatPart and blobApi.Bring(arg) then
				api.Kill(arg)
				return true
			end
		end

		blobApi.RemoveGucci = function(arg)
			if not parent then
				return
			end
			local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
			if not (value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0) then
				return
			end
			local value25, value26, value27 = api.Char(arg)
			local state12 = value26 and value27 and value27.Health > 0

			if state12 then
				state12 = not (value27.Sit and value27.SeatPart)
			end

			if not state12 then
				return
			end

			if blobApi.Release(arg) then
				value27.Sit = true

				task.delay(0.1, function()
					value27.Sit = false
				end)
			end
		end

		blobApi.Lock = function(arg)
			if not parent then
				return
			end
			local value22 = blobApi.GetBlobmanComponents()
			if not value22.RightWeld then
				return
			end
			local value23, value24, value25 = api.Char()
			if not (value24 and value25 and value25.Health > 0) then
				return
			end
			local value26, value27, value28 = api.Char(arg)
			if not (value27 and value28 and value28.Health > 0) then
				return
			end

			if blobApi.Grab(arg) then
				value22.RightWeld.Attachment0 = nil
				task.wait(0.45)
				value25.Sit = false
				task.wait(0.45)
				value22.RightWeld.Attachment0 = value27.RootAttachment
			end
		end

		local followingHumanoids = {}

		blobApi.JumpFollow = function()
			for _, humanoid in followingHumanoids do
				if humanoid.Parent then
					humanoid.Jump = true
				end
			end
		end

		blobApi.Follow = function(arg)
			if not parent then
				return
			end
			local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
			if not (value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0) then
				return
			end
			local value25, value26, value27 = api.Char(arg)
			if not (value26 and value27 and value27.Health > 0) then
				return
			end

			if blobApi.Release(arg) then
				task.spawn(function()
					followingHumanoids[arg] = value27

					while value23.Parent and value24.Health > 0 and value27.Parent and value27.Health > 0 and api.IsNetworkOwner(value26) do
						if api.GetMagnitude(value26, value23) > 50 then
							api.Teleport(value26, value23, CFrame.new(0, 10, 0))
						end

						value27.WalkToPoint = value23.Position

						if toggles.BlobFollowLoopJump and toggles.BlobFollowLoopJump.Value then
							value27.Jump = true
						end

						local blobFollowWalkSpeed = configValues.BlobFollowWalkSpeed

						if blobFollowWalkSpeed and value27.WalkSpeed ~= blobFollowWalkSpeed then
							value27.WalkSpeed = blobFollowWalkSpeed
						end

						local blobFollowJumpPower = configValues.BlobFollowJumpPower

						if blobFollowJumpPower and value27.JumpPower ~= blobFollowJumpPower then
							value27.JumpPower = blobFollowJumpPower
						end

						local blobFollowHipHeight = configValues.BlobFollowHipHeight

						if blobFollowHipHeight and blobFollowHipHeight > 0 and value27.HipHeight ~= blobFollowHipHeight then
							value27.HipHeight = blobFollowHipHeight
						end

						task.wait()
					end

					followingHumanoids[arg] = nil
				end)
			end
		end

		blobApi.LoopKick = function(arg)
			if not parent then
				return
			end
			local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
			if not (value23 and value24 and value24.SeatPart) then
				return
			end
			local value25, value26, value27 = api.Char(arg)
			local rootAttachment = value26 and value26:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment")
			local state12 = value26 and value27 and value27.Health > 0

			if state12 then
				state12 = not (value26.AssemblyRootPart or value26).Massless
			end

			if not (state12 and rootAttachment) then
				return
			end
			local firePlayerPart = value26:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart")
			local weldConstraint = firePlayerPart and firePlayerPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")
			if not (firePlayerPart and weldConstraint and weldConstraint.Enabled) then
				return
			end
			local value28 = blobApi.GetBlobmanComponents()
			if not (value28.CreatureGrab and value28.CreatureDrop and value28.CreatureRelease and value28.RightWeld and value28.RightOri) then
				return
			end
			value28.RightWeld.Enabled = false
			value28.RightOri.Enabled = false

			if api.SNO(value26) or blobApi.Release(arg) then
				value27.Sit = true

				task.delay(0.5, function()
					value27.Sit = false
				end)

				local num36 = value23.CFrame * CFrame.new(options.BlobKickX.Value, options.BlobKickY.Value, options.BlobKickZ.Value)
				api.Teleport(value26, num36)
				local value29, value30 = api.CreateAlign(value26, true)
				value29.Position = num36.Position

				task.delay(0.2, function()
					api.Grab(value26)
					api.RNO(value26)
				end)

				value28.CreatureDrop:FireServer(value28.RightWeld)
				value28.CreatureDrop:FireServer(value28.LeftWeld)
				task.wait(0.06)
				rootAttachment.Parent = value25

				while arg.Parent and value26.Parent and value27.Parent and value27.Health > 0 and parent and parent.Parent and sharedState.SelectedPlayersBlob[arg] and featureState.LoopApplyMethod and configValues.BlobMethod == "Kick" and api.GetMagnitude(value23, value26) <= 41 and not value27.SeatPart and value24.Sit do
					task.spawn(function()
						value27.RequiresNeck = false
						api.ToggleMotor6D(value25, false, true)
						api.RepositionLimbs(value25)
					end)

					task.spawn(api.ToggleCollisions, value25, false)
					value28.RightOri.Enabled = false
					value28.RightWeld.Enabled = false
					value28.RightOri.Attachment0 = nil
					value28.RightWeld.Attachment0 = nil
					value29.Position = (value23.CFrame * CFrame.new(options.BlobKickX.Value, options.BlobKickY.Value, options.BlobKickZ.Value)).Position
					api.ResetVelocity(value26)

					if featureState.DeleteShurInBlobK then
						task.spawn(api.RemoveSticky, value25)
					end

					if configValues.BKMethod == 1 then
						value28.CreatureGrab:FireServer(nil, value26, value28.RightWeld)
						task.wait()
						value28.CreatureDrop:FireServer(value28.RightWeld)
						task.wait()

						if featureState.NetworkOwnerInKick then
							api.Grab(value26)
							api.RNO(value26)
						end
					else
						value28.CreatureGrab:FireServer(nil, value26, value28.RightWeld)
						task.wait()
						value28.CreatureRelease:FireServer(value28.RightWeld)
						api.Grab(value26)
						task.spawn(api.Ragdoll, value25)

						if weldConstraint.Enabled then
							api.RNO(value26)
						end

						task.wait()
					end
				end

				rootAttachment.Parent = value26
				task.spawn(api.ToggleMotor6D, value25, true)
				task.spawn(api.ToggleCollisions, value25, true)
				value30:Destroy()
				value29:Destroy()
			end
		end

		blobApi.LoopLock = function(arg, arg2)
			if not parent then
				return
			end
			local value22, value23 = api.Char()
			local value24, value25, value26 = api.Char(arg)
			if not value26 or value26.Health == 0 or not value25 then
				return false
			end
			local blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript = parent.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript
			local creatureGrab = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript.CreatureGrab
			local creatureRelease = blobmanSeatAndOwnerScript.CreatureRelease
			local leftWeld = parent.LeftDetector.LeftWeld

			if (not value25.Massless or value26.Sit and value26.SeatPart) and blobApi.Release(arg) then
				api.Teleport(value25, value23, CFrame.new(0, 0, -5))
				local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
				bodyPosition.Name = "BlobLockBP"
				bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
				bodyPosition.D = 250
				bodyPosition.Position = value23.Position + Vector3.new(options.BlobKickX.Value, options.BlobKickY.Value, options.BlobKickZ.Value)
				bodyPosition.Parent = value25
				api.ResetVelocity(value25)
				leftWeld.Enabled = false

				while arg.Parent and value25.Parent and value26.Health ~= 0 and featureState[arg2] and parent and configValues.BlobMethod == "Lock" and api.GetMagnitude(value23, value25) < 45 do
					bodyPosition.Position = (value23.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -5)).Position
					api.ResetVelocity(value25)
					creatureGrab:FireServer(nil, value25, leftWeld)
					task.wait()
					creatureRelease:FireServer(leftWeld)
					task.wait()
				end

				bodyPosition:Destroy()

				if leftWeld then
					leftWeld.Enabled = true
				end
			end
		end

		api.NormalizeServerHopServer = function(arg)
			if typeof(arg) ~= "table" then
				return
			end
			local id = arg.Id or arg.id
			local num = tonumber(arg.Playing or arg.playing)
			local num2 = tonumber(arg.MaxPlayers or arg.maxPlayers)
			if type(id) ~= "string" or not num or not num2 or num2 <= 0 then
				return
			end

			return {
				Id = id,
				Playing = math.max(0, num),
				MaxPlayers = num2,
				Ping = tonumber(arg.Ping or arg.ping),
				FPS = tonumber(arg.FPS or arg.fps),
			}
		end

		api.IsServerHopCandidate = function(arg, arg2)
			if arg.Id == jobId or sharedState.ServerHop.Rejected[arg.Id] then
				return false
			end

			if arg2 and not arg2[arg.Id] then
				return false
			end
			local num36 = math.min(configValues.ServerHopMinPlayers, configValues.ServerHopMaxPlayers)
			local num37 = math.max(configValues.ServerHopMinPlayers, configValues.ServerHopMaxPlayers)
			if arg.Playing < num36 or arg.Playing > num37 then
				return false
			end

			if not featureState.ServerHopIncludeFull and arg.Playing >= arg.MaxPlayers then
				return false
			end
			return true
		end

		api.LoadServerHopCache = function()
			if not readfile then
				return {}, nil, false, {}
			end

			if isfile and not isfile(sharedState.ServerHop.CachePath) then
				return {}, nil, false, {}
			end

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(sharedState.ServerHop.CachePath))
			end)

			if not ok or typeof(result) ~= "table" or result.PlaceId ~= placeId or result.Schema ~= 4 then
				return {}, nil, false, {}
			end
			local table16 = {}
			local table17 = {}
			local value22 = ipairs
			local servers = result.Servers or {}

			for _, server in value22(servers) do
				local value23 = api.NormalizeServerHopServer(server)

				if value23 and value23.Id ~= jobId then
					table16[#table16 + 1] = value23
				end
			end

			local value23 = pairs
			local regions = result.Regions or {}

			for k, region in value23(regions) do
				if type(k) == "string" and typeof(region) == "table" then
					table17[k] = {}

					for k2, value24 in pairs(region) do
						if type(k2) == "string" and value24 == true then
							table17[k][k2] = true
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local num36 = tonumber(result.CachedAt) or 0
			local num37 = os.time() - num36
			return table16, num36, num37 >= 0 and num37 <= 120, table17
		end

		api.SaveServerHopCache = function(arg, arg2)
			sharedState.ServerHop.LastCacheError = nil
			if not writefile then
				sharedState.ServerHop.LastCacheError = "writefile is unavailable"
				return false
			end

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				writefile(sharedState.ServerHop.CachePath, HttpService:JSONEncode({
					Schema = 4,
					PlaceId = placeId,
					CachedAt = arg2 or os.time(),
					Servers = arg,
					Regions = sharedState.ServerHop.CachedRegions,
				}))
			end)

			if not ok then
				sharedState.ServerHop.LastCacheError = tostring(result)
			end

			return ok
		end

		api.FetchServerHopPage = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
			local text5 = arg3 or "Roblox"
			local ok, result = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, arg, true)
			if not ok then
				return nil, tostring(result)
			end

			if type(result) ~= "string" or result == "" then
				return nil, text5 .. " returned an empty response"
			end
			local ok2, result2 = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, result)
			if not ok2 or typeof(result2) ~= "table" then
				return nil, text5 .. " returned an invalid JSON response"
			end

			if arg2 and typeof(result2[arg2]) ~= "table" then
				local errors = result2.errors and result2.errors[1]
				local text6

				if errors then
					text6 = tostring(errors.message)
				else
					text6 = text5 .. " returned no server data"
				end

				return nil, text6
			end

			return result2
		end

		api.GetServerHopContinent = function(arg)
			if type(arg) ~= "string" then
				return
			end
			local text5 = " " .. arg:upper() .. " "

			for _, regionName in ipairs(sharedState.ServerHop.RegionNames) do
				local value22 = sharedState.ServerHop.ContinentCountries[regionName]
				if value22 and value22:find(text5, 1, true) then
					return regionName
				end
			end
		end

		api.RefreshServerHopRegions = function()
			local list, value22 = api.FetchServerHopPage("https://apis.rovalra.com/v1/datacenters/list", nil, "RoValra")
			if not list then
				return false, value22
			end
			local serverHop = sharedState.ServerHop
			local regionNames = sharedState.ServerHop.RegionNames
			local table16 = {}
			local regions = {}

			for i = 2, #regionNames do
				regions[regionNames[i]] = {}
			end

			for _, value23 in ipairs(list) do
				local location = value23.location
				local country = location and location.country
				local city = location and location.city
				local text5

				if type(country) == "string" then
					text5 = country:upper()
				else
					text5 = nil
				end

				local text6 = text5 and city and text5 .. "\0" .. city
				local value24 = api.GetServerHopContinent(text5)

				if value23.inactive ~= true and text6 and value24 and not table16[text6] then
					table16[text6] = true

					regions[value24][#regions[value24] + 1] = {
						Country = text5,
						City = city,
						Latitude = tonumber(location.latLong and location.latLong[1]),
						Longitude = tonumber(location.latLong and location.latLong[2]),
					}
				end
			end

			if not next(table16) then
				return false, "RoValra returned no active datacenters"
			end
			serverHop.Regions = regions
			serverHop.AutomaticCountry = nil
			serverHop.PlayerCoordinates = nil
			table.clear(serverHop.ResolvedRegions)
			return true
		end

		api.GetServerHopPlayerCoordinates = function()
			local serverHop = sharedState.ServerHop
			local LocalizationService = game:GetService("LocalizationService")
			local ok, result = pcall(LocalizationService.GetCountryRegionForPlayerAsync, LocalizationService, localPlayer)
			if not ok or type(result) ~= "string" or result == "" then
				return
			end
			local automaticCountry = result:upper()
			if serverHop.AutomaticCountry == automaticCountry then
				local playerCoordinates = serverHop.PlayerCoordinates
				return playerCoordinates and playerCoordinates.X, playerCoordinates and playerCoordinates.Y, automaticCountry
			end
			local match = nil
			local value22 = nil

			for _, value23 in ipairs({
				"https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/google/dspl@master/samples/google/canonical/countries.csv",
				"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/google/dspl/master/samples/google/canonical/countries.csv",
			}) do
				local ok2, result2 = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, value23, true)

				if ok2 and type(result2) == "string" then
					match, value22 = result2:match("\n" .. automaticCountry .. ",([%d%.%-]+),([%d%.%-]+),")
					if not match then
						continue
					end
				else
					continue
				end

				break
			end

			local num = tonumber(match)
			local num2 = tonumber(value22)
			local value23 = serverHop.CountryCoordinates[automaticCountry]

			if not num and value23 then
				num = value23.X
				num2 = value23.Y
			end

			serverHop.AutomaticCountry = automaticCountry
			local vector2

			if num and num2 then
				vector2 = Vector2.new(num, num2)
			else
				vector2 = nil
			end

			serverHop.PlayerCoordinates = vector2
			table.clear(serverHop.ResolvedRegions)
			return num, num2, automaticCountry
		end

		api.GetServerHopRegions = function()
			local serverHop = sharedState.ServerHop
			local serverHopRegion = configValues.ServerHopRegion

			if not next(serverHop.Regions) then
				local value22, value23 = api.RefreshServerHopRegions()
				if not value22 then
					return nil, value23
				end
			end

			if serverHopRegion ~= "Automatic" and not serverHop.Regions[serverHopRegion] then
				configValues.ServerHopRegion = "Automatic"
				serverHopRegion = "Automatic"

				if options.ServerHopRegion then
					options.ServerHopRegion:SetValue("Automatic")
				end
			end

			local value22, value23, value24 = api.GetServerHopPlayerCoordinates()
			local text5 = value24 and value24 .. "\0" .. serverHopRegion
			if text5 and serverHop.ResolvedRegions[text5] then
				return serverHop.ResolvedRegions[text5]
			end
			local value25 = value22 and math.rad(value22)

			local function func21(arg)
				if not (value25 and arg.Latitude and arg.Longitude) then
					return math.huge
				end
				local value26 = math.rad(arg.Latitude - value22)
				local value27 = math.rad(arg.Longitude - value23)
				local value28 = math.sin(value26 / 2)
				local value29 = math.sin(value27 / 2)
				return value28 * value28 + math.cos(value25) * math.cos(math.rad(arg.Latitude)) * value29 * value29
			end

			if serverHopRegion == "Automatic" then
				serverHopRegion = api.GetServerHopContinent(value24)

				if not serverHopRegion and value22 then
					local huge = math.huge

					for i = 2, #serverHop.RegionNames do
						local value26 = serverHop.RegionNames[i]
						local value27 = ipairs
						local table16 = serverHop.Regions[value26] or {}

						for _, value28 in value27(table16) do
							local value29 = func21(value28)

							if value29 < huge then
								huge = value29
								serverHopRegion = value26
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local value26 = serverHop.Regions[serverHopRegion]
			if not value26 or #value26 == 0 then
				return
			end
			local value27 = table.clone(value26)

			table.sort(value27, function(arg, arg2)
				local value28 = func21(arg)
				local value29 = func21(arg2)

				if value28 == value29 then
					local state12 = arg.Country == value24
					if state12 ~= arg2.Country == value24 then
						return state12
					end
					return tostring(arg.City) < tostring(arg2.City)
				end

				return value28 < value29
			end)

			if text5 then
				serverHop.ResolvedRegions[text5] = value27
			end

			return value27
		end

		api.FetchServerHopRegionIds = function(arg)
			local text5 = "https://apis.rovalra.com/v1/servers/region?place_id=" .. placeId .. "&country=" .. HttpService:UrlEncode(arg.Country) .. "&cursor=0"

			if arg.City then
				text5 ..= "&city=" .. HttpService:UrlEncode(arg.City)
			end

			local value22, value23 = api.FetchServerHopPage(text5, "servers", "RoValra")
			if not value22 then
				return nil, value23
			end
			local table16 = {}

			for _, server in ipairs(value22.servers) do
				local serverId = server.server_id or server.id

				if type(serverId) == "string" and serverId ~= jobId then
					table16[serverId] = true
				end
			end

			return table16
		end

		api.FetchServerHopServersPage = function(arg, arg2)
			local num36 = math.min(configValues.ServerHopMinPlayers, configValues.ServerHopMaxPlayers)
			local text5

			if configValues.ServerHopSort == "Highest Players" or num36 >= Players.MaxPlayers / 2 then
				text5 = "Desc"
			else
				text5 = "Asc"
			end

			local text6 = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. placeId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=" .. text5 .. "&limit=100&excludeFullGames=" .. tostring(not featureState.ServerHopIncludeFull)

			if type(arg) == "string" and arg ~= "" then
				text6 ..= "&cursor=" .. HttpService:UrlEncode(arg)
			end

			local value22, value23

			repeat
				if unloaded or arg2 ~= sharedState.ServerHop.Token then
					return
				end
				value22, value23 = api.FetchServerHopPage(text6, "data", "Roblox")
				local value24 = unloaded
				local state12

				if unloaded then
					state12 = value24
				else
					state12 = arg2 ~= sharedState.ServerHop.Token
				end

				if state12 then
					return
				end

				if not value22 and not unloaded then
					task.wait(0.25)
				end
			until value22 or unloaded or arg2 ~= sharedState.ServerHop.Token

			if unloaded or arg2 ~= sharedState.ServerHop.Token then
				return
			end

			if not value22 then
				sharedState.ServerHop.LastFetchError = value23
				return
			end
			local table16 = {}

			for _, value24 in ipairs(value22.data) do
				local value25 = api.NormalizeServerHopServer(value24)

				if value25 and value25.Id ~= jobId then
					table16[#table16 + 1] = value25
				end
			end

			return table16, value22.nextPageCursor
		end

		api.FilterServerHopServers = function(arg, arg2)
			local table16 = {}

			for _, value22 in ipairs(arg) do
				if api.IsServerHopCandidate(value22, arg2) then
					table16[#table16 + 1] = value22
				end
			end

			return table16
		end

		api.SelectServerHopServer = function(arg)
			if #arg == 0 then
				return
			end

			if configValues.ServerHopSort == "Closest Datacenter" or configValues.ServerHopSort == "Lowest Ping" or configValues.ServerHopSort == "Random" then
				return arg[math.random(1, #arg)]
			end

			table.sort(arg, function(arg2, arg3)
				local playing, playing2

				if configValues.ServerHopSort == "Lowest Players" then
					playing = arg2.Playing
					playing2 = arg3.Playing
				elseif configValues.ServerHopSort == "Highest Players" then
					playing = -arg2.Playing
					playing2 = -arg3.Playing
				elseif configValues.ServerHopSort == "Highest FPS" then
					playing = -(arg2.FPS or 0)
					playing2 = -(arg3.FPS or 0)
				else
					playing = arg2.Ping or math.huge
					playing2 = arg3.Ping or math.huge
				end

				if playing == playing2 then
					return arg2.Id < arg3.Id
				end
				return playing < playing2
			end)

			return arg[1]
		end

		api.UpdateServerHopButton = function()
			local serverHop = sharedState.ServerHop
			if unloaded or not serverHop.Button then
				return
			end
			local busy = serverHop.Busy or serverHop.Pending or serverHop.Retrying
			serverHop.Button.DoubleClick = not busy
			local button = serverHop.Button
			local setText = button.SetText
			local text5

			if busy then
				text5 = "Cancel Server Hop"
			else
				text5 = "Server-hop"
			end

			setText(button, text5)
		end

		api.CancelServerHop = function()
			local serverHop = sharedState.ServerHop
			local pending = serverHop.Pending
			serverHop.Token = serverHop.Token + 1
			serverHop.AutoToken = serverHop.AutoToken + 1
			serverHop.Busy = false
			serverHop.Pending = nil
			serverHop.Retrying = false
			serverHop.Cancelled = true

			if serverHop.Notification then
				serverHop.Notification:Destroy()
				serverHop.Notification = nil
			end

			api.UpdateServerHopButton()

			if not unloaded then
				if pending then
					notify("Server-hop retries cancelled. Roblox may still complete the teleport already requested.")
				end

				if featureState.AutoServerHop then
					api.ScheduleAutoServerHop()
				end
			end
		end

		api.ServerHop = function(arg, arg2)
			if unloaded then
				return false
			end

			if sharedState.ServerHop.Busy or sharedState.ServerHop.Pending or sharedState.ServerHop.Retrying then
				notify("A server-hop is already in progress.")
				return false
			end
			local serverHop = sharedState.ServerHop
			serverHop.Token = serverHop.Token + 1
			local token = sharedState.ServerHop.Token

			local function func21()
				return not unloaded and token == sharedState.ServerHop.Token
			end

			sharedState.ServerHop.Busy = true
			sharedState.ServerHop.Cancelled = false
			api.UpdateServerHopButton()

			local value22 = uiLib:Notify({
				Title = "Resonance",
				Description = "Attempting to serverhop..",
				Persist = true,
				SoundId = noticeSoundId,
				BigIcon = "rbxassetid://79685990879972",
				Volume = noticeVolume,
				IconColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			})

			if not func21() then
				value22:Destroy()
				return false
			end
			sharedState.ServerHop.Notification = value22

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				local value23, value24 = api.GetServerHopRegions()
				if not func21() then
					return false
				end

				if not value23 then
					local value25 = notify
					local text5

					if value24 then
						text5 = " Error: " .. value24
					else
						text5 = ""
					end

					value25("Couldn't determine the server-hop region; server-hop cancelled." .. text5)
					return false
				end

				local table16, now3, value25, cachedRegions = api.LoadServerHopCache()
				if not func21() then
					return false
				end

				if not value25 then
					table16 = {}
				end

				local table17 = {}
				local value26 = nil
				local num36 = 0
				local num37 = 0
				local table18 = {}
				local table19 = {}
				local serverHop2 = sharedState.ServerHop

				if not value25 then
					cachedRegions = {}
				end

				serverHop2.CachedRegions = cachedRegions
				sharedState.ServerHop.LastFetchError = nil

				for _, value27 in ipairs(table16) do
					table19[value27.Id] = true
				end

				local function func22(arg3)
					for _, value27 in ipairs(value23) do
						if not func21() then
							return {}
						end
						local text5 = value27.Country .. "\0" .. tostring(value27.City or "")
						local value28 = sharedState.ServerHop.CachedRegions[text5]

						if not value28 then
							if num37 > 0 then
								task.wait(1)
							end

							if not func21() then
								return {}
							end
							local value29
							value28, value29 = api.FetchServerHopRegionIds(value27)
							value26 = value29
							if not func21() then
								return {}
							end
							num37 += 1
							if not value28 then
								return {}
							end
							sharedState.ServerHop.CachedRegions[text5] = value28
						end

						if not table18[text5] then
							table18[text5] = true
							num36 += 1
						end

						local value29 = api.FilterServerHopServers(arg3, value28)
						if #value29 > 0 then
							return value29
						end
					end

					return {}
				end

				if value25 and #api.FilterServerHopServers(table16) > 0 then
					table17 = func22(table16)
				end

				if #table17 == 0 and not value26 then
					table.clear(table16)
					table.clear(table19)
					local text5 = ""

					for i = 1, 10 do
						if i > 1 then
							task.wait(1)
						end

						if not func21() then
							return false
						end
						local value27, value28 = api.FetchServerHopServersPage(text5, token)
						if not func21() then
							return false
						end

						if not value27 then
							break
						end
						now3 = os.time()

						for _, value29 in ipairs(value27) do
							if not table19[value29.Id] then
								table19[value29.Id] = true
								table16[#table16 + 1] = value29
							end
						end

						if #api.FilterServerHopServers(value27) > 0 then
							table17 = func22(value27)
							if #table17 > 0 or value26 then
								break
							end
						end

						if type(value28) ~= "string" or value28 == "" then
							break
						end
						text5 = value28
					end
				end

				if not func21() then
					return false
				end

				if #table17 == 0 then
					if value26 then
						notify("Region search failed. Error: " .. value26)
					elseif sharedState.ServerHop.LastFetchError then
						notify("Roblox server search stopped. Error: " .. sharedState.ServerHop.LastFetchError)
					elseif #table16 == 0 then
						local value27 = notify
						local text5

						if sharedState.ServerHop.LastFetchError then
							text5 = " Error: " .. sharedState.ServerHop.LastFetchError
						else
							text5 = ""
						end

						value27("Roblox didn't return any public servers. Try again in a few seconds." .. text5)
					else
						local serverHopRegion = configValues.ServerHopRegion
						notify(("Checked %d servers across %d datacenters, but none matched %d-%d players in %s."):format(#table16, num36, math.min(configValues.ServerHopMinPlayers, configValues.ServerHopMaxPlayers), math.max(configValues.ServerHopMinPlayers, configValues.ServerHopMaxPlayers), serverHopRegion))
					end

					if now3 then
						api.SaveServerHopCache(table16, now3)
					end

					return false
				end

				local value27 = api.SelectServerHopServer(table17)

				for i = #table16, 1, -1 do
					if table16[i].Id == value27.Id then
						table.remove(table16, i)
						break
					end
				end

				local value28 = api.SaveServerHopCache(table16, now3)
				if not func21() then
					return false
				end

				if not value28 then
					notify("Server cache couldn't be saved: " .. tostring(sharedState.ServerHop.LastCacheError))
				end

				api.QueueTeleport(arg, arg2)
				if not func21() then
					return false
				end
				sharedState.ServerHop.Pending = { ServerId = value27.Id, AntiLag = arg, RestartScript = arg2 }
				local ok, result = pcall(TeleportService.TeleportToPlaceInstance, TeleportService, placeId, value27.Id, localPlayer)
				if not func21() then
					return false
				end

				if not ok then
					local text5 = tostring(result)
					if text5:lower():find("full", 1, true) then
						return api.RetryFullServerHop()
					end
					sharedState.ServerHop.Pending = nil
					notify("Server-hop failed: " .. text5)
				end

				return ok
			end)

			if not func21() then
				return false
			end
			sharedState.ServerHop.Busy = false
			value22:Destroy()
			sharedState.ServerHop.Notification = nil
			local state12 = not ok

			if state12 then
				sharedState.ServerHop.Pending = nil
			end

			api.UpdateServerHopButton()
			if state12 then
				notify("Server-hop failed: " .. tostring(result))
				return false
			end
			return result
		end

		api.RetryFullServerHop = function()
			local value22 = unloaded
			local cancelled

			if unloaded then
				cancelled = value22
			else
				cancelled = sharedState.ServerHop.Cancelled
			end

			if cancelled then
				return false
			end
			local pending = sharedState.ServerHop.Pending
			if not pending then
				return sharedState.ServerHop.Retrying == true
			end
			sharedState.ServerHop.Pending = nil
			sharedState.ServerHop.Rejected[pending.ServerId] = true
			sharedState.ServerHop.Busy = false
			sharedState.ServerHop.Retrying = true
			local token = sharedState.ServerHop.Token
			api.UpdateServerHopButton()
			notify("Server is full; trying another server in the selected region.")

			task.delay(1, function()
				if unloaded or token ~= sharedState.ServerHop.Token then
					return
				end
				sharedState.ServerHop.Retrying = false

				if not unloaded then
					api.ServerHop(pending.AntiLag, pending.RestartScript)
				end
			end)

			return true
		end

		api.ScheduleAutoServerHop = function(arg)
			local serverHop = sharedState.ServerHop
			serverHop.AutoToken = serverHop.AutoToken + 1
			local autoToken = sharedState.ServerHop.AutoToken
			local value22 = unloaded
			local state12

			if unloaded then
				state12 = value22
			else
				state12 = not featureState.AutoServerHop
			end

			if state12 then
				return
			end

			task.delay(arg or configValues.AutoServerHopTime, function()
				if unloaded or autoToken ~= sharedState.ServerHop.AutoToken or not featureState.AutoServerHop then
					return
				end

				if not api.ServerHop(featureState.RejoinWithAntiLag ~= false, featureState.RejoinWithScript) and autoToken == sharedState.ServerHop.AutoToken and featureState.AutoServerHop then
					api.ScheduleAutoServerHop(math.min(15, configValues.AutoServerHopTime))
				end
			end)
		end

		TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed:Connect(function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
			if arg ~= localPlayer or unloaded then
				return
			end

			if sharedState.ServerHop.Cancelled or sharedState.ServerHop.Busy and not sharedState.ServerHop.Pending then
				return
			end

			if sharedState.ServerHop.Pending and arg5 and arg5.ServerInstanceId ~= "" and arg5.ServerInstanceId ~= sharedState.ServerHop.Pending.ServerId then
				return
			end
			local state12 = arg2 == Enum.TeleportResult.GameFull

			if not state12 then
				state12 = tostring(arg3):lower():find("full", 1, true)
			end

			if state12 then
				if api.RetryFullServerHop() then
					return
				end
			else
				sharedState.ServerHop.Pending = nil
			end

			api.UpdateServerHopButton()

			if featureState.AutoServerHop then
				api.ScheduleAutoServerHop(10)
			end
		end)

		api.CreatePlayerDropdown = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5, arg6, arg7)
			if not arg or not arg3 then
				notify("Something went wrong, tell the owner about this notification.")
				return
			end
			local state12 = arg5 ~= false
			local state13 = arg4 ~= false
			local table16 = { Dropdown = nil, Values = arg3, Selected = {}, Index = arg2, Multi = state13 }

			local value22 = arg:AddDropdown(arg2 .. "Dropdown", {
				SpecialType = "Player",
				Default = nil,
				ExcludeLocalPlayer = true,
				Multi = state13,
				AllowNull = true,
				Searchable = true,
				Text = arg7 or "Targets",
				FormatListValue = api.FormatPlayer,
				FormatDisplayValue = api.FormatPlayer,
				EnablePlayerImages = true,
				Callback = function(arg8)
					table.clear(arg3)

					if state13 then
						for k, value22 in pairs(arg8) do
							if not api.IsProtectedPlayer(k) then
								arg3[k] = value22
							end
						end
					elseif arg8 and not api.IsProtectedPlayer(arg8) then
						arg3[arg8] = true
					end

					table.clear(table16.Selected)

					if state13 then
						for k, value22 in pairs(arg8) do
							if not api.IsProtectedPlayer(k) then
								table16.Selected[k] = value22
							end
						end
					elseif arg8 and not api.IsProtectedPlayer(arg8) then
						table16.Selected[arg8] = true
					end

					if arg6 then
						arg6(arg8)
					end
				end,
			})

			table16.Dropdown = value22
			table.insert(sharedState.PlayerDropdowns, table16)

			local function func21(arg8)
				if api.IsProtectedPlayer(arg8) then
					return notify("This player has Resonance immunity.")
				end

				if not state13 then
					value22:SetValue(arg8)
					local value23 = notify
					local text5 = ("Selected %* in %*!"):format(arg8.Name, arg2)
					value23(text5)
					return
				end

				local table17 = {}

				for k, value23 in pairs(value22.Value) do
					if k ~= arg8 then
						table17[k] = value23
					end
				end

				if not value22.Value[arg8] then
					table17[arg8] = true
					local value23 = notify
					local text5 = ("Selected %* in %*!"):format(arg8.Name, arg2)
					value23(text5)
				else
					local value23 = notify
					local text5 = ("Deselected %* in %*!"):format(arg8.Name, arg2)
					value23(text5)
				end

				value22:SetValue(table17)
			end

			if state12 then
				arg:AddLabel("Add To List"):AddKeyPicker(arg2 .. "DropdownST", {
					Text = "Add To List",
					Mode = "Press",
					NoUI = true,
					Callback = function()
						local target = mouse.Target
						if not target or not target.Parent then
							return
						end
						local value23 = Players:FindFirstChild(target.Parent.Name)
						if not value23 then
							return
						end
						func21(value23)
					end,
				})
			end

			return value22
		end

		api.GetTargets = function(arg, arg2)
			local table16 = {}

			if arg then
				for _, value22 in ipairs(sharedState.PlayerList) do
					if value22 ~= localPlayer and not api.IsProtectedPlayer(value22, true) then
						table.insert(table16, value22)
					end
				end
			else
				for k in pairs(arg2) do
					if k and k.Parent and not api.IsProtectedPlayer(k) then
						table.insert(table16, k)
					else
						arg2[k] = nil
					end
				end
			end

			return table16
		end

		api.RunPlayerMethod = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
			if not arg2 then
				return
			end

			if arg4 then
				methodQueue.Enqueue(arg, arg2, arg3, arg5)
				return
			end
			local ok, result = pcall(arg2, arg3)

			if not ok then
				warn(("[Resonance] %s failed: %s"):format(arg, tostring(result)))
			end
		end

		api.ProcessPlayer = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
			local function func21(arg6, arg7)
				api.RunPlayerMethod(arg6, arg7, arg, arg2, arg5)
			end

			if arg4 then
				local blobMethod = configValues.BlobMethod

				if blobMethod == "Bring" then
					func21("Blob_Bring", blobApi.Bring)
				elseif blobMethod == "Kill" then
					func21("Blob_Kill", blobApi.Kill)
				elseif blobMethod == "Follow" then
					func21("Blob_Follow", blobApi.Follow)
				elseif blobMethod == "Remove Gucci" then
					func21("Blob_RemoveGucci", blobApi.RemoveGucci)
				elseif blobMethod == "Lock" then
					local func22

					if arg2 then
						func22 = function(arg6)
							blobApi.LoopLock(arg6, arg5)
						end
					else
						func22 = blobApi.Lock
					end

					func21("Blob_Lock", func22)
				elseif blobMethod == "Kick" and not arg3 then
					local loopKick

					if arg2 then
						loopKick = blobApi.LoopKick
					else
						loopKick = blobApi.Kick
					end

					func21("Blob_Kick", loopKick)
				end

				return
			end

			local targetMethods = sharedState.TargetMethods
			local value22, value23 = api.Char(arg)

			if targetMethods["Remove Invisibility"] and value23 then
				value23.Massless = false
			end

			if targetMethods["Resolve Desync"] then
				func21("Resolve Desync", api.ResolveDesync)
			end

			if targetMethods.Bring then
				func21("Bring", api.BringPlayer)
			end

			local kick = targetMethods.Kick
			local state12

			if kick then
				local state13 = arg3 and configValues.KickServerMethod == "Void"

				if state13 then
					state12 = state13
				else
					state12 = not arg3 and not arg2
				end
			else
				state12 = kick
			end

			if state12 then
				func21("Kick", api.Void)
			end

			if targetMethods.Kill then
				func21("Kill", api.Kill)
			end

			if targetMethods.Void then
				func21("Void", api.Void)
			end

			if targetMethods["Remove Gucci"] then
				func21("Remove Gucci", api.RemoveGucci)
			end

			if targetMethods["Break Holdable"] then
				func21("Break Holdable", api.EatAntiInput)
			end

			if targetMethods["Delete Seatables"] then
				func21("Delete Seatables", api.DeleteSeatables)
			end
		end

		api.ProcessToyEffects = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
			local toyEffects = sharedState.ToyEffects

			if toyEffects.Oven then
				api.RunPlayerMethod("ToyEffect_Oven", api.MakeBlack, arg, arg2, arg5)
			end

			if toyEffects.Microwave then
				api.RunPlayerMethod("ToyEffect_Microwave", api.Microwave, arg, arg2, arg5)
			end

			if toyEffects.Banana then
				api.RunPlayerMethod("ToyEffect_Banana", api.BananaRagdoll, arg, arg2, arg5)
			end

			if toyEffects["Delete Limbs"] then
				api.RunPlayerMethod("ToyEffect_DeleteLimbs", function(arg6)
					api.BreakLimb(arg6, configValues.TargetedLimbTE)
				end, arg, arg2, arg5)
			end

			if toyEffects.Campfire then
				api.RunPlayerMethod("ToyEffect_Campfire", api.Campfire, arg, arg2, arg5)
			end

			if toyEffects.Fling then
				api.RunPlayerMethod("ToyEffect_Fling", api.Fling, arg, arg2, arg5)
			end

			if toyEffects["Ragdoll Kill"] then
				api.RunPlayerMethod("ToyEffect_RagdollKill", function(arg6)
					local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char(arg6)

					if value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0 and api.SNO(value23) then
						api.Ragdoll(value22)
						api.Kill(arg6)
					end
				end, arg, arg2, arg5)
			end
		end

		api.KickServer = function(arg)
			local kickServerState = sharedState.KickServerState
			local table16 = {}
			if kickServerState.Running then
				return false
			end
			local now3 = os.clock()

			for _, value22 in ipairs(api.GetTargets(true, sharedState.SelectedPlayers)) do
				local value23, value24, value25 = api.Char(value22)
				local state12 = value24 and value25 and value25.Health > 0

				if state12 then
					state12 = not arg

					if not state12 then
						state12 = now3 - (sharedState.KickServerCooldowns[value22] or -math.huge) >= 15
					end
				end

				if state12 then
					table16[#table16 + 1] = value22
				end
			end

			if #table16 == 0 then
				return false
			end
			kickServerState.Running = true
			local table17 = {}
			local table18 = {}
			local connection = nil

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				options.LineLagSSlider:SetValue(11)

				if not toggles.LineLagToggle.Value then
					toggles.LineLagToggle:SetValue(true)
				end

				toggles.LineLagToggle:SetDisabled(true)
				local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
				if not (value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0) then
					return
				end
				local position = value23.Position

				connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					for k, value25 in table18, nil, nil do
						if k.Parent then
							k.CFrame = value25
							api.ResetVelocity(k)
						else
							table18[k] = nil
						end
					end
				end)

				for i, value25 in ipairs(table16) do
					if not unloaded then
						local value26, value27, value28 = api.Char(value25)

						if value27 and value28 and value28.Health > 0 then
							if arg then
								sharedState.KickServerCooldowns[value25] = os.clock()
							end

							if api.SNO(value27) then
								local num36 = i / #table16 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
								local value29 = table17
								local num37 = #table17 + 1
								local value30 = table18
								local cframe = CFrame.new(position + Vector3.new(math.cos(num36) * 15, 15, math.sin(num36) * 15))
								value29[num37] = value27
								value30[value27] = cframe
								api.Teleport(value27, table18[value27])
							end
						end

						continue
					end

					break
				end

				for i, value25 in ipairs(table17) do
					local num36 = i / #table17 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
					table18[value25] = CFrame.new(position + Vector3.new(math.cos(num36) * 15, 15, math.sin(num36) * 15))
				end

				RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			end)

			task.spawn(function()
				local num36 = os.clock() + 3

				while not unloaded and os.clock() < num36 do
					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				end

				if connection then
					connection:Disconnect()
					connection = nil
				end
			end)

			task.wait(0.15)

			for _, value22 in ipairs(table17) do
				if value22.Parent then
					api.RNO(value22)
				end
			end

			for _, value22 in ipairs(table17) do
				if not unloaded and value22.Parent then
					task.spawn(api.RemoveSticky, value22.Parent)
				end
			end

			task.wait(6)

			if not unloaded then
				toggles.LineLagToggle:SetDisabled(false)
				toggles.LineLagToggle:SetValue(false)
			end

			kickServerState.Running = false

			if not ok then
				warn("[Resonance] Target Kick Server failed: " .. tostring(result))
			end

			return ok and #table17 > 0
		end

		api.CreateLoopToggle = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5, arg6, arg7)
			local text5

			if arg4 then
				text5 = "Server"
			else
				text5 = "Selected"
			end

			local text6

			if arg4 then
				text6 = "all"
			else
				text6 = "selected"
			end

			local processPlayer = arg7 or api.ProcessPlayer
			local state12 = processPlayer == api.ProcessPlayer

			arg:AddToggle(arg2, {
				Text = "Loop Apply Method " .. text5,
				Default = false,
				Tooltip = "Keeps applying selected methods to " .. text6 .. " players",
				Callback = function(arg8)
					api.SetRuntimeToggle(arg3, arg8)
					methodQueue.LoopTokens[arg3] = (methodQueue.LoopTokens[arg3] or 0) + 1
					methodQueue.Cancel(arg3)
					if not arg8 then
						return
					end
					local value22 = methodQueue.LoopTokens[arg3]

					task.spawn(function()
						while not unloaded and featureState[arg3] and methodQueue.LoopTokens[arg3] == value22 do
							if arg5 and arg4 and configValues.BlobMethod == "Kick" then
								blobApi.KickServer()
								task.wait(1)
							else
								if state12 and not arg5 and arg4 and sharedState.TargetMethods.Kick and configValues.KickServerMethod == "Circle + Lag" then
									api.KickServer(true)
								end


								for _, value23 in ipairs(api.GetTargets(arg4, arg6)) do
									if not (not featureState[arg3] or methodQueue.LoopTokens[arg3] ~= value22) then
										processPlayer(value23, true, arg4, arg5, arg3)
										continue
									end
									break
								end
							end

							if state12 and not arg5 and arg4 and sharedState.TargetMethods.Kick and configValues.KickServerMethod == "Circle + Lag" then
								task.wait(10)
							else
								task.wait()
							end
						end
					end)
				end,
			})
		end

		api.CreateApplyButton = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
			local text5

			if arg2 then
				text5 = "Server"
			else
				text5 = "Selected"
			end

			local processPlayer = arg5 or api.ProcessPlayer

				Func = function()
					task.spawn(function()

							blobApi.KickTargets(api.GetTargets(arg2, arg4), arg2)
							return
						end

						if state12 and not arg3 and arg2 and sharedState.TargetMethods.Kick and configValues.KickServerMethod == "Circle + Lag" then
							api.KickServer(false)
						end

						for _, value22 in ipairs(api.GetTargets(arg2, arg4)) do
							processPlayer(value22, false, arg2, arg3)
						end
					end)
				end,
			})
		end

		api.AddRainbowToggle = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)

			local state12 = false

				Text = arg3,
				Default = false,

					end

					num36 += 1

					if not arg5 then
						if value and options[arg4] then
							options[arg4]:SetValue(value)
						end

						value = nil
						return
					end

					value = options[arg4] and options[arg4].Value
					local value22 = num36

					task.spawn(function()
						while num36 == value22 and toggles[arg2] and toggles[arg2].Value do
							if options[arg4] then
								options[arg4]:SetValue(Color3.fromHSV(os.clock() / 5 % 1, 1, 1))
							end

							task.wait(0.05)
						end
					end)
				end,
			})
		end

		task.spawn(function()

				end

					return
				end
				local value22, value23 = api.Char()
				local position = value23 and value23.Position
				local num36 = configValues.AuraDistance * configValues.AuraDistance

				for _, value24 in pairs(sharedState.PlayerList) do
					if not (featureState.ExcludeTarget and (sharedState.SelectedPlayers[value24] or sharedState.SelectedPlayersAC[value24] or sharedState.SelectedPlayersBlob[value24])) then
						if value24 ~= localPlayer and not api.IsProtectedPlayer(value24, true) and value24.Character then
							local value25, value26, value27 = api.Char(value24)

							if position and value26 and value27 and value27.Health ~= 0 then
								local num37 = position - value26.Position

								if num37:Dot(num37) <= num36 then
									if sharedState.Auras["Kick [Slow]"] then
										if not value26:FindFirstChild("KickBP") then
											local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
											bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
											bodyPosition.Position = value26.Position + Vector3.new(0, 7, 0)
											bodyPosition.Name = "KickBP"
											bodyPosition.Parent = value26

													api.Grab(value26)

											end)
										end

										if magneticDistance > 4 then
											api.Grab(value26)

											if api.IsPartOwner(value26) then

											end
										end
									end

										if creatureGrab and rightWeld then
											api.Grab(value26)

												api.RNO(value26)

										end
									end

									if sharedState.Auras["Blob-Kill"] and parent then

											creatureGrab:FireServer(nil, value26, rightWeld)
											task.wait()
											creatureRelease:FireServer(rightWeld)
											task.wait()

											if api.IsNetworkOwner(value26) then
												value27:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
											end
										end
									end

									if sharedState.Auras["Blob-Grab"] and parent then
										local creatureGrab = parent and parent:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript") and parent.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab")

									end

									if sharedState.Auras["Banana Ragdoll"] then

									end

										api.BreakLimb(value24, configValues.TargetedLimbAura)

		task.spawn(function()

				local state12 = toysAuraSettings.Enabled and toysAuraSettings.OwnType == "Aura"

					local toyAuraDistance = configValues.ToyAuraDistance

							charRegistry.Candidates[k] = nil
							sharedState.ToyAuraRoots[k] = nil

							local value26 = charRegistry.GetRoot(k)
							local num37 = value26 and value26.Position - value23.Position

							if not (not num37 or num37:Dot(num37) > num36) then
								if state12 and (not ownInventoryOnly or candidate == toysFolder) then
									charRegistry.AddModel(k, true, 0.25, value26)
								end

								if state13 and candidate ~= toysFolder then
									local value27 = sharedState.Anchored[k] or api.SNO(value26)

									if sharedState.ToyAuras.Destroy and value27 then
										api.DestroyObject(k)
									else
										if sharedState.ToyAuras.Anchor and value27 and not sharedState.Anchored[k] then
											task.spawn(api.SetAnchored, k, true)
										end

										if sharedState.ToyAuras.Fling and value27 then
											value26.Velocity = value23.CFrame.LookVector * 50 + Vector3.new(0, 25, 0)
										end

										if sharedState.ToyAuras["Teleport to Spawn"] and value27 then
											api.Teleport(value26, workspace.SpawnLocation.Position + Vector3.new(0, 25, 0))
										end

										if sharedState.ToyAuras.Bounce and not value26:FindFirstChild("SmashBV") then
											local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
											bodyVelocity.Name = "SmashBV"
											bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 100, 0)
											bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
											bodyVelocity.Parent = value26

											task.delay(0.75, function()
												if not value26.Parent or not bodyVelocity.Parent then
													return
												end
												bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
												bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
												value26.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, -120, 0)
												task.wait(0.4)

												if bodyVelocity.Parent then
													bodyVelocity:Destroy()
												end
											end)
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if unloaded then
				return
			end

			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
				if featureState.Zoom then
					if input.Position.Z > 0 then
						api.SetFOV(configValues.ZoomFOV - configValues.ZoomStep, true)
					elseif input.Position.Z < 0 then
						api.SetFOV(configValues.ZoomFOV + configValues.ZoomStep, true)
					end

					return
				end

				if configValues.LineDistance < 11 then
					configValues.LineDistance = 11
				end

				if input.Position.Z > 0 then
					configValues.LineDistance = configValues.LineDistance + configValues.IncreaseLineDistance
				elseif input.Position.Z < 0 then
					configValues.LineDistance = configValues.LineDistance - configValues.IncreaseLineDistance
				end
			end
		end)

		workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if unloaded then
				return
			end

			if child.Name == "GrabParts" then
				local beamPart = child:WaitForChild("BeamPart", 0.1)
				beamPart = beamPart and beamPart:WaitForChild("GrabBeam", 0.1)

				if featureState.CustomLine and beamPart then
					beamPart.Texture = sharedState.LineTypes[configValues.CustomLine]
				end

				local dragPart = child:WaitForChild("DragPart", 0.1)
				local grabPart = child:WaitForChild("GrabPart", 0.1)
				local weldConstraint = grabPart and grabPart:WaitForChild("WeldConstraint", 0.1)
				local part1 = weldConstraint and weldConstraint.Part1

				if toggles.RealisticFirstPersonT.Value then

			local AK_TOY_NAMES = {

						local function func22(arg)

						local result = nil

							notify("Starting PCLD Break...", "PCLD Breaker")

							task.spawn(function()
								repeat
									value24.Health = 0
									value24:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
									waitAtLeast()
								until result
							end)
						else
							notify("Detected death, breaking PCLD again...", "PCLD Breaker")
						end

						result = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
						local humanoid = result:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

						if humanoid then
							local result2 = nil

							task.spawn(function()
								repeat
									humanoid.Health = 0
									humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
									waitAtLeast()
								until result2
							end)

							result2 = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
							local humanoid2 = result2:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

							if humanoid2 then
								humanoid2:SetAttribute("BrokenPCLD", true)
								notify("Done!", "PCLD Breaker")
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		do

			RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
				if unloaded or not state12 or not featureState.AutoAntiLag then
					return
				end
				local antiLagSens = configValues.AntiLagSens

				if math.floor(1 / deltaTime) < antiLagSens and toggles.AntiLag and not toggles.AntiLag.Value then
					notify("Anti Lag Enabled || Too low fps", "Auto Anti Lag")
					toggles.AntiLag:SetValue(true)
				end
			end)
		end

							if value23 and value23:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") then
								continue

				if not (not weldHRP or not value24 or value24.Health == 0 or not value23) then

						value24.Sit = false

						ContextActionService:UnbindAction("JumpRemover")

			local gameCorrectionsNotify = ReplicatedStorage.GameCorrectionEvents.GameCorrectionsNotify

			if gameCorrectionsNotify then
				local num36 = 0

				gameCorrectionsNotify.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(arg)
					if unloaded then
						return
					end

					if arg == "Flying" then
						local now3 = tick()

						if now3 - num36 > 1 then
							num36 = now3
							configValues.KickFlags = 1
						else
							configValues.KickFlags = configValues.KickFlags + 1
						end

						if featureState.AutoReset then
							if configValues.KickFlags >= 4 and featureState.AutoLeave then
								return localPlayer:Kick("Resonance (Auto Reset)\nAvoided Kick")
							end
							local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()

							if value24 then
								value24:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
								value24:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)

								if configValues.KickFlags == 1 then
									notify("Reset due to kick flag", "Auto Reset")
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end

		do
			local connection = nil

			local function func21()
				local value22, head = api.Char()
				head = head and value22:FindFirstChild("Head")
				if not head then
					return
				end

				connection = head.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
					if unloaded then
						return
					end

					if not child:IsA("StringValue") then
						return
					end
					local value23 = Players:FindFirstChild(child.Value)

					if child.Name == "PartOwner" and value23 then
						configValues.WhoGrabbed = api.FormatPlayer(value23)

						if api.UpdateLastGrabbedLabel then
							api.UpdateLastGrabbedLabel()
						end

						if featureState.CounterAttack and not api.IsProtectedPlayer(value23, true) then
							local value24, value25 = api.Char(value23)

							if configValues.CounterMethod == "Kill" then
								api.Kill(value23)
							elseif configValues.CounterMethod == "Void" then
								api.Void(value23)
							elseif configValues.CounterMethod == "Grab" and value25 then
								api.Grab(value25)
							elseif configValues.CounterMethod == "Delete Limbs" then
								api.BreakLimb(value23, configValues.TargetedLimbCA)
							elseif configValues.CounterMethod == "Fling" and value25 and api.SNO(value25) then
								local _, ownRoot = api.Char()
                                if ownRoot then value25.AssemblyLinearVelocity = ownRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 100 + Vector3.new(0, 25, 0) end
							elseif configValues.CounterMethod == "Teleport to Spawn" and api.SNO(value25) then
								api.Teleport(value25, CFrame.new(0, 10, 0))
							elseif configValues.CounterMethod == "Anchor" then
								task.spawn(api.SetAnchored, value23, true)
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
				if connection then
					connection:Disconnect()
				end

				while true do
					task.wait()
					local head = unloaded

					if not unloaded then
						head = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
					end

					if not head then
						continue
					end
					break
				end

				if unloaded then
					return
				end
				func21()
			end)

			func21()
		end

			if not unloaded and featureState.PlayerESP and arg ~= localPlayer then

				if featureState.StickyESP then

				if featureState.PCLDESP and not api.IsWaitingPCLD(arg) then
					api.CreatePCLDESP(arg)
				end
			end
		end)

		workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if unloaded then
				return
			end

			if child.Name == "BlackHoleKick" then
				configValues.BlackHoles = configValues.BlackHoles + 1
				local value22 = nil

				for _, value23 in pairs(sharedState.PlayerList) do
					if value23 == localPlayer or sharedState.KickLogs[localPlayer] then
						value22 = nil
					else
						local character = value23.Character
						character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if character and character.Anchored and not sharedState.KickLogs[value23] then
							api.NotifyEvent("Kicked Player", { kickedPlayer = api.FormatPlayer(value23) }, "Player Kicked")
							sharedState.KickLogs[value23] = true
							value22 = value23
							break
						else
							value22 = nil
						end
					end
				end

				local hole = child:WaitForChild("Hole", 1.5)
				if not hole then
					return
				end
				local billboardGui = hole:WaitForChild("BillboardGui", 1.5)
				if not billboardGui then
					return
				end
				local large = billboardGui:WaitForChild("Large", 1.5)
				local small = large and billboardGui:WaitForChild("Small", 1.5)
				if not small then
					return
				end

				if featureState.CustomBlackholeSound and configValues.CustomBlackholeSound then
					local drone = hole:WaitForChild("Drone", 1.5)
					if not drone then
						return
					end
					drone.SoundId = configValues.CustomBlackholeSound
				end

				if featureState.CustomBlackHole and configValues.CustomBlackhole then
					large.Image = configValues.CustomBlackhole
					hole.Transparency = 1
					small.Visible = false
				end

				if configValues.CustomBHColor then
					large.ImageColor3 = configValues.CustomBHColor
					small.ImageColor3 = configValues.CustomBHColor
				end

				api.CreateBlackHoleESP(child, value22)

				if featureState.BlackholeStaying then
					task.wait(4.75)
					if not featureState.BlackholeStaying then
						return
					end
					child.Archivable = true
					local clone = child:Clone()
					clone.Name = "StayedBH"
					child:Destroy()
					clone.Parent = workspace
					local hole2 = clone:FindFirstChild("Hole")

					if value22 then
						hole2 = hole2 and hole2:FindFirstChild("Blackhole")

						if hole2 then
							hole2:Destroy()
						end

						api.CreateBlackHoleESP(clone, value22)
					end

					repeat
						task.wait(0.1)
					until not featureState.BlackholeStaying

					if clone.Parent then
						clone:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			local table16 = {}
			local handle = workspace.Slots:GetChildren()[1].SlotHandle.Handle
			local handle2 = workspace.Slots:GetChildren()[2].SlotHandle.Handle
			local handle3 = workspace.Slots:GetChildren()[3].SlotHandle.Handle
			local handle4 = workspace.Slots:GetChildren()[4].SlotHandle.Handle
			local handle5 = workspace.Slots:GetChildren()[5].SlotHandle.Handle
			table16[1] = handle
			table16[2] = handle2
			table16[3] = handle3
			table16[4] = handle4
			table16[5] = handle5
			local timeText = workspace.Slots:GetChildren()[5].Screen.SlotGui.TimeLeftFrame.TimeText
			local coinAmount = workspace.Slots:GetChildren()[5].Screen.SlotGui.CoinsFrame.CoinAmount

			while task.wait(0.5) do
				if unloaded then
					return
				end

				if not featureState.AutoSlot and not api.WaitForRuntimeToggle("AutoSlot") then
					return
				end
				local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()

				if timeText.Text == "0:00" and value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0 then
					for i = 1, #table16 do
						if featureState.AutoSlot then
							if api.SNO(table16[i]) then
								task.wait(1)

								if timeText.Text ~= "0:00" then
									notify("Rolled the casino and got: " .. string.sub(table16[i].Parent.Parent.Screen.SlotGui.CoinsFrame.CoinAmount.Text, -3) .. " Coins", "Auto Slots")
									break
								else
									continue
								end
							else
								continue
							end
						end

						break
					end

					task.wait(1)

					if timeText.Text == "0:00" then
						notify("No available lottery found, to farm coins switch server or retoggle", "Auto Slots")

						repeat
							task.wait()
						until not featureState.AutoSlot
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			while task.wait() do
				if unloaded then
					return
				end

				if not featureState.AutoExplodeTarget and not api.WaitForRuntimeToggle("AutoExplodeTarget") then
					return
				end

				if configValues.ExplosionMethod then

							if not (unloaded or not featureState.AutoExplodeTarget or not configValues.ExplosionMethod) then
								if not (not sharedState.SelectedPlayersExp[value22] or value22.Parent ~= Players) then
									local value = value22.InPlot.Value

									if value then
										value = not (localPlayer.InOwnedPlot.Value or configValues.ExplosionMethod == "BallSnowball")
									end

									if not value then

											api.ExplodeMissile(value24, configValues.ExplosionMethod, configValues.MissilesToFire)
										end
									end
								end

								continue
							end

							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		sharedState.PacketDetectCallbacks = {}
