		isHeld:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
			if unloaded then
				return
			end

			if isHeld.Value and featureState.FasterEscape then
				api.Char()
				local heldTimer = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("HeldTimer")
				local struggle = remotes.Struggle
				local playerGui2 = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
				playerGui2 = playerGui2 and playerGui2:FindFirstChild("ControlsGui")
				local escape = playerGui2 and playerGui2:FindFirstChild("PCFrame") and playerGui2.PCFrame:FindFirstChild("Escape")
				local fasterEscape = configValues.FasterEscape
				local num36 = tick() + fasterEscape + 1

				while isHeld.Value and not unloaded and task.wait() do
					local value = math.floor(num36 - tick())

					if value <= 0 then
						heldTimer.Value = 0
						escape.Visible = true
					else
						heldTimer.Value = value
					end

					if value <= 0 and UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
						struggle:FireServer(localPlayer)
						ContextActionService:UnbindAction("Escape")
						ContextActionService:UnbindAction("JumpRemover")
					end
				end

				escape.Visible = false
			end
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			local table16 = {}
			local weakCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
			local table17 = { "Torso", "Right Leg", "Left Leg", "Right Arm", "Left Arm", "Head" }

			local table18 = {
				["Right Leg"] = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
				["Left Leg"] = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
				["Right Arm"] = Vector3.new(1, 0.5, 0),
				["Left Arm"] = Vector3.new(-1, 0.5, 0),
				Head = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
			}

			local function func21(arg)
				local autoClickerTransparency = configValues.AutoClickerTransparency

				for _, value22 in ipairs(arg.Character:QueryDescendants("BasePart")) do
					if arg.Transparency[value22] == nil then
						arg.Transparency[value22] = value22.LocalTransparencyModifier
					end

					value22.LocalTransparencyModifier = autoClickerTransparency
				end

				arg.TransparencyValue = autoClickerTransparency
			end

			local function func22(arg)
				local value22 = weakCache[arg]
				weakCache[arg] = nil

				if value22 then
					if value22.TransparencyConnection then
						value22.TransparencyConnection:Disconnect()
					end

					for k, value23 in value22.Transparency, nil, nil do
						if k.Parent then
							k.LocalTransparencyModifier = value23
						end
					end
				end

				local character = value22 and value22.Character or api.Char(arg)
				local torso = character and character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
				local autoClickerBP = torso and torso:FindFirstChild("AutoClickerBP")
				torso = torso and torso:FindFirstChild("AutoClickerBG")

				if autoClickerBP then
					autoClickerBP:Destroy()
				end

				if torso then
					torso:Destroy()
				end

				if character then
					for _, value23 in ipairs(table17) do
						local value24 = character:FindFirstChild(value23)

						if value24 then
							if value23 == "Torso" or value23 == "Head" then
								value24.CanCollide = true
							end

							local ballSocketConstraint = value24:FindFirstChild("BallSocketConstraint")

							if ballSocketConstraint then
								ballSocketConstraint.LimitsEnabled = true
								ballSocketConstraint.TwistLimitsEnabled = true
								local value25 = table18[value23]

								if value25 then
									ballSocketConstraint.Attachment1.Position = value25
								end
							end

							local autoClickerBG = value24:FindFirstChild("AutoClickerBG")

							if autoClickerBG then
								autoClickerBG:Destroy()
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			api.CleanupAutoClicker = function()
				for k in weakCache, nil, nil do
					func22(k)
				end
			end

			while task.wait() do
				local selectedPlayersAC = sharedState.SelectedPlayersAC

				if unloaded then
					for k in selectedPlayersAC, nil, nil do
						func22(k)
					end

					return
				end

				for k in table16, nil, nil do
					if not selectedPlayersAC[k] then
						func22(k)
						table16[k] = nil
					end
				end

				if not featureState.AutoClicker then
					for k in selectedPlayersAC, nil, nil do
						func22(k)
					end

					if not api.WaitForRuntimeToggle("AutoClicker") then
						return
					end
				end

				local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
				local torso = value22 and value22:FindFirstChild("Torso")
				local selectedACPoseCenter

				if featureState.CustomACPoseCenter then
					selectedACPoseCenter = configValues.SelectedACPoseCenter
				else
					selectedACPoseCenter = localPlayer
				end

				local torso2, value25, value26

				if selectedACPoseCenter == localPlayer then
					torso2 = value22
					value25 = value23
					value26 = value24
				else
					torso2, value25, value26 = api.Char(selectedACPoseCenter)
				end

				torso2 = torso2 and torso2:FindFirstChild("Torso")

				if featureState.AutoClicker then
					local head = value22 and value22:FindFirstChild("Head")

					if head and head.CanCollide then
						head.CanCollide = false
					end

					if torso and torso.CanCollide then
						torso.CanCollide = false
					end
				end

				for k in selectedPlayersAC, nil, nil do
					if value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0 and torso then
						if value25 and value26 and value26.Health > 0 and torso2 then
							if k == selectedACPoseCenter then
								if table16[k] then
									func22(k)
									table16[k] = nil
								end
							else
								table16[k] = true
								local value27, value28, value29 = api.Char(k)
								local table19 = weakCache[k]

								if table19 and table19.Character ~= value27 then
									func22(k)
									table19 = nil
								end

								local torso3 = table19 and table19.Character == value27 and table19.Parts.Torso or value27 and value27:FindFirstChild("Torso")

								if value29 and torso3 then
									local value30, value31, value32 = api.GetACPose(k)

									if not table19 or table19.Character ~= value27 then
										table19 = {
											Character = value27,
											Gyros = {},
											GyroTargets = {},
											Parts = {},
											RagdollParts = {},
											Sockets = {},
											SocketAttachments = {},
											LimbOffsets = {},
											RotationValues = {},
											RotationFrames = {},
											Transparency = {},
										}

										weakCache[k] = table19
										local value33 = table19
										local value34 = k

										value33.TransparencyConnection = value27.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
											if weakCache[value34] ~= value33 or not descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
												return
											end
											value33.Transparency[descendant] = descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier
											descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = configValues.AutoClickerTransparency
										end)

										local head = value22:FindFirstChild("Head")

										if head and head.CanCollide then
											head.CanCollide = false
										end

										if torso.CanCollide then
											torso.CanCollide = false
										end

										for _, value35 in value27:GetChildren() do
											if value35:IsA("BasePart") then
												if value35.CanCollide then
													value35.CanCollide = false
												end

												local ragdollLimbPart = value35:FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart")

												if ragdollLimbPart and ragdollLimbPart.CanCollide then
													ragdollLimbPart.CanCollide = false
												end
											end
										end

										for _, value35 in ipairs(table17) do
											local value36 = value27:FindFirstChild(value35)

											if value36 then
												table19.Parts[value35] = value36
												table19.RagdollParts[value35] = value36:FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart")
												local ballSocketConstraint = value36:FindFirstChild("BallSocketConstraint")
												table19.Sockets[value35] = ballSocketConstraint
												table19.SocketAttachments[value35] = ballSocketConstraint and ballSocketConstraint.Attachment1
											end
										end
									end

									if table19.TransparencyValue ~= configValues.AutoClickerTransparency then
										func21(table19)
									end

									local bodyPosition = table19.BodyPosition

									if not bodyPosition or bodyPosition.Parent ~= torso3 then
										bodyPosition = torso3:FindFirstChild("AutoClickerBP") or Instance.new("BodyPosition")
										bodyPosition.Name = "AutoClickerBP"
										bodyPosition.D = math.clamp(configValues.ACBodyDamping or 850, 50, 2000)
										bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
										bodyPosition.Parent = torso3
										table19.BodyPosition = bodyPosition
										table19.BodyTarget = nil
									end

									local position

									if featureState.HoldACPosition then
										if not table19.HeldPosition then
											table19.HeldPosition = torso3.Position
										end

										position = table19.HeldPosition
									else
										table19.HeldPosition = nil
										position = (value25.CFrame * value30).Position
									end

									bodyPosition.D = math.clamp(configValues.ACBodyDamping or 850, 50, 2000)

									if table19.BodyTarget ~= position then
										bodyPosition.Position = position
										table19.BodyTarget = position
									end

									local cframe = CFrame.lookAt(torso3.Position, Vector3.new(torso2.Position.X, torso3.Position.Y, torso2.Position.Z))

									for _, value33 in ipairs(table17) do
										local value34 = table19.Parts[value33]

										if not value34 or value34.Parent ~= value27 then
											value34 = value27:FindFirstChild(value33)
											table19.Parts[value33] = value34
											table19.RagdollParts[value33] = value34 and value34:FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart")
											table19.Sockets[value33] = value34 and value34:FindFirstChild("BallSocketConstraint")
											table19.SocketAttachments[value33] = table19.Sockets[value33] and table19.Sockets[value33].Attachment1
											table19.Gyros[value33] = nil
											table19.GyroTargets[value33] = nil
											table19.LimbOffsets[value33] = nil
										end

										if value34 then
											if value34.CanCollide then
												value34.CanCollide = false
											end

											local ragdollLimbPart = table19.RagdollParts[value33]

											if not ragdollLimbPart or not ragdollLimbPart.Parent then
												ragdollLimbPart = value34:FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart")
												table19.RagdollParts[value33] = ragdollLimbPart
											end

											if ragdollLimbPart and ragdollLimbPart.CanCollide then
												ragdollLimbPart.CanCollide = false
											end

											local ballSocketConstraint = table19.Sockets[value33]

											if not ballSocketConstraint or not ballSocketConstraint.Parent then
												ballSocketConstraint = value34:FindFirstChild("BallSocketConstraint")
												table19.Sockets[value33] = ballSocketConstraint
												table19.SocketAttachments[value33] = ballSocketConstraint and ballSocketConstraint.Attachment1
												table19.LimbOffsets[value33] = nil
											end

											if ballSocketConstraint then
												if ballSocketConstraint.LimitsEnabled then
													ballSocketConstraint.LimitsEnabled = false
												end

												if ballSocketConstraint.TwistLimitsEnabled then
													ballSocketConstraint.TwistLimitsEnabled = false
												end

												local value35 = value32[value33]
												local value36 = table19.SocketAttachments[value33]

												if value35 and value36 and table19.LimbOffsets[value33] ~= value35 then
													value36.Position = value35
													table19.LimbOffsets[value33] = value35
												end
											end

											local autoClickerBG = table19.Gyros[value33]

											if not autoClickerBG or autoClickerBG.Parent ~= value34 then
												autoClickerBG = value34:FindFirstChild("AutoClickerBG") or Instance.new("BodyGyro")
												autoClickerBG.Name = "AutoClickerBG"
												autoClickerBG.D = math.clamp(configValues.ACLimbDamping or 500, 50, 2000)
												autoClickerBG.P = 7500
												autoClickerBG.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
												autoClickerBG.Parent = value34
												table19.Gyros[value33] = autoClickerBG
												table19.GyroTargets[value33] = nil
											end

											local value35 = value31[value33]

											if table19.RotationValues[value33] ~= value35 then
												table19.RotationValues[value33] = value35
												table19.RotationFrames[value33] = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(value35.X), math.rad(value35.Y), math.rad(value35.Z))
											end

											local value36 = table19.RotationFrames[value33]
											local cFrame2

											if value33 == "Torso" then
												cFrame2 = cframe * value36
											else
												cFrame2 = torso3.CFrame * value36
											end

											if table19.GyroTargets[value33] ~= cFrame2 then
												autoClickerBG.CFrame = cFrame2
												table19.GyroTargets[value33] = cFrame2
											end
										end
									end

									local num36 = value23.Position - torso3.Position

									if num36:Dot(num36) < 900 or api.SNO(torso3) then
										api.Grab(torso3)
										local ragdolled = value29:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")

										if featureState.RagdollInAC and ragdolled and not ragdolled.Value then
											task.spawn(api.Ragdoll, value27)
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		do
			local weakCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

			local function func21()
				return featureState.ImmunityAntiGrab or featureState.ImmunityAntiExplosion
			end

			local function func22(arg, arg2)
				local table16 = weakCache[arg]

				if not table16 or table16.Root ~= arg2 then
					table16 = { Root = arg2, CFrame = arg2.CFrame }
					weakCache[arg] = table16
				end

				return table16
			end

			local function func23(arg)
				local root = arg.Root
				local cFrame2 = arg.CFrame
				local restoring = arg.Restoring

				if not restoring then
					restoring = not (root and root.Parent and cFrame2)
				end

				if restoring then
					return
				end
				arg.Restoring = true
				arg.OwnGrab = true

				task.spawn(function()
					pcall(function()
						if api.SNO(root, 0.4) then
							for i = 1, 3 do
								if root.Parent then
									root.CFrame = cFrame2
									api.ResetVelocity(root)
									RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
									continue
								end

								break
							end

							api.RNO(root)
						end
					end)

					arg.Restoring = false
				end)
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				while task.wait() do
					if unloaded then
						return
					end

					if not func21() then
						table.clear(weakCache)
						if not api.WaitForRuntimeCondition("ImmunityPosition", func21) then
							return
						end
					end

					for k in sharedState.ImmunePlayers, nil, nil do
						local head, value22, value23 = api.Char(k)
						head = head and head:FindFirstChild("Head")
						head = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
						local value = head and head:IsA("StringValue") and head.Value
						local inPlot2 = k:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

						if value22 and value23 and value23.Health > 0 and inPlot2 and not inPlot2.Value then
							local value24 = func22(k, value22)

							if value and value ~= "" then
								if value ~= localPlayer.Name and not value24.OwnGrab and not value24.Held and featureState.ImmunityAntiGrab then
									value24.Held = true
									func23(value24)
								end
							elseif value24.Held then
								local lockUntil = os.clock() + 0.35
								value24.Held = false
								value24.LockUntil = lockUntil
								func23(value24)
							elseif value24.OwnGrab then
								if not value24.Restoring then
									value24.OwnGrab = nil
								end
							elseif os.clock() >= (value24.LockUntil or 0) and not value24.Restoring then
								value24.CFrame = value22.CFrame
							end
						end
					end

					for k in weakCache, nil, nil do
						if not (k.Parent and sharedState.ImmunePlayers[k]) then
							weakCache[k] = nil
						end
					end
				end
			end)

			uiLib:GiveSignal(workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				if unloaded or not featureState.ImmunityAntiExplosion or not child:IsA("BasePart") or child.Name ~= "Part" then
					return
				end

				for k in sharedState.ImmunePlayers, nil, nil do
					local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char(k)
					local inPlot2 = k:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

					if value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0 and inPlot2 and not inPlot2.Value and (child.Position - value23.Position).Magnitude <= 20 then
						local value25 = func22(k, value23)
						value25.LockUntil = os.clock() + 0.5
						func23(value25)
					end
				end
			end))
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			while task.wait(0.03) do
				if unloaded then
					return
				end

				if not featureState.ImmunityAntiKick then
					for k, value22 in sharedState.ImmuneShurikens, nil, nil do
						api.DestroyToy(value22)
						sharedState.ImmuneShurikens[k] = nil
					end

					if not api.WaitForRuntimeToggle("ImmunityAntiKick") then
						return
					end
				end

				for k in sharedState.ImmunePlayers, nil, nil do
					local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()
					local value25, ragdollTouchedHitbox, value26 = api.Char(k)
					ragdollTouchedHitbox = ragdollTouchedHitbox and ragdollTouchedHitbox:FindFirstChild("RagdollTouchedHitbox")
					local inPlot2 = k:FindFirstChild("InPlot")

					if not (not value25 or not value23 or not value24 or value24.Health == 0 or not ragdollTouchedHitbox or inPlot2 and inPlot2.Value) then
						local value27 = sharedState.ImmuneShurikens[k]
						local stickyWeld = value27 and value27:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") and value27.StickyPart:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld")

						if not (stickyWeld and stickyWeld.Part1 == ragdollTouchedHitbox) then
							api.DestroyToy(sharedState.ImmuneShurikens[k])
							local NinjaShuriken = api.SpawnToy("NinjaShuriken")
							local stickyPart = NinjaShuriken and NinjaShuriken:WaitForChild("StickyPart", 1.5)
							local stickyWeld2 = stickyPart and stickyPart:WaitForChild("StickyWeld", 1.5)

							if not (stickyPart and stickyWeld2) or not api.SNO(stickyPart) then
								api.DestroyToy(NinjaShuriken)
							else
								task.spawn(function()
									local touchInterest = stickyPart:WaitForChild("TouchInterest", 2.5)

									if touchInterest then
										touchInterest:Destroy()
									end
								end)

								remotes.StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, ragdollTouchedHitbox, CFrame.new(configValues.AKOffsetX, configValues.AKOffsetY, configValues.AKOffsetZ) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(configValues.AKRotX), math.rad(configValues.AKRotY), math.rad(configValues.AKRotZ)))
								local now3 = tick()

								while true do
									waitAtLeast()
									if not (stickyWeld2.Part1 == ragdollTouchedHitbox or not stickyWeld2.Parent or not ragdollTouchedHitbox.Parent or not value26.Parent or value26.Health == 0 or tick() - now3 > 0.5) then
										continue
									end
									break
								end

								sharedState.ImmuneShurikens[k] = NinjaShuriken
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			local table16 = {}

			local function func21(arg, arg2)
				if not arg or not featureState.EKI then
					return
				end

				return sethiddenproperty and pcall(function()
					sethiddenproperty(arg, "PhysicsRepRootPart", arg2)
				end)
			end

			local function func22(arg)
				if not arg then
					return
				end
				local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char(arg)

				if value23 then
					local kickAP = value23:FindFirstChild("KickAP")
					local kickAO = value23:FindFirstChild("KickAO")
					local weldHRP = value23:FindFirstChild("WeldHRP")

					if kickAP then
						kickAP:Destroy()
					end

					if kickAO then
						kickAO:Destroy()
					end

					if weldHRP and value24:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled") and value24.Ragdolled.Value then
						weldHRP.Enabled = true
					end

					if api.IsNetworkOwner(value23) then
						func21(value23, nil)
					end
				end

				if value22 then
					task.spawn(api.ToggleMotor6D, value22, true, true)
					task.spawn(api.ToggleCollisions, value22, true)
				end
			end

			local function func23(arg, arg2, arg3)
				local nkOffset = configValues.NKOffset

				if arg3 <= 1 then
					return arg.Position + Vector3.new(0, nkOffset, 0)
				end

				local num36 = 6.2831853071795862 / arg3 * (arg2 - 1)
				return arg.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(num36) * 10, nkOffset, math.sin(num36) * 10)
			end

			while task.wait() do
				if unloaded then
					for k in table16, nil, nil do
						func22(k)
					end

					return
				end

				if not featureState.LoopApplyMethodT then
					for k in table16, nil, nil do
						func22(k)
						table16[k] = nil
					end

					if not api.WaitForRuntimeToggle("LoopApplyMethodT") then
						return
					end
				end

				local loopApplyMethodT = featureState.LoopApplyMethodT
				local selectedPlayers = sharedState.SelectedPlayers
				local targetMethods = sharedState.TargetMethods

				for k in table16, nil, nil do
					if k and (not k.Parent or not targetMethods.Kick or inPlot.Value or not loopApplyMethodT or not selectedPlayers[k]) then
						func22(k)
						table16[k] = nil
					end
				end

				if not (not loopApplyMethodT or not targetMethods.Kick or inPlot.Value) then
					local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()

					if value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0 then
						local table17 = {}

						for k in selectedPlayers, nil, nil do
							if k and k.Parent and k:IsA("Player") then
								table.insert(table17, k)
								table16[k] = true
							end
						end

						if #table17 ~= 0 then
							for i, value25 in ipairs(table17) do
								if value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0 and not inPlot.Value then
									local value26, value27, value28 = api.Char(value25)
									local torso = value26 and value26:FindFirstChild("Torso")

									if not (value27 and value28 and torso and value28.Health > 0) then
										func22(value25)
									else
										local value29 = func23(value23, i, #table17)
										local kickAP = value27:FindFirstChild("KickAP")
										local kickAO = value27:FindFirstChild("KickAO")
										local weldHRP = value27:FindFirstChild("WeldHRP")

										if not kickAP or not kickAO then
											if kickAP then
												kickAP:Destroy()
											end

											if kickAO then
												kickAO:Destroy()
											end

											kickAP = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
											local bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
											kickAP.D = 200
											kickAP.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
											kickAP.Position = value29
											kickAP.Parent = value27
											bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
											bodyGyro.D = 200
											bodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.new()
											bodyGyro.Parent = value27
											kickAP.Name = "KickAP"
											bodyGyro.Name = "KickAO"
										end

										if weldHRP then
											weldHRP.Enabled = false
										end

										kickAP.Position = value29
										local state12

										if not (api.GetMagnitude(value23, value27) > 33) then
											api.Grab(value27)
											state12 = configValues.NKDelayMode == "Faster but worse" or not configValues.NKDelayMode

											if state12 then
												task.delay(0.2, api.RNO, value27)
											elseif configValues.NKDelayMode == "Normal" then
												task.spawn(function()
													task.wait()
													api.RNO(value27)
												end)
											end

											if featureState.RagdollInNK then
												task.spawn(api.Ragdoll, value26)
											end

											if featureState.DeleteShurInNK then
												task.spawn(api.RemoveSticky, value26)
											end

											api.ToggleCollisions(value26, false)
											api.RepositionLimbs(value26, true)

											task.delay(0.6, function()
												if featureState.LoopApplyMethodT and sharedState.TargetMethods.Kick and sharedState.SelectedPlayers[value25] and api.IsNetworkOwner(value27) then
													func21(value27, value27)
												end
											end)
										elseif api.SNO(value27) then
											api.Teleport(value27, value29)
											api.Grab(value27)
											state12 = configValues.NKDelayMode == "Faster but worse" or not configValues.NKDelayMode

											if state12 then
												task.delay(0.2, api.RNO, value27)
											elseif configValues.NKDelayMode == "Normal" then
												task.spawn(function()
													task.wait()
													api.RNO(value27)
												end)
											end

											if featureState.RagdollInNK then
												task.spawn(api.Ragdoll, value26)
											end

											if featureState.DeleteShurInNK then
												task.spawn(api.RemoveSticky, value26)
											end

											api.ToggleCollisions(value26, false)
											api.RepositionLimbs(value26, true)

											task.delay(0.6, function()
												if featureState.LoopApplyMethodT and sharedState.TargetMethods.Kick and sharedState.SelectedPlayers[value25] and api.IsNetworkOwner(value27) then
													func21(value27, value27)
												end
											end)
										else
											func21(value27, nil)
										end
									end
								end
							end

							if configValues.NKDelayMode == "Normal" then
								task.wait()
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)

			while Watermark and Watermark.Label and Watermark.Label.Parent do
				if unloaded then
					return
				end

				local updateSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(configValues.WatermarkUpdateSpeed) or 1, 0.1, 5)
				local smoothing = math.clamp(tonumber(configValues.WatermarkSmoothing) or 0.2, 0, 0.9)
				local dataPing = Stats2.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]
				watermarkFPS = Stats2.Workspace.Heartbeat:GetValue() * (1 - smoothing) + watermarkFPS * smoothing
				watermarkPing = dataPing:GetValue() * (1 - smoothing) + watermarkPing * smoothing

				local watermarkParts = {}

				if not toggles.WatermarkVersion or toggles.WatermarkVersion.Value then
					table.insert(watermarkParts, "Resonance v2.10")
				end

				if not toggles.WatermarkFPS or toggles.WatermarkFPS.Value then
					table.insert(watermarkParts, ("%d fps"):format(math.floor(watermarkFPS + 0.5)))
				end

				if not toggles.WatermarkPing or toggles.WatermarkPing.Value then
					table.insert(watermarkParts, ("%d ms"):format(math.floor(watermarkPing + 0.5)))
				end

				if toggles.WatermarkTime and toggles.WatermarkTime.Value then
					table.insert(watermarkParts, os.date("%H:%M:%S"))
				end

				local watermarkText = table.concat(watermarkParts, " | ")

				if watermarkText ~= watermarkLastText then
					watermarkLastText = watermarkText
					Watermark.SetText(Watermark, watermarkText)
				end

				task.wait(updateSpeed)
			end
		end)

			for _, value22 in toggles, nil, nil do
				if value22 and value22.Value then
					num36 += 1
				end
			end

			if uiLib.Labels.TogglesCurrentlyOn then
				local togglesCurrentlyOn = uiLib.Labels.TogglesCurrentlyOn
				local setText = togglesCurrentlyOn.SetText
				local text5 = ("Enabled toggles: <b>%*</b>"):format(num36)
				setText(togglesCurrentlyOn, text5)
			end
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			local value22 = nil
			local value23 = nil
			local value24 = nil
			local value38 = nil
			local value39 = nil

			while task.wait(0.1) and Watermark and Watermark.Label and Watermark.Label.Parent do
				if unloaded then
					return
				end
				local value25 = os.date("%H:%M")
				local blackHoles = configValues.BlackHoles
				local num36 = math.floor(tick() - now2)

				if value25 ~= value22 then
					local currentTime = uiLib.Labels.CurrentTime
					local setText = currentTime.SetText
					local text5 = ("Local time: <b>%*</b>"):format(value25)
					setText(currentTime, text5)
					value22 = value25
				end

				if blackHoles ~= value23 then
					local labelHi = uiLib.Labels.LabelHi
					local setText = labelHi.SetText
					local text5 = ("Kicked players: <b>%*</b>"):format(blackHoles)
					setText(labelHi, text5)
					value23 = blackHoles
				end

				if num36 ~= value24 then
					local spentTime = uiLib.Labels.SpentTime
					local setText = spentTime.SetText
					local text5 = ("Session time: <b>%dm %ds</b>"):format(num36 // 60, num36 % 60)
					setText(spentTime, text5)
					value24 = num36
				end

				local serverPlayers = #Players:GetPlayers() .. "/" .. tostring(Players.MaxPlayers)

				if serverPlayers ~= value38 and uiLib.Labels.ServerPlayers then
					local serverPlayersLabel = uiLib.Labels.ServerPlayers
					local setText = serverPlayersLabel.SetText
					setText(serverPlayersLabel, ("Server players: <b>%*</b>"):format(serverPlayers))
					value38 = serverPlayers
				end

				local serverHops = sharedState.ServerHop.Attempts or 0

				if serverHops ~= value39 and uiLib.Labels.ServerHopsCount then
					local serverHopsLabel = uiLib.Labels.ServerHopsCount
					local setText = serverHopsLabel.SetText
					setText(serverHopsLabel, ("Server hops: <b>%d</b>"):format(serverHops))
					value39 = serverHops
				end
			end
		end)

				["Moon Dance"] = "rbxassetid://45834924",
				["Full Punch"] = "rbxassetid://204062532",
				["Full Swing"] = "rbxassetid://218504594",
				["Arm Turbine"] = "rbxassetid://259438880",
				["Barrel Roll"] = "rbxassetid://136801964",
				["Arm Detach"] = "rbxassetid://33169583",
				["Insane Arms"] = "rbxassetid://27432691",
			}

			local table17 = {}

			for k, value22 in pairs(table16) do
				table.insert(table17, k)
				sharedState.AnimationWhitelist[value22:match("%d+")] = true
			end

			table.sort(table17)

			local AnimationToPlay = Animations:AddDropdown("AnimationToPlay", {
				Values = table17,
				Default = 1,
				Multi = false,
				Searchable = true,
				Text = "Animation to Play",
			})

			local AnimS = Animations:AddSlider("AnimS", { Text = "Animation Speed", Default = 1, Min = 0, Max = 100 })
			local value22 = nil
			local text5 = "Struggle"

			local function func21()
				if value22 then
					value22:Stop()
					value22:Destroy()
					value22 = nil
				end
			end

			local function func22(arg)
				func21()
				if not (toggles.AnimationToggle and toggles.AnimationToggle.Value) then
					return
				end
				local character = localPlayer.Character
				if not character then
					return
				end
				local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if not humanoid then
					return
				end
				local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
				if not animator then
					return
				end
				local value23 = table16[arg]
				if not value23 then
					return
				end
				local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
				animation.AnimationId = value23
				value22 = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
				value22.Looped = true
				value22:Play()
				value22:AdjustSpeed(AnimS.Value)
				text5 = arg
			end

			local function func23()
				if text5 then
					func22(text5)
				end
			end

			Animations:AddToggle("AnimationToggle", {
				Text = "Toggle Animation",
				Default = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					if not arg then
						func21()
					else
						func23()
					end
				end,
			})

			AnimS:OnChanged(function()
				if value22 then
					value22:AdjustSpeed(AnimS.Value)
				end
			end)

			AnimationToPlay:OnChanged(function(arg)
				text5 = arg

				if toggles.AnimationToggle and toggles.AnimationToggle.Value then
					func22(AnimationToPlay.Value)
				end
			end)

			localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
				task.wait(1)

				if toggles.AnimationToggle and toggles.AnimationToggle.Value then
					func21()
					func22(AnimationToPlay.Value)
				end
			end)
		end
	end

			Text = "Anti Paint",
			Default = false,
			Tooltip = "You won't get painted",
			Callback = function(antiPaint)
				featureState.AntiPaint = antiPaint

				task.spawn(function()
					local table16 = {}

					local function func21(descendant)
						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.Name == "PaintPlayerPart" then
							descendant.CanTouch = not antiPaint

							if antiPaint then
								table16[descendant] = descendant:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanTouch"):Connect(function()
									if not featureState.AntiPaint then
										return
									end
									descendant.CanTouch = false
								end)
							end
						end
					end

					for _, value20 in pairs(workspace:QueryDescendants("BasePart#PaintPlayerPart")) do
						func21(value20)
					end

					if antiPaint then
						local connection = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(func21)

						repeat
							task.wait()
						until not featureState.AntiPaint

						connection:Disconnect()

						for _, value20 in pairs(table16) do
							value20:Disconnect()
						end
					end
				end)
			end,
		})

		Antis:AddToggle("AntiPoison", {
			Text = "Anti Poison",
			Default = false,
			Tooltip = "Poison parts won't kill you",
			Callback = function(arg)
				local hole = map:FindFirstChild("Hole")
				local poisonBigHole = hole and hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonBigHole")
				hole = hole and hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonSmallHole")

				for _, value20 in pairs({ poisonBigHole, hole }) do
					if value20 then
						local poisonHurtPart = value20:FindFirstChild("PoisonHurtPart")
						local paintPlayerPart = value20:FindFirstChild("PaintPlayerPart")

						if poisonHurtPart then
							poisonHurtPart.CanTouch = not arg
						end

						if paintPlayerPart then
							paintPlayerPart.CanTouch = not arg
						end
					end
				end
			end,
		})

		local antiLagCharConn = nil

		local function setRakNetPacketFilter(filterOn)
			if typeof(getconnections) ~= "function" then
				return
			end

			for _, value20 in ipairs({ remotes.Packet, remotes.CreateGrabLine }) do
				if value20 then
					local ok, connections = pcall(getconnections, value20.OnClientEvent)

					if ok and connections then
						for _, value21 in ipairs(connections) do
							if value21.Disable and value21.Function then
								local isOurs = false

								for _, value22 in ipairs(sharedState.PacketDetectCallbacks or {}) do
									if value21.Function == value22 then
										isOurs = true
										break
									end
								end

								if not isOurs then
									if filterOn then
										value21:Disable()
									elseif value21.Enable then
										value21:Enable()
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local function applyDeepAntiLag(antiLagOn)
			local playerScripts = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerScripts")

			setRakNetPacketFilter(antiLagOn)

			if not playerScripts then
				return
			end

			for _, descendant in ipairs(playerScripts:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("LocalScript") and (descendant.Name == "StickyPartsTouchDetection" or descendant.Name:lower():find("sticky") or descendant.Name:lower():find("touchdetect")) then
					descendant.Enabled = not antiLagOn
				end
			end
		end

		local function stopDeepAntiLag()
			if antiLagCharConn then
				antiLagCharConn:Disconnect()
				antiLagCharConn = nil
			end
		end

		local function startDeepAntiLag()
			applyDeepAntiLag(true)
			stopDeepAntiLag()
			antiLagCharConn = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
				task.wait(1)

				if unloaded or not (toggles.AntiLag and toggles.AntiLag.Value and configValues.AntiLagMode == "New") then
					return
				end

				applyDeepAntiLag(true)
			end)
		end

		Antis:AddToggle("AntiLag", {
			Text = "Anti Lag",
			Default = true,
			Tooltip = "Anti Line-Lag & Sticky-Lag",
			Callback = function(arg)
				local enabled = not arg
				if enabled and sharedState.LookFigures.Enabled then
					toggles.AntiLag:SetValue(true)
					return
				end
				localPlayer.PlayerScripts.CharacterAndBeamMove.Enabled = enabled
				localPlayer.PlayerScripts.StickyPartsTouchDetection.Enabled = enabled
				api.SetAntiShurikenLag(arg)

				stopDeepAntiLag()

				if configValues.AntiLagMode == "New" then
					if arg then
						startDeepAntiLag()
					else
						applyDeepAntiLag(false)
					end
				end

				if arg then
					task.delay(1.5, function()
						if unloaded or not toggles.AntiLag.Value then
							return
						end

						for _, value20 in pairs(sharedState.PlayerList) do
							local value21 = api.Char(value20)

							if value21 then
								for _, child in pairs(value21:GetChildren()) do
									if child.Name == "GrabParts" then
										child:Destroy()
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end
			end,
		}):AddKeyPicker("AntiLagK", { Text = "Anti Lag", Default = nil, Mode = "Toggle", NoUI = false, SyncToggleState = true })

		Antis:AddDropdown("AntiLagMode", {
			Values = { "Default", "New" },
			ValueImages = {
				Default = "circle",
				New = "zap",
			},
			Default = 1,
			Text = "Anti Lag Mode",
			Tooltip = "New also disables every sticky and touch detection script in PlayerScripts, keeps them disabled after respawn and protects you from packet lag via RakNet",
			Callback = function(mode)
				configValues.AntiLagMode = mode

				if not (toggles.AntiLag and toggles.AntiLag.Value) then
					return
				end

				if mode == "New" then
					startDeepAntiLag()
				else
					stopDeepAntiLag()
					applyDeepAntiLag(false)
					localPlayer.PlayerScripts.CharacterAndBeamMove.Enabled = false
					localPlayer.PlayerScripts.StickyPartsTouchDetection.Enabled = false
				end
			end,
		})

		Antis:AddToggle("AutoAntiLag", {
			Text = "Auto Anti Lag",
			Default = true,
			Tooltip = "Enables anti-lag for you if too low fps",
			Callback = function(autoAntiLag)
				featureState.AutoAntiLag = autoAntiLag
			end,
		})

		Antis:AddToggle("AntiInvis", {
			Text = "Anti Invis",
			Default = false,
			Tooltip = "Makes any invisible players visible",
			Callback = function()
				task.spawn(function()
					while toggles.AntiInvis and toggles.AntiInvis.Value do
						for _, value20 in sharedState.PlayerList, nil, nil do
							if value20 ~= localPlayer then
								local value21, value22, value23 = api.Char(value20)

								if value22 then
									value22.Massless = false
								end

								if value23 and value23.Sit and value23.SeatPart then
									local seatPart = value23.SeatPart
									local seatWeld = seatPart:FindFirstChild("SeatWeld")

									if seatPart.Parent and seatWeld and not seatWeld.Enabled then
										seatWeld.Enabled = true
									end
								end
							end
						end

						task.wait(0.1)
					end
				end)
			end,
		})

		Antis:AddToggle("AutoReset", {
			Text = "Auto Reset",
			Default = true,
			Tooltip = "Checks for kick frame and resets when needed",
			Callback = function(autoReset)
				featureState.AutoReset = autoReset
			end,
		})

		Antis:AddToggle("LoopTP", {
			Text = "Loop TP",
			Default = false,
			Tooltip = "Teleports you infinitely so you're unable to be grabbed",
			Callback = function(loopTP)
				featureState.LoopTP = loopTP

				task.spawn(function()
					local value20
					value20, value20 = api.Char()
					local cFrame2

					if value20 then
						cFrame2 = value20.CFrame
					else
						cFrame2 = CFrame.new(0, -5, 0)
					end

					while featureState.LoopTP and waitAtLeast(0.04) do
						local value21 = api.Char()

						if value21 then
							local random = math.random
							local cframe = CFrame.new(math.random(-1000, 1000), -7.35, random(-1000, 1000))
							api.PivotTo(value21, cframe)
						end
					end

					local value21 = api.Char()

					if value21 then
						api.PivotTo(value21, cFrame2)
					end
				end)
			end,
		})
	end

		Miscellaneous:AddToggle("WaterWalk", {
			Text = "Water Walk",
			Default = false,
			Tooltip = "Walk on water",
			Callback = function(arg)
				for _, child in pairs(alwaysHereTweenedObjects.Ocean.Object.ObjectModel:GetChildren()) do
					local table16 = sharedState.WaterPartStates[child]

					if arg and not table16 then
						table16 = { child.CanCollide, child.Transparency }
						sharedState.WaterPartStates[child] = table16
					end

					if arg then
						child.CanCollide = true

						if child.Transparency ~= 1 and table16[2] ~= 1 then
							child.Transparency = 0.5
						end
					elseif table16 then
						child.CanCollide = table16[1]
						local transparency

						if toggles.CustomWaterT and toggles.CustomWaterT.Value then
							transparency = 1
						else
							transparency = table16[2]
						end

						child.Transparency = transparency

						if not (toggles.CustomWaterT and toggles.CustomWaterT.Value) then
							sharedState.WaterPartStates[child] = nil
						end
					end
				end
			end,
		})

		Miscellaneous:AddToggle("DeleteBarriers", {
			Text = "Noclip Barrier",
			Default = false,
			Callback = function(arg)
				local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
				if not plots then
					return
				end

				for _, child in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
					local barrier = child:FindFirstChild("Barrier")

					if barrier then
						for _, child2 in ipairs(barrier:GetChildren()) do
							if child2:IsA("BasePart") and child2.Name == "PlotBarrier" then
								child2.CanCollide = not arg
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end,
		})

		Miscellaneous:AddToggle("OtherInvCollisions", {
			Text = "Anti-Fling [Objects Noclip]",
			Default = false,
			Callback = function(arg)
				if arg then
					task.spawn(function()
						while task.wait(0.1) and toggles.OtherInvCollisions.Value and not unloaded do
							for _, value20 in api.Char():QueryDescendants("BasePart") do
								PhysicsService:SetPartCollisionGroup(value20, "PlotPlayers")
							end
						end

							if not localPlayer.InOwnedPlot.Value then
								text5 = "Players"
							else
								text5 = "PlotPlayers"
							end

							setPartCollisionGroup(value21, value20, text5)
						end
					end)
				end
			end,
		})

	do
		local Highlight = tabPages.Invincibility:AddRightTabbox("Highlight", "highlighter")

		local PositionsTab = Highlight:AddTab("Positions", "move-diagonal")
		local PresetsTab = Highlight:AddTab("Presets", "settings-2")

		local applyAntiKickOffsets = function()
			local character, root, humanoid = api.Char()

			if not (character and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0) then
				return
			end

			local hitbox = character:FindFirstChild("RagdollTouchedHitbox")

			if not hitbox then
				return
			end

			local cframe = CFrame.new(configValues.AKOffsetX, configValues.AKOffsetY, configValues.AKOffsetZ) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(configValues.AKRotX), math.rad(configValues.AKRotY), math.rad(configValues.AKRotZ))

			for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
				if part:IsA("BasePart") then
				for _, connected in ipairs(part:GetConnectedParts()) do
					local weld = connected:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld")

					if weld and weld.Part1 == hitbox then
						remotes.StickyPartEvent:FireServer(connected, hitbox, cframe)
					end
				end
			end
			end
		end

		local reanchorAntiKickToy = function()
			local character, root, humanoid = api.Char()

			if not character then
				return
			end

			local hitbox = character:FindFirstChild("RagdollTouchedHitbox")

			if not hitbox then
				return
			end

			for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
				if part:IsA("BasePart") then
				for _, connected in ipairs(part:GetConnectedParts()) do
					local weld = connected:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld")

					if weld and weld.Part1 == hitbox then
						local model = connected:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

						if model then
							api.DestroyToy(model)
						else
							weld:Destroy()
						end

						return
					end
				end
			end
			end
		end

		PositionsTab:AddDropdown("AKToy", {

				["Shuriken"] = "star",
				["Katana"] = "sword",
				["Kunai"] = "wand",
				["Cleaver"] = "utensils",
				["Rusty Fork"] = "utensils",
				["Pencil"] = "pencil",
				["Pickaxe"] = "hammer",
				["Any Sticky Toy"] = "sticker",
			},
			Default = "Shuriken",
			Multi = false,
			Text = "Toy",

				configValues.AKType = akToy == "Any Sticky Toy" and "Any" or akToy

					reanchorAntiKickToy()
				end
			end,
		})

		PresetsTab:AddDropdown("AKPreset", {

			ValueImages = {
				["Pickaxe on Back"] = "hammer",

		PresetsTab:AddButton({
			Text = "Load Preset",
			Icon = "settings",
			Func = function()
				local akPreset = configValues.AKPresetChoice or (options.AKPreset and options.AKPreset.Value)

				local akPresets = {
					["Pickaxe on Back"] = { 0, 0.2, 1.1, 45, 0, 180 },
					["Head Katana"] = { 0, 1.6, 0, 45, 0, 0 },
					["Ass Shuriken"] = { 0, 0.4, -1, 90, 0, 0 },
					["Belt Kunai"] = { 1.2, 0.2, 0, 0, 0, 90 },
					["Pencil Penis"] = { 0, -0.4, 0.9, 0, 90, 0 },
				}
				local preset = akPresets[akPreset]

				if not preset then
					return notify("Select a preset first!")
				end

				configValues.AKOffsetX, configValues.AKOffsetY, configValues.AKOffsetZ = preset[1], preset[2], preset[3]
				configValues.AKRotX, configValues.AKRotY, configValues.AKRotZ = preset[4], preset[5], preset[6]

				if options.AKOffsetXS then
					options.AKOffsetXS:SetValue(preset[1])
				end

				if options.AKOffsetYS then
					options.AKOffsetYS:SetValue(preset[2])
				end

				if options.AKOffsetZS then
					options.AKOffsetZS:SetValue(preset[3])
				end

				if options.AKRotXS then
					options.AKRotXS:SetValue(preset[4])
				end

				if options.AKRotYS then
					options.AKRotYS:SetValue(preset[5])
				end

				if options.AKRotZS then
					options.AKRotZS:SetValue(preset[6])
				end

				if featureState.AntiKick then
					applyAntiKickOffsets()
				end
			end,
		})

		PresetsTab:AddInput("AKCustomPresetName", {
			Text = "Custom Preset Name",
			ClearTextOnFocus = false,
			Callback = function() end,
		})

		PresetsTab:AddButton({
			Text = "Save Custom Preset",
			Icon = "settings",
			Func = function()
				local presetName = options.AKCustomPresetName and options.AKCustomPresetName.Value or ""

				if presetName == "" then
					return notify("Enter a preset name first!")
				end

				if not (writefile and makefolder and isfolder) then
					return notify("Your executor does not support filesystem presets.")
				end

				local ok = pcall(function()
					if not isfolder("Resonance") then
						makefolder("Resonance")
					end

					if not isfolder("Resonance/AntiKickPresets") then
						makefolder("Resonance/AntiKickPresets")
					end

					writefile("Resonance/AntiKickPresets/" .. presetName .. ".json", HttpService:JSONEncode({
						X = configValues.AKOffsetX,
						Y = configValues.AKOffsetY,
						Z = configValues.AKOffsetZ,
						RotX = configValues.AKRotX,
						RotY = configValues.AKRotY,
						RotZ = configValues.AKRotZ,
					}))
				end)

				notify(ok and ("Preset saved: " .. presetName) or "Couldn't write the preset file.")
			end,
		})

		PresetsTab:AddButton({
			Text = "Load Custom Preset",
			Icon = "settings-2",
			Func = function()
				local presetName = options.AKCustomPresetName and options.AKCustomPresetName.Value or ""

				if presetName == "" then
					return notify("Enter a preset name first!")
				end

				if not (readfile and isfile) then
					return notify("Your executor does not support filesystem presets.")
				end

				local path = "Resonance/AntiKickPresets/" .. presetName .. ".json"

				if not isfile(path) then
					return notify("Preset not found: " .. presetName)
				end

				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path))
				end)

				if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then
					return notify("Couldn't read the preset file.")
				end

				configValues.AKOffsetX = tonumber(result.X) or 0
				configValues.AKOffsetY = tonumber(result.Y) or -0.3
				configValues.AKOffsetZ = tonumber(result.Z) or 0
				configValues.AKRotX = tonumber(result.RotX) or 90
				configValues.AKRotY = tonumber(result.RotY) or 0
				configValues.AKRotZ = tonumber(result.RotZ) or 0

				if options.AKOffsetXS then
					options.AKOffsetXS:SetValue(configValues.AKOffsetX)
				end

				if options.AKOffsetYS then
					options.AKOffsetYS:SetValue(configValues.AKOffsetY)
				end

				if options.AKOffsetZS then
					options.AKOffsetZS:SetValue(configValues.AKOffsetZ)
				end

				if options.AKRotXS then
					options.AKRotXS:SetValue(configValues.AKRotX)
				end

				if options.AKRotYS then
					options.AKRotYS:SetValue(configValues.AKRotY)
				end

				if options.AKRotZS then
					options.AKRotZS:SetValue(configValues.AKRotZ)
				end

				if featureState.AntiKick then
					applyAntiKickOffsets()
				end

				notify("Preset loaded: " .. presetName)
			end,
		})

				options.MethodConfigDropdown:SetValue(vals[1])
			end
		end

	do
		local Barrier = tabPages.Toys:AddRightGroupbox("Barrier", "circle-slash")
		local state12 = false
		local state13 = false

		Barrier:AddToggle("BreakBarrierT", {
			Text = "Disable House Barrier",
			Default = false,
			Tooltip = "Disables the destruction of non-house toys inside houses and vice-versa",
			Callback = function(arg)
				state12 = arg and not unloaded
				if not state12 or state13 then
					return
				end
				state13 = true

				task.spawn(function()
					local FoodHamburger = nil
					local value20 = nil
					local value21 = nil
					local state14 = false

					local function func21()
						if value21 and value20 and value20.Parent and localPlayer.Character and value20:IsDescendantOf(localPlayer.Character) then
							api.Teleport(value20, value21)
						end

						value21 = nil
						value20 = nil

						if state14 then
							sharedState.States.Owning = false
							state14 = false
						end

						if FoodHamburger then
							sharedState.ToyAuraReserved[FoodHamburger] = nil
							api.DestroyToy(FoodHamburger)
							FoodHamburger = nil
						end
					end

					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						if api.IsBarrierBroken() then
							if state12 then
								notify("Barrier is already broken!")

								if toggles.DeleteBarriers then
									toggles.DeleteBarriers:SetValue(true)
								end
							end

							return
						end

						while true do
							if state12 and task.wait() then
								local value22, value23, value24 = api.Char()

								if not (value23 and value24 and value24.Health > 0) or sharedState.States.Owning then
									continue
								else
									local function func22()
										return state12 and localPlayer.Character == value22 and value23.Parent and value24.Health > 0
									end

									FoodHamburger = api.SpawnToy("FoodHamburger", nil, 4, true)

									if not FoodHamburger then
										continue
									else
										local holdPart = FoodHamburger:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 0.35)
										local holdItemRemoteFunction = holdPart and holdPart:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 0.35)
										local rigidConstraint = holdItemRemoteFunction and holdPart:WaitForChild("RigidConstraint", 0.35)

										if not (func22() and holdItemRemoteFunction and rigidConstraint) then
											func21()
											continue
										else
											while func22() and sharedState.States.Owning do
												task.wait()
											end

											if not func22() then
												func21()
												break
											else
												local cFrame2 = value23.CFrame
												value20 = value23
												value21 = cFrame2
												state14 = true
												sharedState.States.Owning = true
												api.Teleport(value23, workspace.Waypoints.BlueHouse)
												local now2 = os.clock()

												while true do
													task.wait()
													if not (not func22() or localPlayer.InPlot.Value or os.clock() - now2 > 0.7) then
														continue
													end
													break
												end

												if not func22() or not localPlayer.InPlot.Value then
													func21()
													continue
												else
													local value25 = FoodHamburger

													task.spawn(function()
														if func22() and value25.Parent and holdItemRemoteFunction.Parent then
															pcall(function()
																holdItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(value25, value22)
															end)
														end
													end)

													local now3 = os.clock()

													while true do
														task.wait()
														if not (not func22() or not rigidConstraint.Parent or rigidConstraint.Attachment1 or os.clock() - now3 > 0.7) then
															continue
														end
														break
													end

													if not func22() or not rigidConstraint.Parent or not rigidConstraint.Attachment1 then
														func21()
														continue
													else
														api.DestroyToy(FoodHamburger)
														sharedState.ToyAuraReserved[FoodHamburger] = nil
														FoodHamburger = nil
														task.wait(0.5)
														func21()

														if not func22() then
															break
														else
															task.wait(1)

															if state12 and api.IsBarrierBroken() then
																if state12 then
																	notify("Barrier has been broken!")

																	if toggles.DeleteBarriers then
																		toggles.DeleteBarriers:SetValue(true)
																	end
																end

																break
															else
																continue
															end
														end
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end

							break
						end
					end)

					func21()
					state13 = false

					if not ok then
						warn("[House Barrier] " .. tostring(result))
					end
				end)
			end,
		})

		Barrier:AddLabel("BarrierStatus", { Text = "Current barrier status: 🟢" })
	end

	do
		local Effects = tabPages.Toys:AddRightGroupbox("Effects", "flask-conical")
		api.CreatePlayerDropdown(Effects, "Toy Effects", sharedState.SelectedPlayersEffect)
		Effects:AddDivider("Method")

		Effects:AddDropdown("MethodSettingsTEf", {
			Values = { "Oven", "Microwave", "Campfire", "Banana", "Ragdoll Kill", "Delete Limbs", "Fling" },
			ValueImages = {
				["Oven"] = "flame",

		local function func22(arg, arg2, arg3, min, max, rounding, arg4)
			local value21 = value20
			local addSlider = value21.AddSlider
			local table16 = { Text = arg2 }
			local value22

			if arg4 then
				value22 = toysAuraSettings[arg3][arg4]
			else
				value22 = toysAuraSettings[arg3]
			end

			table16.Default = value22
			table16.Min = min
			table16.Max = max
			table16.Compact = true
			table16.Rounding = rounding

			table16.Callback = function(arg5)
				if not arg4 then
					toysAuraSettings[arg3] = arg5
					return
				end
				local value23 = toysAuraSettings[arg3]
				local x

				if arg4 == "X" then
					x = arg5
				else
					x = value23.X
				end

				local y

				if arg4 == "Y" then
					y = arg5
				else
					y = value23.Y
				end

				if arg4 ~= "Z" then
					arg5 = value23.Z
				end

				local value24 = toysAuraSettings
				local value25 = arg3
				local cframe

				if arg3 == "Offset" then
					cframe = CFrame.new(x, y, arg5)
				else
					cframe = Vector3.new(x, y, arg5)
				end

				value24[value25] = cframe
			end

			addSlider(value21, arg, table16)
		end

					end

		api.ToysSourceFolders = function()
			local plotItems = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
			local table16

			if featureState.ToysAuraOnlyPlot and plotItems then
				table16 = {}

				for _, child in plotItems:GetChildren() do
					table.insert(table16, child)
				end
			elseif toysAuraSettings.OwnInventoryOnly then
				return { toysFolder }
			else
				table16 = sharedState.Inventories
			end

			if not featureState.ToysAuraExcludeProtected then
				return table16
			end

			local plotsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
			local plotOwnerNames = nil

			if plotsFolder and plotItems then
				plotOwnerNames = {}

				for _, plot in plotsFolder:GetChildren() do
					local plotSign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
					local plotOwners = plotSign and plotSign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")

					if plotOwners then
						for _, ownerValue in plotOwners:GetChildren() do
							if ownerValue:IsA("StringValue") then
								plotOwnerNames[plot.Name] = ownerValue.Value
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local function isProtectedOwner(ownerName)
				if ownerName == localPlayer.Name then
					return true
				end

				local owner = Players:FindFirstChild(ownerName)

				return owner ~= nil and sharedState.Whitelisted[owner] == true
			end

			local table17 = {}

			for _, value22 in table16 do
				local ownerName = value22.Name:match("^(.+)SpawnedInToys$")
				local skipFolder = false

				if ownerName then
					skipFolder = isProtectedOwner(ownerName)
				elseif value22 == toysFolder then
					skipFolder = true
				elseif plotOwnerNames and plotOwnerNames[value22.Name] then
					skipFolder = isProtectedOwner(plotOwnerNames[value22.Name])
				end

				if not skipFolder then
					table.insert(table17, value22)
				end
			end

			return table17
		end

		api.IsToyAllowed = function(arg)
			local toyWhitelist = sharedState.ToyWhitelist

			if toyWhitelist and toyWhitelist[arg.Name] then
				return false
			end

			return true
		end

		local function refreshToyWhitelistValues()
			local table16 = {}

			for _, value22 in sharedState.Inventories do
				for _, child in value22:GetChildren() do
					if child:IsA("Model") then
						local name = child.Name

						if not table16[name] then
							table16[name] = true
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local table17 = {}

			for name in table16 do
				table.insert(table17, name)
			end

			table.sort(table17)

			if options.ToyWhitelist then
				options.ToyWhitelist:SetValues(table17)
			end
		end

		task.delay(10, refreshToyWhitelistValues)

		value20:AddToggle("ToysAuraOnlyPlot", {

	do
		local value20 = tabPages.Toys:AddLeftGroupbox("Object Groups", "group")
		local state12 = false

		local function func21(arg, arg2)
			local value21 = sharedState.ObjectGroups[configValues.ObjectGroup]
			if state12 or unloaded or not value21 then
				return
			end
			value21[arg] = arg2
			api.UpdateObjectGroup(value21)
		end

		local function func22()
			local value21 = sharedState.ObjectGroups[configValues.ObjectGroup]
			state12 = true

			for k, value22 in pairs({ GroupNoclip = "Noclip", GroupHighlight = "Highlight" }) do
				if toggles[k] then
					toggles[k]:SetDisabled(not value21)
					local value23 = toggles[k]
					local setValue = value23.SetValue
					local state13

					if value21 then
						state13 = value21[value22]
					else
						state13 = false
					end

					setValue(value23, state13)
				end
			end

			if options.GroupFill then
				local groupFill = options.GroupFill
				local setValue = groupFill.SetValue

				if value21 then
					fillColor = value21.FillColor

				end

				setValue(groupFill, fillColor)
			end

			if options.GroupOutline then
				local groupOutline = options.GroupOutline
				local setValue = groupOutline.SetValue
				local outlineColor

				if value21 then
					outlineColor = value21.OutlineColor
				else
					outlineColor = sharedState.AnchorSettings.OutlineColor
				end

				setValue(groupOutline, outlineColor)
			end

			state12 = false
		end

	do
		local Experimental = tabPages.Toys:AddRightGroupbox("Experimental", "flask-conical")
		Experimental:AddDivider("Anchored Parts")
		local num36 = 1
		local enabled = false
		local num37 = 0
		local weakCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
		local brokenPartHighlight = sharedState.BrokenPartHighlight

		Experimental:AddToggle("BreakPartAffectsYou", {
			Text = "Affects you",
			Default = false,
			Callback = function(enabled2)
				enabled = enabled2

				for k in weakCache, nil, nil do
					if k.Parent then
						k.Enabled = enabled2
					else
						weakCache[k] = nil
					end
				end
			end,
		})

		local HighlightBrokenParts = Experimental:AddToggle("HighlightBrokenParts", {
			Text = "Highlight Broken Parts",
			Default = false,
			Callback = api.SetBrokenPartHighlights,
		})

		HighlightBrokenParts:AddColorPicker("BrokenPartFill", {
			Default = brokenPartHighlight.FillColor,
			Transparency = brokenPartHighlight.FillTransparency,
			Title = "Fill Color",
			Callback = function(fillColor)
				local brokenPartFill = options.BrokenPartFill
				brokenPartHighlight.FillColor = fillColor
				brokenPartHighlight.FillTransparency = brokenPartFill and brokenPartFill.Transparency or brokenPartHighlight.FillTransparency
				api.SetBrokenPartHighlights(featureState.HighlightBrokenParts)
			end,
		})

		HighlightBrokenParts:AddColorPicker("BrokenPartOutline", {
			Default = brokenPartHighlight.OutlineColor,
			Transparency = brokenPartHighlight.OutlineTransparency,
			Title = "Outline Color",
			Callback = function(outlineColor)
				local brokenPartOutline = options.BrokenPartOutline
				brokenPartHighlight.OutlineColor = outlineColor
				brokenPartHighlight.OutlineTransparency = brokenPartOutline and brokenPartOutline.Transparency or brokenPartHighlight.OutlineTransparency
				api.SetBrokenPartHighlights(featureState.HighlightBrokenParts)
			end,
		})

		local function func21(arg)
			local value20 = arg or api.GetTarget()
			if not value20 or not value20:IsA("BasePart") then
				return
			end
			local cframe

			if num36 == 1 then
				cframe = CFrame.new(1e15, 1e15, 1e15)
			else
				cframe = CFrame.new(0, -2.3e9, 0)
			end

			local NinjaShuriken = api.SpawnToy("NinjaShuriken")
			local stickyPart = NinjaShuriken and NinjaShuriken:WaitForChild("StickyPart", 0.5)
			local stickyWeld = stickyPart and stickyPart:WaitForChild("StickyWeld", 0.5)
			if not stickyWeld then
				return api.DestroyToy(NinjaShuriken)
			end

			if not api.SNO(stickyPart) then
				return api.DestroyToy(NinjaShuriken)
			end
			api.TrackBrokenPart(value20, stickyWeld)
			num37 += 1
			local text5 = "BreakPartAffectsYou" .. num37

			api.SetRuntimeConnection(text5, stickyWeld:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"), true, function()
				if stickyWeld.Enabled ~= enabled then
					stickyWeld.Enabled = enabled
				end
			end)

			api.SetRuntimeConnection(text5 .. "Cleanup", stickyWeld.Destroying, true, function()
				weakCache[stickyWeld] = nil
				api.UntrackBrokenPart(value20, stickyWeld)

				api.SetRuntimeConnection(text5 .. "Cleanup", nil, false)
			end)

			weakCache[stickyWeld] = true
			stickyWeld.Enabled = value21
			remotes.StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, value20, cframe)
		end

				for _, inventory in pairs(sharedState.Inventories) do

					BigIcon = "rbxassetid://79685990879972",

					IconColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),

					api.DestroyObject(value25)
					value24:ChangeDescription("Destroyed " .. i .. "/" .. #table16 .. " toys")
				end

				while configValues.DestroyThread and not unloaded do
					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

					value24:Destroy()
				end)
			end,
		})

	do

		Camera:AddDivider()

		local function func21(localTransparencyModifier)
			if not localTransparencyModifier then
				return
			end
			local value20 = api.Char()
			if not (value20 and value20:FindFirstChild("Head")) then
				return
			end

			if (currentCamera.CFrame.Position - currentCamera.Focus.Position).Magnitude > 1.5 then
				localTransparencyModifier = 0
			end

			for _, child in ipairs(value20:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name ~= "Head" and child.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and child.Name ~= "Torso" then
					child.LocalTransparencyModifier = localTransparencyModifier
				end
			end
		end

		local function func22()
			func21(options.RFPTransparency.Value)
		end

		Camera:AddToggle("RealisticFirstPersonT", {

			Callback = function(arg)
				api.SetRuntimeConnection("RealisticFirstPerson", RunService.RenderStepped, arg, func22)

				if arg then
					func22()
				else
					func21(1)
				end
			end,
		})

	do
		local Graphics = tabPages.Visual:AddRightGroupbox("Graphics", "file-image")
		local num36 = 0
		local num37 = 0.004
		local num38 = 128

		local function func21(arg)
			if os.clock() - arg < num37 then
				return arg
			end
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			return os.clock()
		end

		local function func22(arg)
			local now2 = os.clock()

			for i = #sharedState.WaterParts, 1, -1 do
				if arg ~= num36 then
					return false
				end
				local value20 = sharedState.WaterParts[i]
				local value21 = value20[1]
				local transparency = value20[2]

				if value21 and value21.Parent then
					local value = toggles.WaterWalk and toggles.WaterWalk.Value

					if value and transparency ~= 1 then
						transparency = 0.5
					end

					value21.Transparency = transparency

					if not value and sharedState.WaterPartStates[value21] then
						value21.CanCollide = sharedState.WaterPartStates[value21][1]
						sharedState.WaterPartStates[value21] = nil
					end
				end

				sharedState.WaterParts[i] = nil
				now2 = func21(now2)
			end

			for i = #sharedState.WaterRegions, 1, -1 do
				if arg ~= num36 then
					return false
				end
				terrain:FillRegion(sharedState.WaterRegions[i], 4, Enum.Material.Air)
				sharedState.WaterRegions[i] = nil
				now2 = func21(now2)
			end

			table.clear(sharedState.WaterRegions)
			table.clear(sharedState.WaterParts)
			return true
		end

		local function func23(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
			local value20 = Region3.new(arg, arg2):ExpandToGrid(4)
			local num39 = value20.CFrame.Position - value20.Size / 2
			local num40 = value20.CFrame.Position + value20.Size / 2
			local x = num39.X

			while x < num40.X do
				local num41 = math.min(x + num38, num40.X)
				local y = num39.Y
				local exitTo = nil

				while true do
					if not (y < num40.Y) then
						exitTo = 1
						break
					else
						local num42 = math.min(y + num38, num40.Y)
						local z = num39.Z
						local exitTo2 = nil

						while true do
							if not (z < num40.Z) then
								exitTo2 = 1
								break
							else
								if arg3 == num36 then
									local num43 = math.min(z + num38, num40.Z)
									local value21 = Region3.new(Vector3.new(x, y, z), Vector3.new(num41, num42, num43))
									sharedState.WaterRegions[#sharedState.WaterRegions + 1] = value21
									terrain:FillRegion(value21, 4, Enum.Material.Water)
									arg4 = func21(arg4)
									z = num43
									continue
								end

								break
							end
						end

						if exitTo2 == 1 then
							y = num42
							continue
						end
						break
					end
				end

				if exitTo == 1 then
					x = num41
					continue
				end
				return arg4, false
			end

			return arg4, true
		end

		local function func24(arg)
			num36 += 1
			local value20 = num36

			task.spawn(function()
				if not func22(value20) or not arg then
					return
				end
				local objectModel = workspace.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.Ocean.Object.ObjectModel
				local vector = Vector3.new(0, configValues.WaterYOffset, 0)
				local table16 = {}

				for _, value21 in ipairs(objectModel:QueryDescendants("BasePart")) do
					local table17 = sharedState.WaterPartStates[value21]

					if not table17 then
						if value21.Transparency == 1 then
							continue
						end
						table17 = { value21.CanCollide, value21.Transparency }
						sharedState.WaterPartStates[value21] = table17
					end

					if table17[2] ~= 1 then
						table16[#table16 + 1] = { value21, table17[2], value21.Position - value21.Size / 2 + vector, value21.Position + value21.Size / 2 + vector }
					end
				end

				for _, value21 in ipairs(table16) do
					local value22 = value21[1]

					if value22.Parent then
						sharedState.WaterParts[#sharedState.WaterParts + 1] = { value22, value21[2] }
						value22.Transparency = 1
					end
				end

				local now2 = os.clock()

				for _, value21 in ipairs(table16) do
					if value20 ~= num36 then
						return
					end
					local value22, value23 = func23(value21[3], value21[4], value20, now2)
					if not value23 then
						return
					end
					now2 = value22
				end
			end)
		end

		local table16 = {}

		local function func25(arg, humanoid)
			if table16.Humanoid ~= humanoid or not arg then
				if table16.Connection then
					table16.Connection:Disconnect()
				end

				if table16.Humanoid and table16.Humanoid.Parent and table16.Enabled ~= nil then
					table16.Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming, table16.Enabled)
				end

				table.clear(table16)
			end

			if not arg or not humanoid then
				return
			end

			if not table16.Humanoid then
				table16.Humanoid = humanoid
				table16.Enabled = humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming)

				table16.Connection = humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(old, new)
					if new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming and featureState.NonSwimmableWater and toggles.CustomWaterT.Value then
						humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
					end
				end)
			end

			humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming, false)
			local swimming = Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming

			if humanoid:GetState() == swimming then
				humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
			end
		end

		api.RestoreWaterSettings = function()
			func24(false)
			func25(false)

			for k, waterPartState in pairs(sharedState.WaterPartStates) do
				if k.Parent then
					local value20 = waterPartState[2]
					k.CanCollide = waterPartState[1]
					k.Transparency = value20
				end
			end

			table.clear(sharedState.WaterPartStates)

			for k, waterSetting in pairs(sharedState.WaterSettings) do
				terrain[k] = waterSetting
			end
		end

		localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
			if unloaded or not featureState.NonSwimmableWater or not toggles.CustomWaterT.Value then
				return
			end
			local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

			if humanoid then

		end)

		Graphics:AddDivider("Shaders")

		local table17 = {
			Lighting = {
				Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
				Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
				ColorShift_Bottom = Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom,

			},
			Effects = {},
		}

		local function func26()
			for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
				if child:GetAttribute("RSC") then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end

			table.clear(table17.Effects)
		end

		Graphics:AddToggle("ShadersT", {
			Text = "Shaders",
			Default = false,
			Callback = function(shaders)
				featureState.Shaders = shaders
				func26()

				if shaders then
					local settings_ = table17.Settings

					for k in pairs(table17.Lighting) do
						table17.Lighting[k] = Lighting[k]
					end

					Lighting.Brightness = settings_.Brightness
					Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(11, 0, 20)
					Lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(240, 127, 14)
					Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(34, 0, 49)
					Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(94, 76, 106)
					Lighting.FogEnd = 1000
					Lighting.FogStart = 0
					Lighting.ExposureCompensation = settings_.Exposure
					Lighting.ShadowSoftness = 0
					Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(59, 33, 27)

					for _, value21 in ipairs({
						{ "BloomEffect", { Intensity = settings_.BloomIntensity, Threshold = 0, Size = 100 } },
						{ "BlurEffect", { Size = 2 } },
						{ "ColorCorrectionEffect", { Saturation = settings_.Saturation, TintColor = settings_.TintColor } },
						{ "SunRaysEffect", { Intensity = 0.05 } },
					}) do
						local instance = Instance.new(value21[1])
						instance.Name = "RSC"

						for k, value22 in pairs(value21[2]) do
							instance[k] = value22
						end

						instance.Parent = Lighting
						table17.Effects[value21[1]] = instance
					end
				else
					for k, value21 in pairs(table17.Lighting) do
						Lighting[k] = value21
					end
				end
			end,
		}):AddColorPicker("ShaderTintColor", {

		local function applyShaderValues()
			local settings_ = table17.Settings

			Lighting.Brightness = settings_.Brightness
			Lighting.ExposureCompensation = settings_.Exposure

			local bloomEffect = table17.Effects.BloomEffect

			if bloomEffect and bloomEffect.Parent then
				bloomEffect.Intensity = settings_.BloomIntensity
			end

			local colorCorrectionEffect = table17.Effects.ColorCorrectionEffect

			if colorCorrectionEffect and colorCorrectionEffect.Parent then
				colorCorrectionEffect.Saturation = settings_.Saturation
				colorCorrectionEffect.TintColor = settings_.TintColor
			end
		end

		local function setShaderPreset(preset)
			table17.Settings.Brightness = preset.Brightness
			table17.Settings.Exposure = preset.Exposure
			table17.Settings.BloomIntensity = preset.BloomIntensity
			table17.Settings.Saturation = preset.Saturation
			table17.Settings.TintColor = preset.TintColor

			if options.ShaderBrightness then
				options.ShaderBrightness:SetValue(preset.Brightness)
			end

			if options.ShaderExposure then
				options.ShaderExposure:SetValue(preset.Exposure)
			end

			if options.ShaderBloomIntensity then
				options.ShaderBloomIntensity:SetValue(preset.BloomIntensity)
			end

			if options.ShaderSaturation then
				options.ShaderSaturation:SetValue(preset.Saturation)
			end

			if options.ShaderTintColor then
				options.ShaderTintColor:SetValue(preset.TintColor)
			end

			if featureState.Shaders then
				applyShaderValues()
			end
		end

		local function refreshShaderPresets()
			local names = {}

			if listfiles and isfolder and isfolder("Resonance/VisualPresets") then
				for _, filePath in listfiles("Resonance/VisualPresets") do
					local fileName = filePath:match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")

					if fileName then
						names[#names + 1] = fileName
					end
				end
			end

			table.sort(names)

			if options.ShaderSavedPreset then
				options.ShaderSavedPreset:SetValues(names)
			end

			return names
		end

		value21:AddLabel("CurrentShaderPreset", { Text = "Current Preset: <b>Custom</b>", DoesWrap = true })
