			Text = "Preset Name",
			ClearTextOnFocus = false,
			Callback = function() end,
		})

		value21:AddDropdown("ShaderSavedPreset", {
			Values = {},
			Default = nil,
			Multi = false,
			AllowNull = true,
			Searchable = true,
			Text = "Saved Preset",
			Callback = function() end,
		})

		value21:AddButton({
			Text = "Save Current",
			Icon = "settings",
			Func = function()
				local presetName = options.ShaderPresetName and options.ShaderPresetName.Value or ""

				if presetName == "" then
					return notify("Enter a preset name first!")
				end

				if not (writefile and makefolder and isfolder) then
					return notify("Your executor does not support filesystem presets.")
				end

				local settings_ = table17.Settings
				local ok = pcall(function()
					if not isfolder("Resonance") then
						makefolder("Resonance")
					end

					if not isfolder("Resonance/VisualPresets") then
						makefolder("Resonance/VisualPresets")
					end

					writefile("Resonance/VisualPresets/" .. presetName .. ".json", HttpService:JSONEncode({
						Brightness = settings_.Brightness,
						Exposure = settings_.Exposure,
						BloomIntensity = settings_.BloomIntensity,
						Saturation = settings_.Saturation,
						Tint = { settings_.TintColor.R, settings_.TintColor.G, settings_.TintColor.B },
						Skybox = configValues.SkyboxImage,
					}))
				end)

				if ok then
					refreshShaderPresets()
				end

				notify(ok and ("Preset saved: " .. presetName) or "Couldn't write the preset file.")
			end,
		})

		value21:AddButton({
			Text = "Load",
			Icon = "settings-2",
			Func = function()
				local presetName = (options.ShaderSavedPreset and options.ShaderSavedPreset.Value) or (options.ShaderPresetName and options.ShaderPresetName.Value) or ""

				if presetName == "" then
					return notify("Select or enter a preset name first!")
				end

				if not (readfile and isfile) then
					return notify("Your executor does not support filesystem presets.")
				end

				local path = "Resonance/VisualPresets/" .. presetName .. ".json"

				if not isfile(path) then
					return notify("Preset not found: " .. presetName)
				end

				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path))
				end)

				if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then
					return notify("Couldn't read the preset file.")
				end

				local tintColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)

				if type(result.Tint) == "table" then
					tintColor = Color3.new(result.Tint[1] or 1, result.Tint[2] or 1, result.Tint[3] or 1)
				end

				setShaderPreset({
					Brightness = tonumber(result.Brightness) or 2.14,
					Exposure = tonumber(result.Exposure) or 0.24,
					BloomIntensity = tonumber(result.BloomIntensity) or 0.1,
					Saturation = tonumber(result.Saturation) or 0.05,
					TintColor = tintColor,
				})

				if type(result.Skybox) == "string" and result.Skybox ~= "" then
					configValues.SkyboxImage = result.Skybox
				end

				if uiLib.Labels.CurrentShaderPreset then
					local currentLabel = uiLib.Labels.CurrentShaderPreset
					local setText = currentLabel.SetText
					setText(currentLabel, ("Current Preset: <b>%*</b>"):format(presetName))
				end

				notify("Preset loaded: " .. presetName)
			end,
		})

		value21:AddButton({
			Text = "Delete",
			Func = function()
				local presetName = (options.ShaderSavedPreset and options.ShaderSavedPreset.Value) or (options.ShaderPresetName and options.ShaderPresetName.Value) or ""

				if presetName == "" then
					return notify("Select or enter a preset name first!")
				end

				if not (isfile and delfile) then
					return notify("Your executor does not support filesystem presets.")
				end

				local path = "Resonance/VisualPresets/" .. presetName .. ".json"

				if not isfile(path) then
					return notify("Preset not found: " .. presetName)
				end

				local ok = pcall(delfile, path)

				if ok then
					refreshShaderPresets()
				end

				notify(ok and ("Preset deleted: " .. presetName) or "Couldn't delete the preset file.")
			end,
		})

		api.UpdateBlackHoleImage = function()
			local state12 = featureState.CustomBlackHole and configValues.CustomBlackhole ~= nil

			for _, value24 in workspace:GetChildren() do
				if not (value24.Name ~= "BlackHoleKick" and value24.Name ~= "StayedBH") then
					local hole = value24:FindFirstChild("Hole")
					local billboardGui = hole and hole:FindFirstChild("BillboardGui")
					local large = billboardGui and billboardGui:FindFirstChild("Large")
					billboardGui = billboardGui and billboardGui:FindFirstChild("Small")

					if hole and large and large:IsA("ImageLabel") then
						local customBlackhole

						if state12 then
							customBlackhole = configValues.CustomBlackhole
						else
							customBlackhole = "rbxassetid://9920612764"
						end

						large.Image = customBlackhole
						local customBHColor

						if state12 then
							customBHColor = configValues.CustomBHColor
						else
							customBHColor = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
						end

						large.ImageColor3 = customBHColor
						local transparency

						if state12 then
							transparency = 1
						else
							transparency = 0
						end

						hole.Transparency = transparency

						if billboardGui then
							billboardGui.Visible = not state12
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local value24 = value20:AddTab(nil, "volume-2")
		local mapNoises = map:FindFirstChild("MapNoises")
		local table17 = {}

		local table18 = {
			{
				"MuteAmbience",
				"Mute Ambience",
				function(arg)
					return mapNoises and arg:IsDescendantOf(mapNoises)
				end,
			},

			{

					return parent2 and parent2:IsA("BasePart") and parent2.Name == "Part" and parent2.Parent == workspace

				end,

				"Mute Rhythm Makers",
				function(arg)
					return arg.Name:lower():match("^sound[1-8]$") ~= nil
				end,
				true,
			},
			{
				"MuteInstruments",
				"Mute Instruments",
				function(arg)
					return arg.Parent and arg.Parent.Name == "MusicPart"
				end,
				true,

		local function func21(arg)
			for _, value25 in table18, nil, nil do
				local value26 = toggles[value25[1]]
				if value26 and value26.Value and value25[3](arg) then
					return value25
				end
			end

			return false
		end

		local function func22(arg)
			if not arg:IsA("Sound") then
				return
			end
			local value25 = func21(arg)
			local value26 = table17[arg]

			if value25 then
				if not value26 then
					local table19 = { arg.Volume }
					table17[arg] = table19

					if value25[4] then
						table19[2] = arg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Volume"):Connect(function()
							if func21(arg) and arg.Volume ~= 0 then
								arg.Volume = 0
							end
						end)
					end
				end

				arg.Volume = 0
			elseif value26 then
				table17[arg] = nil

				if value26[2] then
					value26[2]:Disconnect()
				end

				if arg.Parent then
					arg.Volume = value26[1]
				end
			end
		end

		local function func23(arg)
			local state12 = false

			for _, value25 in table18, nil, nil do
				state12 = state12 or toggles[value25[1]].Value
			end

			api.SetRuntimeConnection("MutedSounds", workspace.DescendantAdded, state12, func22)

			for k in table17, nil, nil do
				func22(k)
			end

			if arg then
				for _, value25 in workspace:QueryDescendants("Sound") do
					func22(value25)
				end
			end
		end

		api.ClearMutedSounds = function()
			api.SetRuntimeConnection("MutedSounds", nil, false)

			for k, value25 in table17, nil, nil do
				if value25[2] then
					value25[2]:Disconnect()
				end

				if k.Parent then
					k.Volume = value25[1]
				end
			end

			table.clear(table17)
		end

		for _, value25 in table18, nil, nil do
			value24:AddToggle(value25[1], {
				Text = value25[2],
				Default = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					func23(arg)
				end,
			})
		end
	end

			local value20 = sky
			local name

			if sky.Name ~= "Sky" then

		end

				SkyboxBk = table16.SkyboxBk,
				SkyboxDn = table16.SkyboxDn,

				SkyboxLf = table16.SkyboxLf,

			Default = math.round(table16.CloudDensity * 1000) / 1000,

			Max = 1,
			Rounding = 3,

				clouds.Density = density
			end,
		})

			Max = 1,

				clouds.Cover = cover
			end,
		})

				clouds.Enabled = enabled
			end,

			Lighting.ClockTime = table16.ClockTime

			if table16.Created then
				if table16.Instance.Parent then
					table16.Instance:Destroy()
				end
			elseif table16.Instance.Parent then
				sky = table16.Instance
				sky.SunAngularSize = table16.SunAngularSize
				func22()
			end

			if table16.CloudsCreated then
				if clouds.Parent then
					clouds:Destroy()
				end

				clouds.Density = table16.CloudDensity
				clouds.Cover = table16.CloudCover
				clouds.Enabled = table16.CloudsEnabled
			end
		end

		local text5 = "Resonance/AutoClickerPresets"

			if not options.ACCustomPoseS then
				return
			end
			local table17 = {}

			for k in sharedState.CustomACPresets, nil, nil do
				table.insert(table17, k)
			end

			table.sort(table17, function(arg2, arg3)
				return tostring(arg2):lower() < tostring(arg3):lower()
			end)

			options.ACCustomPoseS:SetValues(table17)
			local selectedCustomACPose = arg or configValues.SelectedCustomACPose
			local acCustomPoseS = options.ACCustomPoseS
			local setValue = acCustomPoseS.SetValue

			if not table.find(table17, selectedCustomACPose) then
				selectedCustomACPose = nil
			end

			setValue(acCustomPoseS, selectedCustomACPose)
		end

		api.SaveACCustomPresets = function(arg, arg2)
			if not value20 then
				return false, "Your exploit does not support filesystem presets."
			end

			local ok = pcall(function()
				if not isfolder("Resonance") then
					makefolder("Resonance")
				end

				if not isfolder("Resonance/AutoClickerPresets") then
					makefolder("Resonance/AutoClickerPresets")
				end

				writefile(text5 .. "/" .. arg .. ".json", HttpService:JSONEncode(arg2))
			end)

			local text6

			if ok then
				text6 = nil
			else
				text6 = "Couldn't write the preset file."
			end

			return ok, text6
		end

		if value20 then
			if not pcall(function()
				if not isfolder("Resonance") then
					makefolder("Resonance")
				end

				if not isfolder("Resonance/AutoClickerPresets") then
					makefolder("Resonance/AutoClickerPresets")
				end

				for _, value21 in ipairs(listfiles("Resonance/AutoClickerPresets")) do
					if value21:sub(-5) == ".json" then
						local match = value21:match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")

						local ok, result = pcall(function()
							return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(value21))
						end)

						if ok and typeof(result) == "table" then
							sharedState.CustomACPresets[match] = result
						end
					end
				end
			end) then
				notify("Custom Auto-Clicker presets couldn't be loaded.")
			end
		end

		Presets:AddDropdown("ACPoseS", {

		Presets:AddButton({
			Text = "Save Current Preset",
			Disabled = state12,
			DisabledTooltip = "Your exploit is missing readfile/writefile support.",
			Func = function()
				local text6 = tostring(configValues.CustomACPresetName or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", ""):gsub("[%c<>:\"/\\|%?%*]", "")
				if text6 == "" then
					notify("Enter a preset name first.")
					return
				end
				local value21 = sharedState.CustomACPresets[text6]
				local table17 = {}
				local bodyPos = {}
				local num = tonumber(string.format("%.1f", configValues.AutoClickerOffset.X))
				local num2 = tonumber(string.format("%.1f", configValues.AutoClickerOffset.Y))
				local value22 = tonumber
				local format = string.format
				local z = configValues.AutoClickerOffset.Z
				bodyPos[1] = num
				bodyPos[2] = num2

				do
					local values = table.pack(value22(format("%.1f", z)))
					table.move(values, 1, values.n, 3, bodyPos)
				end

				table17.BodyPos = bodyPos
				table17.LimbOffsets = {}

				for k, value23 in sharedState.ACRotation, nil, nil do
					local table18 = {}
					local num3 = tonumber(string.format("%.1f", value23.X))
					local num4 = tonumber(string.format("%.1f", value23.Y))
					local value24 = tonumber
					local format2 = string.format
					local z2 = value23.Z
					table18[1] = num3
					table18[2] = num4

					do
						local values = table.pack(value24(format2("%.1f", z2)))
						table.move(values, 1, values.n, 3, table18)
					end

					table17[k] = table18
				end

				for k, value23 in sharedState.ACOffset, nil, nil do
					local limbOffsets = table17.LimbOffsets
					local table18 = {}
					local num3 = tonumber(string.format("%.1f", value23.X))
					local num4 = tonumber(string.format("%.1f", value23.Y))
					local value24 = tonumber
					local format2 = string.format
					local z2 = value23.Z
					table18[1] = num3
					table18[2] = num4

					do
						local values = table.pack(value24(format2("%.1f", z2)))
						table.move(values, 1, values.n, 3, table18)
					end

					limbOffsets[k] = table18
				end

				sharedState.CustomACPresets[text6] = table17
				local value23, value24 = api.SaveACCustomPresets(text6, table17)

				if not value23 then
					sharedState.CustomACPresets[text6] = value21
					notify("Couldn't save custom preset: " .. value24)
					return
				end

				api.SyncACCustomPresets(text6)
				options.ACCustomPresetName:SetValue(text6)
				local value25 = notify
				local text7

				if value21 then
					text7 = "Updated custom preset!"
				else
					text7 = "Saved custom preset!"
				end

				value25(text7)
			end,
		})

		Presets:AddButton({
			Text = "Load Custom Preset",
			Disabled = state12,
			DisabledTooltip = "Your exploit is missing readfile/writefile support.",
			Func = function()
				if not configValues.SelectedCustomACPose then
					notify("Select a custom preset first.")
					return
				end
				local value21 = sharedState.CustomACPresets[configValues.SelectedCustomACPose]

				if not value21 then
					notify("That custom preset doesn't exist anymore.")
					api.SyncACCustomPresets()
					return
				end

				if api.LoadACPreset(value21) then
					options.ACCustomPresetName:SetValue(configValues.SelectedCustomACPose)
					notify("Successfully loaded custom preset!")
				end
			end,
		})

		Presets:AddButton({
			Text = "Delete Custom Preset",
			Disabled = state12,
			DisabledTooltip = "Your exploit is missing readfile/writefile support.",
			DoubleClick = true,
			Risky = true,
			Func = function()
				if not configValues.SelectedCustomACPose then
					notify("Select a custom preset first.")
					return
				end
				local value21 = sharedState.CustomACPresets[configValues.SelectedCustomACPose]
				sharedState.CustomACPresets[configValues.SelectedCustomACPose] = nil

				if not pcall(function()
					delfile(text5 .. "/" .. configValues.SelectedCustomACPose .. ".json")
				end) then
					sharedState.CustomACPresets[configValues.SelectedCustomACPose] = value21
					notify("Couldn't delete custom preset.")
					return
				end

				configValues.SelectedCustomACPose = nil
				options.ACCustomPresetName:SetValue("")
				api.SyncACCustomPresets()
				notify("Deleted custom preset!")
			end,
		})

		if state12 then
			Presets:AddLabel("Custom presets require readfile/writefile support.", true)
		end
	end

	api.GetTarget = function()
		if not uiLib.IsMobile and mouse then
			return mouse.Target
		end
		local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
		local value20 = currentCamera:ViewportPointToRay(viewportSize.X / 2, viewportSize.Y / 2)
		local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
		raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		local value21 = api.Char()

		if value21 then
			raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { value21 }
		end

		local hit = workspace:Raycast(value20.Origin, value20.Direction * 1000, raycastParams)
		return hit and hit.Instance
	end

	api.GetHit = function()
		if not uiLib.IsMobile and mouse then
			return mouse.Hit
		end
		local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
		local value20 = currentCamera:ViewportPointToRay(viewportSize.X / 2, viewportSize.Y / 2)
		local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
		raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		local value21 = api.Char()

		if value21 then
			raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { value21 }
		end

		local hit = workspace:Raycast(value20.Origin, value20.Direction * 1000, raycastParams)
		local cframe

		if hit then
			cframe = CFrame.new(hit.Position)
		else
			cframe = CFrame.new(value20.Origin + value20.Direction * 1000)
		end

		return cframe
	end

	api.AddKeybind = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5, arg6)
		arg:AddLabel(arg3):AddKeyPicker(arg2, { Text = arg3, Default = arg4, NoUI = true, Mode = arg5, Callback = arg6 })

		if arg5 == "Toggle" then
			sharedState.ToggleStates[arg3] = false
		end

		sharedState.RegisteredKeybinds[arg3] = function()
			if arg5 == "Toggle" then
				sharedState.ToggleStates[arg3] = not sharedState.ToggleStates[arg3]
				arg6(sharedState.ToggleStates[arg3])
			else
				arg6(true)
			end
		end

		table.insert(sharedState.KeybindNames, arg3)
	end

	api.AddMobileButton = function(arg, position)
		if not sharedState.RegisteredKeybinds[arg] or sharedState.ActiveDraggableButtons[arg] then
			return
		end

		local value20 = uiLib:AddDraggableButton(arg, function()
			local value20 = sharedState.RegisteredKeybinds[arg]

			if value20 then
				value20()
			end
		end)

		if typeof(position) == "UDim2" then
			value20.Button.Position = position
		end

		sharedState.ActiveDraggableButtons[arg] = value20
		return value20
	end

	api.RemoveMobileButton = function(arg)
		local value20 = sharedState.ActiveDraggableButtons[arg]
		if not value20 then
			return
		end
		value20:Destroy()
		sharedState.ActiveDraggableButtons[arg] = nil
	end

			Callback = function(smoothClickTP)
				featureState.SmoothClickTP = smoothClickTP
			end,
		})

		local num36 = 0
		local clickTPGliding = false
		local clickTPTarget = nil

		api.AddKeybind(LocalPlayer, "ClickTPKey", "Click TP", "Z", "Press", function()
			if not api.GetTarget() then
				return
			end
			local value21, assemblyRootPart = api.Char()
			assemblyRootPart = assemblyRootPart and assemblyRootPart.AssemblyRootPart
			local value22 = api.GetHit()
			if not assemblyRootPart or not value22 then
				return
			end
			local num37 = value22.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
			local num38 = assemblyRootPart.CFrame - assemblyRootPart.Position

			if not featureState.SmoothClickTP then
				api.Teleport(assemblyRootPart, CFrame.new(num37) * num38)
				return
			end

			if not clickTPGliding then
				clickTPGliding = true
				clickTPTarget = num37
				num36 += 1

				task.spawn(function()
					local value20 = num36
					local position = assemblyRootPart.Position
					local now2 = tick()

					while true do
					if value20 ~= num36 or not assemblyRootPart.Parent then
						break
					end

					local num39 = math.min((tick() - now2) / 0.3, 1)
					local num40 = num39 * (2 - num39)
					api.Teleport(assemblyRootPart, CFrame.new(position:Lerp(clickTPTarget, num40)) * num38)
					task.wait()

					if num40 >= 1 then
						break
					end
				end

				if value20 == num36 then
					clickTPGliding = false
					clickTPTarget = nil
				end
				end)
			else
				clickTPTarget = num37
			end
			end)

			local value20, value21, value22 = api.Char()

			end
		end)

				end)

			else
				pcall(function()
					RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RSCZoom")
				end)

		end)

				value22:ChangeState("Jumping")
				task.wait()

				for i = 1, 360 do
					task.delay(i / 720, function()

						value21.CFrame = value21.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(-0.0174533, 0, 0)
					end)
				end

				task.wait(0.55)
				value22.Sit = false
			end
		end)

			local value20 = api.GetTarget()

			if not parent2 or sharedState.AutoClicker[parent2] or parent2:IsDescendantOf(map) then
				if parent2 then
					sharedState.AutoClicker[parent2] = nil
				end

				return
			end

			local state12 = nil

			if Players:FindFirstChild(parent2.Name) then
				state12 = true
			end

			local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
			highlight.Adornee = parent2
			highlight.Parent = parent2
			highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(152, 171, 28)
			sharedState.AutoClicker[parent2] = true

			if state12 and parent2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				local humanoidRootPart = parent2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local humanoid = parent2:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
				bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
				bodyPosition.D = 100
				bodyPosition.Position = humanoidRootPart.Position
				bodyPosition.Parent = humanoidRootPart

				task.spawn(function()
					while true do
						if sharedState.AutoClicker[parent2] and humanoidRootPart.Parent and humanoid and humanoid.Parent and humanoid.Health ~= 0 and waitAtLeast() then
							local value21, value22 = api.Char()

							if not value22 then
								continue
							elseif not humanoidRootPart:IsDescendantOf(workspace.PlotItems) then
								task.spawn(api.Ragdoll, parent2)

								if api.GetMagnitude(value22, humanoidRootPart) <= 32 then
									bodyPosition.Parent = humanoidRootPart
									highlight.Enabled = true
									api.Grab(humanoidRootPart)
								else
									bodyPosition.Parent = nil
									bodyPosition.Position = humanoidRootPart.Position
									highlight.Enabled = false
								end

								continue
							end
						end

						break
					end

					sharedState.AutoClicker[parent2] = nil
					api.RNO(humanoidRootPart)
					highlight:Destroy()
					bodyPosition:Destroy()
				end)
			elseif not state12 then
				if not api.FindNicePart(parent2) then
					sharedState.AutoClicker[parent2] = false
					return
				end

				task.spawn(function()
					while sharedState.AutoClicker[parent2] and value20.Parent and waitAtLeast() do
						local value21, value22 = api.Char()

						if value22 then
							if api.GetMagnitude(value22, value20) <= 20 then
								highlight.Enabled = true
								api.Grab(value20)
							else
								highlight.Enabled = false
							end
						end
					end

					sharedState.AutoClicker[parent2] = nil
					api.RNO(value20)
					highlight:Destroy()
				end)
			end
		end)

		Gamepasses2:AddToggle("FartherReach", {
			Text = "Further Reach Line",
			Default = false,
			Tooltip = "Increases your reach",
			Callback = function(arg)
				local state12 = arg and not unloaded
				if table16.Enabled == state12 then
					return
				end

				if state12 then
					local gamepassEvents = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GamepassEvents")
					local menuToys = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("MenuToys")
					local furtherReachBoughtNotifier = gamepassEvents and gamepassEvents:FindFirstChild("FurtherReachBoughtNotifier")

					local fartherReach = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("FartherReach")
					if not (furtherReachBoughtNotifier and furtherReachBoughtNotifier:IsA("RemoteEvent") and limitedTimeToyEvent and limitedTimeToyEvent:IsA("RemoteEvent")) or fartherReach and not fartherReach:IsA("BoolValue") then
						toggles.FartherReach:SetValue(false)
						return notify("Further Reach is unavailable: required game objects are missing or invalid.")
					end
					local value20 = table16
					local value21 = table16
					local value22 = table16
					local value23 = table16
					table16.Enabled = true
					value20.Notifier = furtherReachBoughtNotifier
					value21.Event = limitedTimeToyEvent
					value22.Events = gamepassEvents
					value23.Toys = menuToys
					local value24 = table16
					local value25 = table16
					local boolValue = fartherReach or Instance.new("BoolValue")
					fartherReach = fartherReach and fartherReach.Value
					value24.Line = boolValue
					value25.LineValue = fartherReach
					local line = table16.Line

					line2.Parent = value26

					limitedTimeToyEvent.Name = "FurtherReachBoughtNotifier"
					limitedTimeToyEvent.Parent = gamepassEvents
					local connection = nil

					local function func21(character)
						if not connection.Connected or unloaded then
							return
						end
						local grabbingScript = character and character:WaitForChild("GrabbingScript", 5)
						if not (connection.Connected and not unloaded and character == localPlayer.Character and grabbingScript and grabbingScript.Parent == character and grabbingScript:IsA("LocalScript")) then
							return
						end

						if table16.Script then
							table16.Script:Disconnect()
						end

						local function func22()
							if connection.Connected and not unloaded and character == localPlayer.Character and grabbingScript.Parent == character and grabbingScript.Enabled and limitedTimeToyEvent.Parent then
								limitedTimeToyEvent:FireServer()
							end
						end

						grabbingScript.Enabled = false
						grabbingScript.Enabled = true

						table16.Script = grabbingScript:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
							if grabbingScript.Enabled then
								task.delay(1, func22)
							end
						end)

						task.delay(1, func22)
					end

					connection = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(func21)
					table16.Connection = connection
					task.spawn(func21, localPlayer.Character)
				else
					table16.Enabled = false

					if table16.Connection then
						table16.Connection:Disconnect()
					end

					if table16.Script then
						table16.Script:Disconnect()
					end

					if table16.Line and table16.Line.Parent then
						if table16.LineValue == nil then
							table16.Line:Destroy()
						else
							table16.Line.Value = table16.LineValue
						end
					end

					if table16.Event and table16.Event.Parent and table16.Toys.Parent then
						local event = table16.Event
						local toys = table16.Toys
						table16.Event.Name = "LimitedTimeToyEvent"
						event.Parent = toys
					end

					if table16.Notifier and table16.Notifier.Parent and table16.Events.Parent then
						table16.Notifier.Parent = table16.Events
					end

					local value20 = table16
					local value21 = table16
					local value22 = table16
					local value23 = table16
					table16.Connection = nil
					value20.Script = nil
					value21.Line = nil
					value22.Event = nil
					value23.Notifier = nil
					local grabbingScript = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("GrabbingScript")

					if grabbingScript and grabbingScript:IsA("LocalScript") then
						grabbingScript.Enabled = false
						grabbingScript.Enabled = true
					end
				end

				if grabParts then
					grabParts:Destroy()
				end
			end,
		})

	local Cheaters = tabPages.Misc:AddLeftGroupbox("Cheaters", "drama")

	local function collectCheaters()
		local table16 = {}

			for _, value20 in sharedState.PlayerList, nil, nil do
				if value20 ~= localPlayer then
					local value21, value22, value23 = api.Char(value20)
					local func21, ragdollTouchedHitbox, firePlayerPart, value24, value25

					if value23 then
						local seatPart = value23.SeatPart

						if seatPart then
							local seatWeld = seatPart:FindFirstChild("SeatWeld")

							if not seatWeld or not seatWeld.Enabled then
								table16[value20] = "Has Gucci"
							elseif value22 then
								func21 = function(arg)
									if not arg or not arg.Parent then
										return
									end

									for _, value26 in arg:GetConnectedParts() do
										if value26.Name == "StickyPart" then
											return true
										end
									end
								end

								ragdollTouchedHitbox = value22:FindFirstChild("RagdollTouchedHitbox")
								firePlayerPart = value22:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart")
								value24 = func21(value22) or func21(ragdollTouchedHitbox)
								value25 = value24 or func21(firePlayerPart)

								if value25 then
									table16[value20] = "Shuriken in HRP"
								end
							end
						elseif value22 then
							func21 = function(arg)
								if not arg or not arg.Parent then
									return
								end

								for _, value26 in arg:GetConnectedParts() do
									if value26.Name == "StickyPart" then
										return true
									end
								end
							end

							ragdollTouchedHitbox = value22:FindFirstChild("RagdollTouchedHitbox")
							firePlayerPart = value22:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart")
							value24 = func21(value22) or func21(ragdollTouchedHitbox)
							value25 = value24 or func21(firePlayerPart)

							if value25 then
								table16[value20] = "Shuriken in HRP"
							end
						end
					elseif value22 then
						func21 = function(arg)
							if not arg or not arg.Parent then
								return
							end

							for _, value26 in arg:GetConnectedParts() do
								if value26.Name == "StickyPart" then
									return true
								end
							end
						end

						ragdollTouchedHitbox = value22:FindFirstChild("RagdollTouchedHitbox")
						firePlayerPart = value22:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart")
						value24 = func21(value22) or func21(ragdollTouchedHitbox)
						value25 = value24 or func21(firePlayerPart)

						if value25 then
							table16[value20] = "Shuriken in HRP"
						end
					end
				end
			end

		return table16
	end

	local function reportCheaters(table16)
		if not next(table16) then
			return notify("Cheaters not found!")
		end
		local text5 = "Cheaters Found\n\n"

		for k, value20 in pairs(table16) do
			text5 ..= ("[%*]\n%*\n\n"):format(api.FormatPlayer(k), value20)
		end

		notify(text5:sub(1, -3))
	end

	Cheaters:AddButton({
		Text = "Find Cheaters",
		Icon = "eye",
		Func = function()
			reportCheaters(collectCheaters())
		end,
	})

	local autoFindLast = {}
	local nextAutoFind = 0
	local autoFindInterval = 15

	Cheaters:AddToggle("AutoFindCheaters", {
		Text = "Auto Find Cheaters",
		Default = false,
		Tooltip = "Automatically scans players for cheat indicators and notifies you when a new one appears",
		Callback = function(autoFind)
			if autoFind then
				nextAutoFind = 0
			end
		end,
	})

	Cheaters:AddSlider("AutoFindInterval", {
		Text = "Check Interval",
		Default = 15,
		Min = 5,
		Max = 60,
		Rounding = 0,
		Compact = true,
		Suffix = " seconds",
		Callback = function(checkInterval)
			autoFindInterval = checkInterval
		end,
	})

	task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(1) do
			if unloaded then
				return
			end

			if not toggles.AutoFindCheaters or not toggles.AutoFindCheaters.Value then
				continue
			end

			if os.clock() < nextAutoFind then
				continue
			end

			nextAutoFind = os.clock() + math.clamp(autoFindInterval, 5, 60)
			local currentCheaters = collectCheaters()

			for player, reason in currentCheaters do
				if autoFindLast[player] ~= reason then
					autoFindLast[player] = reason
					systemNotify(("Cheater detected: %s (%s)"):format(api.FormatPlayer(player), reason))
				end
			end

			for player in autoFindLast do
				if not currentCheaters[player] then
					autoFindLast[player] = nil
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	do
		local Whitelist = tabPages.Lists:AddLeftGroupbox("Whitelist", "shield-check")
		local value20 = api.CreatePlayerDropdown(Whitelist, "Whitelist", sharedState.Whitelisted)

		value20:OnChanged(function()
			if featureState.SilentAim then
				api.RefreshSilentAim()
			end
		end)

		local table16 = {}
		local friendStatusChanged = nil

		pcall(function()
			friendStatusChanged = localPlayer.FriendStatusChanged
		end)

		local function func21(arg, arg2)
			if unloaded or not featureState.AutoWhitelistFriends or arg == localPlayer or arg.Parent ~= Players or api.IsProtectedPlayer(arg) then
				return
			end
			local state12 = value20.Value[arg] == true

			if arg2 then
				if state12 then
					return
				end
				table16[arg.UserId] = true
			else
				if not table16[arg.UserId] then
					return
				end
				table16[arg.UserId] = nil
				if not state12 then
					return
				end
			end

			local table17 = {}

			for k, value21 in value20.Value, nil, nil do
				if k ~= arg then
					table17[k] = value21
				end
			end

			if arg2 then
				table17[arg] = true
			end

			value20:SetValue(table17)
		end

		local function func22(arg)
			if arg == localPlayer or arg.Parent ~= Players or not featureState.AutoWhitelistFriends then
				return
			end
			local ok, result = pcall(localPlayer.IsFriendsWith, localPlayer, arg.UserId)

			if ok then
				func21(arg, result)
			end
		end

		Whitelist:AddDivider("Settings")

		Whitelist:AddToggle("AutoWhitelistFriends", {
			Text = "Auto Whitelist Friends",
			Default = false,
			Tooltip = "Adds your friends to the whitelist",
			Callback = function(autoWhitelistFriends)
				if featureState.AutoWhitelistFriends == autoWhitelistFriends then
					return
				end
				featureState.AutoWhitelistFriends = autoWhitelistFriends
				api.SetRuntimeConnection("AutoWhitelistFriends", Players.PlayerAdded, autoWhitelistFriends, func22)

				if friendStatusChanged then
					pcall(api.SetRuntimeConnection, "AutoWhitelistStatus", friendStatusChanged, autoWhitelistFriends, function(arg, arg2)
						if arg2 == Enum.FriendStatus.Friend or arg2 == Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend then
							func21(arg, arg2 == Enum.FriendStatus.Friend)
						end
					end)
				end

				if not autoWhitelistFriends then
					return
				end

				task.spawn(function()
					for _, value21 in Players:GetPlayers() do
						if unloaded or not featureState.AutoWhitelistFriends then
							return
						end
						func22(value21)
					end
				end)
			end,
		})

		Whitelist:AddToggle("WhitelistToggle", {
			Text = "Toggle Whitelist",
			Default = false,
			Tooltip = "Prevents server-wide features from affecting people in the list above",
			Callback = function(whitelistedBypass)
				featureState.WhitelistedBypass = whitelistedBypass

				if featureState.SilentAim then
					api.RefreshSilentAim()
				end
			end,
		})
	end

	toggles.AutoWhitelistFriends:SetValue(true)

					return

					return

					return

					boundKeys = Instance.new("StringValue")
					boundKeys.Name = "BoundKeys"
					boundKeys.Parent = mouseLockController
				end

				local value

				if arg then
					value = shiftLockKey
				else
					value = ""
				end

				boundKeys.Value = value
			end,
		}):AddKeyPicker("MouseLockKey", {

					local boundKeys = mouseLockController and mouseLockController:FindFirstChild("BoundKeys")

					if boundKeys then
						boundKeys.Value = arg.Name
					end
				end
			end,
		})

	do
		local function func21(arg)
			if typeof(arg) ~= "UDim2" then
				return
			end
			return { arg.X.Scale, arg.X.Offset, arg.Y.Scale, arg.Y.Offset }
		end

		local function func22(arg)
			if typeof(arg) ~= "table" then
				return
			end

			for i = 1, 4 do
				if typeof(arg[i]) ~= "number" then
					return
				end
			end

			return UDim2.new(arg[1], arg[2], arg[3], arg[4])
		end

		local function func23(arg)
			local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
			local num36 = arg.AbsolutePosition + arg.AbsoluteSize / 2
			local table16 = {}
			local num37 = num36.X / math.max(viewportSize.X, 1)
			local num38 = num36.Y / math.max(viewportSize.Y, 1)
			table16[1] = num37
			table16[2] = num38
			return table16
		end

		local function func24(arg, arg2, position)
			if not arg or not arg.Parent then
				return
			end

			if position then
				arg.Position = position
			end

			local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
			local absoluteSize = arg.AbsoluteSize
			local x = arg.AbsolutePosition.X
			local y = arg.AbsolutePosition.Y

			if typeof(arg2) == "table" and typeof(arg2[1]) == "number" and typeof(arg2[2]) == "number" then
				x = arg2[1] * viewportSize.X - absoluteSize.X / 2
				y = arg2[2] * viewportSize.Y - absoluteSize.Y / 2
			end

			local num36 = math.clamp(x, 6, math.max(6, viewportSize.X - absoluteSize.X - 6))
			local num37 = math.clamp(y, 6, math.max(6, viewportSize.Y - absoluteSize.Y - 6))
			local absolutePosition

			if arg.Parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
				absolutePosition = arg.Parent.AbsolutePosition
			else
				absolutePosition = Vector2.zero
			end

			arg.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.round(num36 - absolutePosition.X + arg.AnchorPoint.X * absoluteSize.X), math.round(num37 - absolutePosition.Y + arg.AnchorPoint.Y * absoluteSize.Y))
		end

		local function func25(arg, arg2)
			if not arg or not arg.Parent or not arg2 then
				return
			end
			local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
			local absoluteSize

			if arg.Parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
				absoluteSize = arg.Parent.AbsoluteSize
			else
				absoluteSize = viewportSize
			end

			local uiScale = arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
			uiScale = uiScale and uiScale.Scale or 1
			local num36 = (absoluteSize.Y * arg2.Y.Scale + arg2.Y.Offset) * uiScale
			local num37 = math.min(1, (viewportSize.X - 12) / math.max((absoluteSize.X * arg2.X.Scale + arg2.X.Offset) * uiScale, 1), (viewportSize.Y - 12) / math.max(num36, 1))
			local num38 = arg2.Y.Scale * num37
			local round = math.round
			local num39 = arg2.Y.Offset * num37
			arg.Size = UDim2.new(arg2.X.Scale * num37, math.round(arg2.X.Offset * num37), num38, round(num39))
		end

		local function func26()
			local mainFrame = window.MainFrame
			local sidebarWidth = window:GetSidebarWidth()

			local table16 = {
				Position = mainFrame and func21(mainFrame.Position),
				ScreenPosition = mainFrame and func23(mainFrame),
				Size = mainFrame and func21(mainFrame.Size),
				SidebarWidth = sidebarWidth,
				SidebarScale = sidebarWidth / math.max(mainFrame.Size.X.Offset, 1),
				WatermarkPosition = Watermark and Watermark.Label and func21(Watermark.Label.Position),
				WatermarkScreenPosition = Watermark and Watermark.Label and func23(Watermark.Label),
				MobileButtons = {},
			}

			for k, value20 in sharedState.ActiveDraggableButtons, nil, nil do
				if value20 and value20.Button and value20.Button.Parent then
					table16.MobileButtons[k] = { Position = func21(value20.Button.Position), ScreenPosition = func23(value20.Button) }
				end
			end

			return table16
		end

		local function func27(arg)
			if typeof(arg) ~= "table" then
				return
			end
			local mainFrame = window.MainFrame
			local value20 = func22(arg.Size)
			local value21 = func22(arg.Position)
			local value22 = func22(arg.WatermarkPosition)
			func25(mainFrame, value20)
			func24(mainFrame, arg.ScreenPosition, value21)
			func24(Watermark and Watermark.Label, arg.WatermarkScreenPosition, value22)
			local num36

			if typeof(arg.SidebarScale) == "number" then
				num36 = mainFrame.Size.X.Offset * arg.SidebarScale
			else
				num36 = arg.SidebarWidth
			end

			if typeof(num36) == "number" then
				window:SetSidebarWidth(num36)
			end

			local table16 = {}

			for k in sharedState.ActiveDraggableButtons, nil, nil do
				table.insert(table16, k)
			end

			for _, value23 in table16, nil, nil do
				api.RemoveMobileButton(value23)
			end

			if typeof(arg.MobileButtons) ~= "table" then
				return
			end

			for k, value23 in arg.MobileButtons, nil, nil do
				if typeof(value23) == "table" then
					local value24 = api.AddMobileButton(k, func22(value23.Position or value23))

					if value24 then
						func24(value24.Button, value23.ScreenPosition)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		themeService:SetLibrary(uiLib)
		themeService:SetFolder("Resonance")
		configService:SetLibrary(uiLib)
		local saveJSON = configService.SaveJSON
		local loadJSON = configService.LoadJSON

		configService.SaveJSON = function(arg, arg2)
			local value20, value21, value22 = saveJSON(arg, arg2)
			if not value21 then
				return value20, value21, value22
			end
			local ok, result = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, value20)
			if not ok or typeof(result) ~= "table" then
				return value20, value21, value22
			end
			result.ResonanceUI = func26()
			local ok2, result2 = pcall(HttpService.JSONEncode, HttpService, result)
			if ok2 then
				return result2, true
			end
			return value20, value21, value22
		end

		configService.LoadJSON = function(arg, arg2)
			local ok, result = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, arg2)
			local value20, value21 = loadJSON(arg, arg2)

			if value20 and ok and typeof(result) == "table" and typeof(result.ResonanceUI) == "table" then
				task.defer(func27, result.ResonanceUI)
			end

			return value20, value21
		end
	end

	configService:IgnoreThemeSettings()

		"WhitelistDropdown",
		"ObjectGroup",
		"GroupNoclip",

	})

		FontFace = "Gotham",
	})

	task.spawn(function()
		themeService:ApplyToTab(tabPages.Settings)
	end)

	task.defer(function()
		local function func21(arg)
			if not arg.Value or arg.Value == "None" then
				return nil
			end
			local value20 = table.clone(arg.Modifiers or {})
			table.sort(value20)
			local text5 = ":" .. arg.Value
			return table.concat(value20, "+") .. text5
		end

		for _, value20 in options, nil, nil do
			if value20.Type == "KeyPicker" and value20.OnChanged then
				local changed = value20.Changed
				local value21 = func21(value20)
				local table16 = {}
				local value = value20.Value
				local mode = value20.Mode
				local clone = table.clone
				local modifiers = value20.Modifiers or {}
				local value22 = table.pack(clone(modifiers))
				table16[1] = value
				table16[2] = mode

				do
					local values = table.pack(table.unpack(value22, 1, value22.n))
					table.move(values, 1, values.n, 3, table16)
				end

				local state12 = false

				value20:OnChanged(function(...)
					if changed then
						changed(...)
					end

					api.UpdateKeybindMenu()
					local value23 = func21(value20)
					local value24 = table16
					local table17 = {}
					local value2 = value20.Value
					local mode2 = value20.Mode
					local clone2 = table.clone
					local modifiers2 = value20.Modifiers or {}
					local value25 = table.pack(clone2(modifiers2))
					table17[1] = value2
					table17[2] = mode2

					do
						local values = table.pack(table.unpack(value25, 1, value25.n))
						table.move(values, 1, values.n, 3, table17)
					end

					table16 = table17
					if value23 == value21 then
						return
					end
					value21 = value23
					if state12 or not value23 or unloaded then
						return
					end
					local table18 = {}

					for k, value26 in options, nil, nil do
						if value26 ~= value20 and value26.Type == "KeyPicker" and func21(value26) == value23 then
							table.insert(table18, value26.Text or k)
						end
					end

					if #table18 > 0 then
						table.sort(table18)

						window:AddDialog("KeybindConflict", {
							Title = "Keybind already assigned",
							Description = "This binding is also assigned to: " .. table.concat(table18, ", ") .. ". What would you like to do?",
							OutsideClickDismiss = false,
							FooterButtons = {
								{
									Title = "Cancel",
									Order = 1,
									Variant = "Secondary",
									Callback = function()
										if unloaded or func21(value20) ~= value23 then
											return
										end
										state12 = true
										value20:SetValue(value24)
										state12 = false
									end,
								},
								{
									Title = "Replace",
									Order = 2,
									Callback = function()
										if unloaded or func21(value20) ~= value23 then
											return
										end

										for _, value26 in options, nil, nil do
											if value26 ~= value20 and value26.Type == "KeyPicker" and func21(value26) == value23 then
												value26:SetValue({ "None", value26.Mode, {} })
											end
										end
									end,
								},
								{ Title = "Keep Both", Order = 3, Variant = "Secondary" },
							},
						})
					end
				end)
			end
		end

		for _, value20 in toggles, nil, nil do
			if value20 and value20.OnChanged then
				value20:OnChanged(api.UpdateEnabledToggleCount)
			end
		end

		api.UpdateKeybindMenu()
		api.UpdateEnabledToggleCount()
	end)

	uiLib:OnUnload(function()
		local now2 = os.clock()
		unloaded = true
		pcall(api.SetGucciAntiGrab, false)
		pcall(api.CancelServerHop)
		pcall(api.SetSilentAim, false)
		pcall(api.SetDesync, false)
		pcall(api.SetLookFigures, false)
		pcall(api.SetAntiShurikenLag, false)

			api.DestroyToy(toysFolder:FindFirstChild("RSCMicrowave"))
		end)

		pcall(api.CleanupAutoClicker)
		pcall(api.SetCharacterHighlight, false)
		pcall(api.SetBoostFPS, false)
		pcall(api.SetChinaHat, false)
		pcall(api.SetCustomCrosshair, false)
		pcall(api.SetCameraNoclip, false)
		pcall(api.ClearDestroyObjects)
		pcall(api.ClearObjectGroups)
		pcall(api.ClearAnchors)
		pcall(api.ClearAuraVisuals)
		pcall(api.ClearCoconutForms)
		pcall(api.CleanupESP)
		pcall(api.ClearMutedSounds)
		pcall(api.WakeRuntimeGates)
		pcall(api.DisconnectRuntimeConnections)
		pcall(api.StopControlCharacter)
		pcall(api.StopFly)
		pcall(methodQueue.CancelAll)
		pcall(charRegistry.ClearModels, true)
		pcall(charRegistry.CleanupCandidates)

		if value18 then
			pcall(api.DestroyToy, value18)
			value18 = nil
		end

		api.ClearCustomAssets()

		pcall(function()
			RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RSCZoom")
		end)

		pcall(function()
			RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RSC-AR")
		end)

		for _, toggle in pairs(toggles) do
			if toggle and toggle.SetValue then
				pcall(function()
					toggle:SetValue(toggle.Default or false)
				end)
			end
		end

		table.clear(sharedState.AutoClicker)
		table.clear(sharedState.AutoClickerTargetPoses)

		for k in pairs(featureState) do
			featureState[k] = false
		end

		for _, option in pairs(options) do
			if option.SetValue and option.Default ~= nil then
				pcall(function()
					option:SetValue(option.Default)
				end)
			end
		end

		pcall(api.RestoreWaterSettings)
		pcall(api.RestoreSkySettings)

		if Watermark and typeof(Watermark.Label) == "Instance" then
			Watermark.Label:Destroy()
		end

		if typeof(screenGui) == "Instance" then
			screenGui:Destroy()
		end

		if typeof(part2) == "Instance" then
			part2:Destroy()
		end

		if typeof(part3) == "Instance" then
			part3:Destroy()
		end

		getgenv().ResonanceRunning = false
        pcall(function() workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = getgenv().ResonanceOriginalFallenHeight end)
		local value20 = print
		local text5 = ("Successfully Unloaded in %* seconds"):format(string.format("%.3f", os.clock() - now2))
		value20(text5)
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		for _, value20 in uiLib.Tabs, nil, nil do
			for _, value21 in value20.Groupboxes, nil, nil do
				value21:Resize()
			end
		end
	end)

	do
		local table16 = {}

		Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
			if unloaded then
				return
			end

			if configValues.SelectedACPoseCenter == player then
				configValues.SelectedACPoseCenter = nil
			end

			local table17 = {}
			local state12 = sharedState.SelectedPlayersAC[player] == true

			for _, playerDropdown in ipairs(sharedState.PlayerDropdowns) do
				if playerDropdown.Selected[player] then
					playerDropdown.Selected[player] = nil
					playerDropdown.Values[player] = nil
					table.insert(table17, playerDropdown)
				end
			end

			if state12 and api.SyncACPoseTargets then
				task.defer(api.SyncACPoseTargets)
			end

			if #table17 == 0 then
				return
			end
			api.NotifyEvent("Player Left", { leftPlayer = api.FormatPlayer(player), listCount = #table17 }, "Player Left")
			table16[player.UserId] = table17
		end)

		Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
			local value20 = table16[player.UserId]
			if not value20 or unloaded or not getgenv().ResonanceRunning then
				return
			end
			table16[player.UserId] = nil

			for _, value21 in ipairs(value20) do
				if not table.find(value21.Dropdown.Values, player) then
					value21.Dropdown:AddValues({ player })
				end

				if value21.Multi == false then
					value21.Dropdown:SetValue(player)
					table.clear(value21.Values)
					value21.Values[player] = true
					table.clear(value21.Selected)
					value21.Selected[player] = true
				else
					local table17 = {}

					for k, value22 in pairs(value21.Selected) do
						table17[k] = value22
					end

					table17[player] = true
					value21.Dropdown:SetValue(table17)
					table.clear(value21.Values)

					for k, value22 in pairs(table17) do
						value21.Values[k] = value22
					end

					value21.Selected[player] = true
				end
			end

			api.NotifyEvent("Player Rejoined", { rejoinedPlayer = api.FormatPlayer(player), listCount = #value20 }, "Player Rejoined")
		end)
	end

	notify("Script loaded in " .. math.round((tick() - getgenv().LoadTime) * 100) / 100 .. " seconds", "Resonance", 71450094482101)
	getgenv().LoadTime = nil

	return {

	}

local base = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/deividcomsono/Obsidian/fb0b7b22604664ab94bea7c67e2a14ab1a47ec27/"
local urls = {
    Interface = base .. "Library.lua",
    Themes = base .. "addons/ThemeManager.lua",
    Configs = base .. "addons/SaveManager.lua",
    Icons = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/deividcomsono/lucide-roblox-direct/refs/heads/main/source.lua",
}

local env = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
assert(type(loadstring) == "function", "loadstring is required")
if env.ResonanceRunning or env.ResonanceStarting then
    warn("Resonance is already running or starting")
    return
end
env.ResonanceStarting = true
local cache, loading = {}, {}
local cleanup
local function registerCleanup(fn)
    cleanup = fn
end
local function requireModule(name)
    if cache[name] then return cache[name] end
    local url = assert(urls[name], "Unknown module: " .. tostring(name))
    assert(not loading[name], "Circular module dependency: " .. tostring(name))
    loading[name] = true
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        local source = game:HttpGet(url)
        local chunk, err = loadstring(source, "@Obsidian/" .. name)
        assert(chunk, err)
        return chunk(requireModule)
    end)
    loading[name] = nil
    assert(ok, "Failed to load " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(result))
    assert(type(result) == "table", "Module " .. name .. " did not return a table")
    cache[name] = result
    return result
end

local reloadPath
if type(writefile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" then
    local ok = pcall(function()
        writefile("Resonance210_Obsidian_Runtime.luau", RESONANCE_RUNTIME_SOURCE)
        local source = [[local source = assert(readfile)("Resonance210_Obsidian_Runtime.luau")
local chunk, err = loadstring(source, "@Resonance/Runtime")
assert(chunk, err)
local base = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/deividcomsono/Obsidian/fb0b7b22604664ab94bea7c67e2a14ab1a47ec27/"
local urls = {Interface=base.."Library.lua", Themes=base.."addons/ThemeManager.lua", Configs=base.."addons/SaveManager.lua", Icons="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/deividcomsono/lucide-roblox-direct/refs/heads/main/source.lua"}
local cache = {}
local function requireModule(name)
 if cache[name] then return cache[name] end
 local url = assert(urls[name], "Missing module "..tostring(name))
 local src = game:HttpGet(url)
 local fn, errorMessage = loadstring(src, "@Obsidian/"..name)
 assert(fn,errorMessage)
 local module = fn(requireModule)
 cache[name]=module
 return module
end
local cleanup
local function registerCleanup(fn) cleanup=fn end
local env=getgenv and getgenv() or _G
env.ResonanceRunning=false
env.ResonanceStarting=false
return chunk(requireModule,"Resonance210_Obsidian_Reload.luau",registerCleanup)]]
        writefile("Resonance210_Obsidian_Reload.luau", source)
        assert(type(readfile("Resonance210_Obsidian_Reload.luau")) == "string")
    end)
    if ok then reloadPath = "Resonance210_Obsidian_Reload.luau" end
end

local ok, result = xpcall(function()
    local runtimeSource = RESONANCE_RUNTIME_SOURCE
    local chunk, err = loadstring(runtimeSource, "@Resonance/v2.10")
    assert(chunk, err)
    return chunk(requireModule, reloadPath, registerCleanup)
end, function(err)
    return debug and debug.traceback and debug.traceback(tostring(err), 2) or tostring(err)
end)
env.ResonanceStarting = false
if not ok then
    if cleanup then pcall(cleanup) end
    env.ResonanceRunning = false
    warn("Resonance v2.10 initialization failed: " .. tostring(result))
end
return result
